Explanatory Remarks:

1. Structural elements are enclosed in <>.

Text:

<1>
<1.1>
<1.1.1>
śrīmadbhāvamiśrapraṇītaḥ
bhāva prakāśaḥ pūrva khaṇḍam
gajamukhamamarapravaraṃ siddhikaraṃ vighnahartāram
gurumavagamanayanapradamiṣṭakarīmiṣṭadevatāṃ vande 1
āyurvedāgamanaṃ krameṇa yenābhavadbhūmau
prathamaṃ likhāmi tamahaṃ nānātantrāṇi saṃdṛśya 2
āyurhitāhitaṃ vyādhernidānaṃ śamanaṃ tathā
vidyate yatra vidvadbhiḥ sa āyurveda ucyate 3
anena puruṣo yasmādāyurvindati vetti ca
tasmānmunivaraireṣa āyurveda iti smṛtaḥ 4
vidhātā'tharvasarvasvamāyurvedaṃ prakāśayan
svanāmnā saṃhitāṃ cakre lakṣaślokamayīmṛjum 5
tataḥ prajāpatiṃ dakṣaṃ dakṣaṃ sakalakarmasu
vidhirdhīnīradhiḥ sāṅgamāyurvedamupādiśat 6
atha dakṣaḥ kriyādakṣaḥ svarvaidyau vedamāyuṣaḥ
vedayāmāsa vidvāṃsau sūryāṃśau surasattamau 7
dakṣādadhītya dasrau vitanutaḥ saṃhitāṃ svīyām
sakalacikitsakalokapratipattivivṛddhaye dhanyām 8
svayambhuvaḥ śiraśchinnaṃ bhairaveṇa ruṣā'tha tat
aśvibhyāṃ saṃhitaṃ tasmāttau jātau yajñabhāginau 9
devāsuraraṇe devā daityairye sakṣatāḥ kṛtāḥ
akṣatāste kṛtāḥ sadyo dasrābhyāmadbhutaṃ mahat 10
vajriṇo'bhūd bhujastambhaḥ sa dasrābhyāṃ cikitsitaḥ
somānnipatitaścandra stābhyāmeva sukhī kṛtaḥ 11
viśīrṇā daśanāḥ pūṣṇo netre naṣṭe bhagasya ca
śaśino rājayakṣmā'bhūdaśvibhyāṃ te cikitsitāḥ 12
bhārgavaścyavanaḥ kāmī vṛddhaḥ san vikṛtiṃ gataḥ
vīryavarṇasvaropetaḥ kṛto'śvibhyāṃ punaryuvā 13
etaiścānyaiśca bahubhiḥ karmabhirbhiṣajāṃ varau
babhūvaturbhṛśaṃ pūjyāvindrā dīnāṃ divaukasām 14
saṃdṛśya dasrayorindraḥ karmāṇyetāni yatnavān
āyurvedaṃ nirudvegaṃ tau yayāce śacīpatiḥ 15
nāsatyau satyasandhena śakreṇa kila yācitau
āyurvedaṃ yathā'dhītaṃ dadatuḥ śatamanyave 16
nāsatyābhyāmadhītyaiva āyurvedaṃ śatakratuḥ
adhyāpayāmāsa bahūnātreyapramukhānmunīn 17
ekadā jagadālokya gadākulamitastataḥ
cintayāmāsa bhagavānātreyo munipuṅgavaḥ 18
kiṃ karomi kva gacchāmi kathaṃ lokā nirāmayāḥ
bhavanti sāmayānetānna śaknomi nirīkṣitum 19
dayālurahamatyarthaṃ svabhāvo duratikramaḥ
eteṣāṃ duḥkhato duḥkhaṃ mamāpi hṛdaye'dhikam 20
āyurvedaṃ paṭhiṣyāmi nairujyāya śarīriṇām
iti niścitya gatavānātreyastridaśālayam 21
tatra mandiramindra sya gatvā śakraṃ dadarśa saḥ
siṃhāsanasamāsīnaṃ stūyamānaṃ surarṣibhiḥ 22
bhāsayantaṃ diśo bhāsā bhāskarapratimaṃ tviṣā
āyurvedamahācāryaṃ śirodhāryaṃ divaukasām 23
śakrastu taṃ nirīkṣyaivaṃ tyaktasiṃhāsanaḥ sthitaḥ
tamagre pūjayāmāsa bhṛśaṃ bhūritapaḥkṛśam 24
kuśalaṃ paripapraccha tathāgamanakāraṇam
sa munirvaktumārebhe nijāgamanakāraṇam 25
devarāja na jānāsi diva eva yato bhavān
vidhātrā vihito yatnāttrilokīlokapālakaḥ 26
vyādhibhirvyathitā lokāḥ śokākulitacetasaḥ
bhūtale santi santāpaṃ teṣāṃ hantuṃ kṛpāṃ kuru 27
āyurvedopadeśaṃ me kuru kāruṇyato nṛṇām
tathetyuktvā sahasrākṣo'dhyāpayāmāsa taṃ munim 28
munīndra indra taḥ sāṅgamāyurvedamadhītya saḥ
abhinandya tamāśīrbhirājagāma punarmahīm 29
athātreyo muniśreṣṭho bhagavānkaruṇākaraḥ
svanāmnā saṃhitāṃ cakre naravargānukampayā 30
tato'gniveśaṃ bheḍañca jātūkarṇaṃ parāśaram
kṣīrapāṇiñca hārītamāyurvedamapāṭhayat 31
tantrasya karttā prathamamagniveśo'bhavatpurā
tato bheḍādayaścakruḥ svaṃ svaṃ tantraṃ kṛtāni ca 32
śrāvayāmāsurātreyaṃ munivṛndena vanditam
śrutvā ca tāni tantrāṇi hṛṣṭo'bhūdatrinandanaḥ 33
yathāvatsūtritaṃ dṛṣṭvā prahṛṣṭā munayo'bhavan
divi devarṣayo devāḥ śrutvā sādhviti cābruvan 34
ekadā himavatpārśve daivādāgatya saṅgatāḥ
munayo bahavasteṣāṃ nāmabhiḥ kathayāmyaham 35
bharadvājo munivaraḥ prathamaṃ samupāgataḥ
tato'ṅgirāstato gargo marīcirbhṛgubhārgavau 36
pulastyo'gastirasito vasiṣṭhaḥ saparāśaraḥ
hārīto gautamaḥ sāṃkhyo maitreyaścyavano'pi ca 37
jamadagniśca gārgyaśca kaśyapaḥ kāśyapo'pi ca
nārado vāmadevaśca mārkaṇḍeyaḥ kapiñjalaḥ 38
śāṇḍilyaḥ sahakauṇḍinyaḥ śākuneyaścaśaunakaḥ
āśvalāyanasāṃkṛtyau viśvāmitraḥ parīkṣakaḥ 39
devalo gālavo dhaumyaḥ kāpyakātyāyanāvubhau
kāṅkāyano vaijapeyaḥ kuśiko bādarāyaṇaḥ 40
hiraṇyākṣaśca laugākṣiḥ śaralomā ca gobhilaḥ
vaikhānasā vālakhilyāstathaivānye maharṣayaḥ 41
brahmajñānasya nidhayo yamasya niyamasya ca
tapasastejasā dīptā hūyamānā ivāgnayaḥ 42
sukhopaviṣṭāste tatra sarve cakruḥ kathāmimām
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ mūlamuktaṃ kalevaram
tacca sarvārthasaṃsiddhyai bhavedyadi nirāmayam 43
tapaḥ svādhyāyadharmāṇāṃ brahmacaryavratāyuṣām
harttāraḥ prasṛtā rogā yatra tatra ca sarvaśaḥ 44
rogāḥ kārśyakarā balakṣayakarā dehasya ceṣṭāharā
dṛṣṭā indri yaśaktisaṅkṣayakarāḥ sarvāṅgapīḍākarāḥ
dharmārthākhilakāmamuktiṣu mahāvighnasvarūpā balāt
prāṇānāśu haranti santi yadi te kṣemaṃ kutaḥ prāṇinām 45
tatteṣāṃ praśamāya kaścana vidhiścintyo bhavadbhirbudhai
ryogyairityabhidhāya saṃsadi bharadvājaṃ muniṃ te'bruvan
tvaṃ yogyo bhagavan sahasranayanaṃ yācasva labdhaṃ kramā
dāyurvedamadhītya yaṃ gadabhayānmuktā bhavāmo vayam 46
itthaṃ sa munibhiryogyaiḥ prārthito vinayānvitaiḥ
bharadvājo muniśreṣṭho jagāma tridaśālayam 47
tatrendra bhavanaṃ gatvā surarṣigaṇamadhyagam
dṛṣṭavān vṛtrahantāraṃ dīpyamānamivānalam 48
dṛṣṭvaiva sa muniṃ prāha bhagavān maghavā mudā
dharmajña svāgataṃ te'tha muniṃ taṃ samapūjayat 49
so'bhigamya jayāśīrbhirabhinandya sureśvaram
ṛṣīṇāṃ vacanaṃ samyak śrāvayāmāsa tattvataḥ 50
vyādhayo hi samutpannāḥ sarvaprāṇibhayaṅkarāḥ
teṣāṃ praśamanopāyaṃ yathāvadvaktumarhasi 51
apāṭhayanmuniṃ sāṅgamāyurvedaṃ śatakratuḥ
jīvedvarṣasahasrāṇi dehī nīruṅ niśamya yam 52
so'nantapāraṃ triskandhamāyurvedaṃ mahāmuniḥ
yathāvadacirātsarvaṃ bubudhe tanmanā muniḥ 53
tenāyuḥ suciraṃ lebhe bharadvājo nirāmayam
anyānapi munīṃścakre nīrujaḥ sucirāyuṣaḥ 54
tattantrajanitajñānacakṣuṣā ṛṣayo'khilāḥ
guṇāndra vyāṇi karmāṇi dṛṣṭvā tadvidhimāśritāḥ 55
ārogyaṃ lebhire dīrghamāyuśca sukhasaṃyutam
āyurvedoktavidhinā'nye'pi syurmunayo yathā 56
yadā matsyāvatāreṇa hariṇā veda uddhṛtaḥ
tadā śeṣaśca tatraiva vedaṃ sāṅgamavāptavān 57
atharvāntargataṃ samyagāyurvedaṃ ca labdhavān
ekadā sa mahīvṛttaṃ dra ṣṭuṃ cara ivāgataḥ 58
tatralokān gadairgrastān vyathayā paripīḍitān
sthaleṣu bahuṣu vyagrān mriyamāṇāṃśca dṛṣṭavān 59
tāndṛṣṭvātidayāyuktasteṣāṃ duḥkhena duḥkhitaḥ
anantaścintayāmāsa rogopaśamakāraṇam 60
sañcintya sa svayaṃ tatra muneḥ putro babhūva ha
prasiddhasya viśuddhasya vedavedāṅgavedinaḥ 61
yataścara ivāyāto na jñātaḥ kenacidyataḥ
tasmāccarakanāmnāsau khyātaśca kṣitimaṇḍale 62
sa bhāti carakācāryo devācāryo yathā divi
sahasravadanasyāṃśo yena dhvaṃso rujāṃ kṛtaḥ 63
ātreyasya muneḥ śiṣyā agniveśādayo'bhavan
munayo bahavastaiśca kṛtaṃ tantraṃ svakaṃ svakam 64
teṣāṃ tantrāṇi saṃskṛtya samāhṛtya vipaścitā
carakeṇātmano nāmnā grantho'ya carakaḥ kṛtaḥ 65
ekadā devarājasya dṛṣṭirnipatitā bhuvi
tatra tena narā dṛṣṭā vyādhibhirbhṛśapīḍitāḥ 66
tāndṛṣṭvā hṛdayaṃ tasya dayayā paripīḍitam
dayārdra hṛdayaḥ śakro dhanvantarimuvāca ha 67
dhanvantare suraśreṣṭha bhagavan kiñciducyate
yogyo bhavasi bhūtānāmupakāraparo bhava 68
upakārāya lokānāṃ kena kiṃ na kṛtaṃ purā
trailokyādhipatirviṣṇurabhūnmatsyādirūpavān 69
tasmāttvaṃ pṛthivīṃ yāhi kāśīmadhye nṛpo bhava
pratīkārāya rogāṇāmāyurvedaṃ prakāśaya 70
ityuktvā suraśārdūlaḥ sarvabhūtahitepsayā
samastamāyuṣo vedaṃ dhanvantarimupādiśat 71
adhītya cāyuṣo vedamindrā ddhanvantariḥ purā
āgatya pṛthivīṃ kāśyāṃ jāto bāhujaveśmani 72
nāmnā tu so'bhavatkhyāto divodāsa iti kṣitau
bāla eva virakto'bhūccacāra sumahattapaḥ 73
yatnena mahatā brahmā taṃ kāśyāmakaronnṛpam
tato dhanvantarirlokaiḥ kāśīrājo'bhidhīyate 74
hitāya dehināṃ svīyā saṃhitā vihitā'munā
atha vidyārthino lokānsaṃhitāṃ tāmapāṭhayat 75
atha jñānadṛśā viśvāmitraprabhṛtayo'vidan
ayaṃ dhanvantariḥ kāśyāṃ kāśirājo'yamucyate 76
viśvāmitro munisteṣu putraṃ suśrutamuktavān
vatsa vārāṇasīṃ gaccha tvaṃ viśveśvaravallabhām 77
tatra nāmnā divodāsaḥ kāśirājo'sti bāhujaḥ
sa hi dhanvantariḥ sākṣādāyurvedavidāṃ varaḥ 78
āyurvedaṃ paṭhasva tvaṃ lokopakṛtihetave
sarvaprāṇidayā tīrthamupakāro mahāmakhaḥ 79
piturvacanamākarṇya suśrutaḥ kāśikāṃ gataḥ
tena sārddhaṃ samadhyetuṃ munisūnuśataṃ yayau 80
atha dhanvantariṃ sarve vānaprasthāśrame sthitam
bhagavantaṃ suraśreṣṭhaṃ munibhirbahubhiḥ stutam 81
kāśirājaṃ divodāsaṃ te'paśyanvinayānvitāḥ
svāgataṃ ca tadā cāha divodāso yaśodhanaḥ 82
kuśalaṃ paripapraccha tathāgamanakāraṇam
tataste suśrutadvārā kathayāmāsuruttaram 83
bhagavanmānavāndṛṣṭvā vyādhibhiḥ paripīḍitān
krandato mriyamāṇāṃśca jātāsmākaṃ hṛdi vyathā 84
āmayānāṃ śamopāyaṃ vijñātuṃ vayamāgatāḥ
āyurvedaṃ bhavānasmānadhyāpayatu yatnataḥ 85
aṅgīkṛtya vacasteṣāṃ nṛpatistānupādiśat
vyākhyātaṃ tena te yatnājjagṛhurmunayo mudā 86
kāśirājaṃ jayāśīrbhirabhinandya mudānvitāḥ
suśrutādyāḥ susiddhārthā jagmurgehaṃ svakaṃ svakam 87
prathamaṃ suśrutasteṣu svatantraṃ kṛtavānsphuṭam
suśrutasya sakhāyo'pi pṛthaktantrāṇi tenire 88
suśrutena kṛtaṃ tantraṃ suśrutaṃ bahubhiryataḥ
tasmāttatsuśrutaṃ nāmnā vikhyātaṃ kṣitimaṇḍale 89
iti bhāvaprakāśe pūrvakhaṇḍe āyurvedapravaktṛprādurbhāvaprakaraṇaṃ samāptam 1
<1.1.2>
atha sṛṣṭiprakaraṇaṃ granthārambhaśca 2
āyurvedābdhimadhyādatimatimunayo yogaratnāni yatnāllabdhvā sve sve nibandhe dadhurakhilajanavyādhividhvaṃsanāya ।
tattadgranthād gṛhītaiḥ suvacanamaṇibhirbhāvamiśraiścikitsāśāstre jāḍyāndhakāraṃ praśamayitumimaṃ saṃvidhatte prakāśam 1
śrīpatipadaprasādādāśīrbhirbhūmidevānām
bhāvaprakāśanāmnā grantho'yaṃ paṭhyatāṃ sarvaiḥ 2
ātmā jyotiścidānandarūpo nityaśca niḥspṛhaḥ
nirguṇaḥ prakṛteryogātsaguṇaḥ kurute jagat 3
sattvaṃ rajastamaśceti guṇāste prakṛteḥ samāḥ
sā jaḍāpi jagatkartrī paramātmacidavyayāt 4
pradhānaṃ prakṛtiḥ śaktirnityā cāvikṛtistathā
etāni tasyā nāmāni puruṣaṃ yā samāśritā 5
sattvaṃ rajastamastrīṇi vijñeyāḥ prakṛterguṇāḥ
taiśca yuktasya cittasya kathayāmyakhilān guṇān 6
āstikyaṃ pravibhajya bhojanamanuttāpaśca tathyaṃ vaco
medhābuddhidhṛtikṣamāśca karuṇā jñānaṃ ca nirdambhatā
karmāninditamaspṛhaṃ ca vinayo dharmaḥ sadaivādarā
dete sattvaguṇānvitasya manaso gītā guṇā jñānibhiḥ 7
krodhastāḍanaśīlatā ca bahulaṃ duḥkhaṃ sukhecchā'dhikā
dambhaḥ kāmukatāpyalīkavacanaṃ cādhīratāhaṅkṛtiḥ
aiśvaryādabhimānitātiśayitānando'dhikaścāṭanaṃ
prakhyātā hi rajoguṇena sahitasyaite guṇāścetasaḥ 8
nāstikyaṃ suviṣaṇṇatātiśayitālasyaṃ ca duṣṭā matiḥ
prītirninditakarmaśarmaṇi sadā nidrā lutāharniśam
ajñānaṃ kila sarvato'pi satataṃ krodhāndhatā mūḍhatā
prakhyātā hi tamoguṇena sahitasyaite guṇāścetasaḥ 9
tatra prabhūtasattvastu sāttvikaḥ puruṣaḥ smṛtaḥ
rājasastāmasaścaiva trividhastena mānavaḥ 10
tato'bhavanmahattattvaṃ buddhitattvāparābhidham
triguṇaṃ sattvabahulaṃ nirmalaṃ sphaṭikopamam
cicchāyāprāptacaitanyaṃ tadicchāmayamīritam 11
mahatastriguṇājjāto'haṅkārastriguṇānvitaḥ
sāttviko rājasaścāpi tāmasaśceti sa tridhā 12
jātāni sāttvikāttasmādindri yāṇi sarājasāt
tāni śrotraṃ tvaco netraṃ rasanā nāsikā tathā 13
vāgghastacaraṇopasthagudānyekādaśaṃ manaḥ
pañca buddhīndri yāṇyāhuḥ prāktanānītarāṇi ca 14
karmendri yāṇi pañcaiva kathayanti vipaścitaḥ 15
mano buddhīndri yaṃ vijñaiḥ karmendri yamapi smṛtam
mano'dhiṣṭhitamevedamindri yaṃ yatpravarttate 16
śabdaḥ sparśaśca rūpaśca raso gandho hyanukramāt
buddhīndri yāṇāṃ viṣayāḥ samākhyātā maharṣibhiḥ 17
vācyaṃ grāhyañca gantavyamānandaṃ tyājyameva ca
karmendri yāṇāṃ viṣayā jñātavya viṣayo hṛdaḥ 18
tāmasādapyahaṅkārastanmātrāṇi sarājasāt
pañcālpasattvasambandhāttalliṅgāni bhavanti hi 19
śabdatanmātrakaṃ sparśatanmātraṃ rūpamātrakam
rasatanmātrakaṃ gandhatanmātramiti tāni tu 20
tanmātrebhyo viyadvāyurvahnirvāri vasundharā
etāni pañca jāyante mahābhūtāni tatkramāt 21
śabdaḥ śrotrendri yaṃ vāpi cchidrā ṇi ca viviktatā
viyataḥ kathitā ete guṇāguṇavicāribhiḥ 22
sparśastvagindri yañcāpi laghutā spandanaṃ tanoḥ
ceṣṭā sarvaśarīrasya vāyorete guṇāḥ smṛtāḥ 23
rūpaṃ netrendri yaṃ pākaḥ santāpastīkṣṇatā tathā
varṇo bhrājiṣṇutā'marṣaḥ śauryaṃ vahnerguṇā amī 24
raso rasendri yaṃ śaityaṃ snehaśca gurutā tathā
sarvadra vasamūhaśca śukraṃ vāriguṇāḥ smṛtāḥ 25
gandho ghrāṇendri yaṃ cāpi kāṭhinyaṃ gauravaṃ tathā
vasundharāguṇā ete gaditā guṇavedibhiḥ 26
śabdaḥ sparśaśca rūpañca raso gandhaśca tatkramāt
tanmātrāṇāṃ viśeṣāḥ syuḥ sthūlabhāvamupāgatāḥ 27
prakṛteḥ kāraṇāyogānmatā prakṛtireva sā
mahattattvādayaḥ sapta śaktervikṛtayaḥ smṛtāḥ 28
indri yāṇāṃ ca bhūtānāṃ kāraṇatvānmaharṣibhiḥ
mahattattvādayaḥ sapta proktāḥ prakṛtayo'pi ca 29
daśendri yāṇi cittañca mahābhūtāni pañca ca
etāni sṛṣṭiṃ jānadbhirvikārāḥ ṣoḍaśa smṛtāḥ 30
evaṃ caturviṃśatibhistattvaiḥ siddhe vapurgṛhe
jīvātmaniyaternighno vasati svāntadūtavān 31
sa dehī kathyate pāpapuṇyaduḥkhasukhādibhiḥ
vyāpto baddhaśca manasā kṛtrimaiḥ karmabandhanaiḥ 32
icchādveṣasukhāsukhāni viṣayajñānaṃ prayatno manaḥ
saṅkalpaśca vicāraṇā smṛtiratho buddhiḥ kalāvijñatā
prāṇasyopariyāpanaṃ gudavaśādvāyoradhaḥ preraṇaṃ
netronmeṣanimeṣakṛtyakaraṇotsāhāśca jīve guṇāḥ 33
iti śrī miśralaṭakanatanaya śrīmanmiśrabhāvaviracite bhāvaprakāśe
sṛṣṭiprakaraṇaṃ samāptam 2
<1.1.3>
atha garbhaprakaraṇam 3
dvādaśādvatsarādūrdhvamāpañcāśatsamāḥ striyaḥ
māsi māsi bhagadvārā prakṛtyaivārtavaṃ sravet 1
ārtavasrāvadivasādṛtuḥ ṣoḍaśa rātrayaḥ
garbhagrahaṇayogyastu sa eva samayaḥ smṛtaḥ 2
ārtavasrāvadivasādahiṃsā brahmacāriṇī
śayīta darbhaśayyāyāṃ paśyedapi patiṃ na ca 3
kare śarāve parṇe vā haviṣyaṃ tryahamāharet
aśrupātaṃ nakhacchedamabhyaṅgamanulepanam 4
netrayorañjanaṃ snānaṃ divāsvāpaṃ pradhāvanam
atyuccaśabdaśravaṇaṃ hasanaṃ bahubhāṣaṇam
āyāsaṃ bhūmikhananaṃ pravātañca vivarjayet 5
ajñānādvā pramādādvā lobhādvā daivataśca vā 6
sā cetkuryānniṣiddhāni garbho doṣāṃstadā'pnuyāt
etasyā rodanādgarbho bhavedvikṛtalocanaḥ 7
nakhacchedena kunakhī kuṣṭhī tvabhyaṅgato bhavet
anulepāttathā snānād duḥkhaśīlo'ñjanādadṛk 8
svāpaśīlo divāsvāpāccañcalaḥ syātpradhāvanāt
atyuccaśabdaśravaṇādbadhiraḥ khalu jāyate 9
tāludantauṣṭhajihvāsu śyāvo hasanato bhavet
pralāpī bhūrikathanādunmattastu pariśramāt
skhalate bhūmikhananādunmatto vātasevanāt 10
pūrvaṃ paśyedṛtusnātā yādṛśaṃ naramaṅganā
tādṛśaṃ janayetputraṃ tataḥ paśyetpatiṃ priyam 11
pravahatsalile kṣiptaṃ dra vyaṃ gacchatyadho yathā
tathā vahati rakte tu kṣiptaṃ vīryamadho vrajet 12
āyuḥ kṣayabhayādbharttā prathame divase striyam
dvitīye'pi dine ratyai tyajedṛtumatīṃ tathā 13
tatra yaścāhito garbho jāyamāno na jīvati
āhito yastṛtīye'hni svalpāyurvikalāṅgakaḥ 14
ataścaturthī ṣaṣṭhī syādaṣṭamī daśamī tathā
dvādaśī vāpi yā rātristasyāṃ tāṃ vidhinā bhajet 15
atrottarottaraṃ vidyādāyurārogyameva ca
prajāsaubhāgyamaiśvaryaṃ balañcābhigamāt phalam 16
manobhavāgāramukhe'balānāṃ tisro bhavanti pramadājanānām
samīraṇā candra masī ca gaurī viśeṣamāsāmupavarṇayāmi 17
pradhānabhūtā madanātapatre samīraṇā nāma viśeṣanāḍī
tasyā mukhe yat patitaṃ tu vīryaṃ tanniṣphalaṃ syāditi candra mauliḥ 18
yā cāparā cāndra masī ca nāḍī kandarpagehe bhavati pradhānā
sā sundarī yoṣitameva sūte sādhyā bhavedalparatotsaveṣu 19
gaurīti nāḍī yadupasthagarbhe pradhānabhūtā bhavati svabhāvāt
putraṃ prasūte bahudhāṅganā sā kaṣṭopabhogyā suratopaviṣṭā 20
yugmāsu putrā jāyante striyo'yugmāsu rātriṣu 21
snātaścandanaliptāṅgaḥ sugandhasumanorcitaḥ
bhuktavṛṣyaḥ suvasanaḥ suveśaḥ samalaṅkṛtaḥ 22
tāmbūlavadanastasyāmanurakto'dhikasmaraḥ
putrārthī puruṣo nārīmupeyācchayane śubhe 23
atyāśito'dhṛtiḥ kṣudvān savyathāṅgaḥ pipāsitaḥ
bālo vṛddho'nyavegārttastyajedro gī ca maithunam 24
puruṣasya guṇairyuktā vihitā nyūnabhojanā
nārī ṛtumatī puṃsā saṅgacchettu sutārthinī 25
rajasvalā vyādhimatī viśeṣādyonirogiṇī
vayo'dhikā ca niṣkāmā malinā garbhiṇī tathā
etāsāṃ saṅgamātpuṃsāṃ vaiguṇyāni bhavanti hi 26
kāmānmithunasaṃyoge śuddhaśoṇitaśukrajaḥ
garbhaḥ sañjāyate nāryāḥ sa jāto bāla ucyate 27
dampatyoḥ kuṣṭhabāhulyādduṣṭaśoṇitaśukrayoḥ
yadapatyaṃ tayorjātaṃ jñeyaṃ tadapi kuṣṭhitamiti 28
ṛtau strīpuṃsayoryoge makaradhvajavegataḥ
meḍhrayonyabhisaṅgharṣāccharīroṣmānilāhataḥ 29
puṃsaḥ sarvaśarīrasthaṃ reto drā vayate'tha tat
vāyurmehanamārgeṇa pātayatyaṅganābhage 30
tat saṃsrutya vyāttamukhaṃ yāti garbhāśayaṃ prati
tatra śukravadāyātenārttavena yutaṃ bhavet 31
śaṅkhanābhyākṛtiryonistryāvarttā sā ca kīrtitā
tasyāstṛtīye tvāvartte garbhaśayyā pratiṣṭhitā 32
yathā rohitamatsyasya mukhaṃ bhavati rūpataḥ
tatsaṃsthānāṃ tathā rūpāṃ garbhaśayyāṃ vidurbudhāḥ 33
śukrārttavasamāśleṣo yadaiva khalu jāyate
jīvastadaiva viśati yuktaḥ śukrārttavāntaraḥ 34
sūryāṃśoḥ sūryamaṇita ubhayasmādyutādyathā
vahniḥ sañjāyate jīvastathā śukrārtavādyutāt 35
ātmānādiranantaścāvyakto vaktuṃ na śakyate
cidānandaikarūpo'yaṃ manasāpi na gamyate 36
evambhūto'pi jagato bhāvinī balavattayā
avidyāsvīkṛte karmavaśo garbhe viśatyasau 37
sa eva vettā rasano dra ṣṭā ghrātā spṛśatyasau
śrotā vaktā ca karttā ca gantā rantotsṛjatyapi 38
dine vyatīte niyataṃ saṃkucatyambujaṃ yathā
ṛtau vyatīte nāryāstu yoniḥ saṃvriyate tathā 39
bīje'ntarvāyunā bhinne dvau jīvau kukṣimāgatau
yamāvityabhidhīyete dharmetarapuraḥsarau 40
ādhikye retasaḥ putraḥ kanyā syādārttave'dhike
napuṃsakaṃ tayoḥ sāmye yathecchā pārameśvarī 41
evaṃ tāmabhisaṅgamya punarmāsādbhajedasau 41
śukraśoṇitayoryonerasrāvo'tha śramodbhavaḥ
sakthisādaḥ pipāsā ca glāniḥ sphūrttirbhage bhavet 42
stanayormukhakārṣṇyaṃ syādro marājyudgamastathā
akṣipakṣmāṇi cāpyasyāḥ saṃmīlyante viśeṣataḥ 43
chardayetpathyabhukcāpi gandhādudvijate śubhāt
prasekaḥ sadanaṃ caiva garbhiṇyā liṅgamucyate 44
putragarbhayutāyāstu nāryā māsi dvitīyake
garbho garbhāśaye lakṣyaḥ piṇḍākāro'paraṃ śṛṇu 45
dakṣiṇākṣimahatvaṃ syāt prākkṣīraṃ dakṣiṇe stane
dakṣiṇoruḥ supuṣṭaḥ syātprasannamukhavarṇatā 46
punnāmadheyadra vyeṣu svapneṣvapi manorathaḥ
āmrādiphalamāpnoti svapneṣu kamalādi ca 47
kanyā garbhavatī garbhe peśī māsi dvitīyake
putragarbhasya liṅgāni viparītāni cekṣate 48
napuṃsakaṃ yadā garbhe bhaved garbho'budākṛtiḥ
unnate bhavataḥ pārśve purastādudaraṃ mahat 49
āsekyaśca sugandhī ca kumbhīkaśceṣyaryakastathā
amī saśukrā boddhavyā aśukraḥ ṣaṇḍhasaṃjñakaḥ 50
pitrostu svalpavīryatvādāsekyaḥ puruṣo bhavet
sa śukraṃ prāśya labhate dhvajonnatimasaṃśayam 51
yaḥ pūtiyonau jāyeta sa hi saugandhiko bhavet
sa yoniśephasorgandhamāghrāya labhate balam 52
sve gude'brahmacaryādyaḥ strīṣu puṃvat pravarttate
sa kumbhīka iti jñeyo gudayonistu sa smṛtaḥ 53
dṛṣṭvā vyavāyamanyeṣāṃ vyāvāye yaḥ pravarttate
īrṣyakaḥ sa tu vijñeyo dṛṣṭiyonistu sa smṛtaḥ 54
yo bhāryāyāmṛtau mohādaṅganeva pravarttate
tatra strīceṣṭitākāro jāyate ṣaṇḍhasaṃjñakaḥ 55
ṛtau ṝtau puruṣavat pravarttetāṅganā yadi
tatra kanyā yadi bhavet sā bhavennaraceṣṭitā 56
yadā nāryāvupeyātāṃ vṛṣasyantyau kathañcana
muñcantyau śukramanyonyamanasthistatra jāyate 57
ṛtusnātā tu yā nārī svapne maithunamācaret
ārttavaṃ vāyurādāya kukṣau garbhaṃ karoti hi 58
māsi māsipravarddheta sa garbho garbhalakṣaṇaḥ
kalalaṃ jāyate tasya varjitaṃ paitṛkairguṇaiḥ 59
sarpavṛścikakūṣmāṇḍākṛtayo vikṛtāśca ye
garbhāste yoṣitastāśca jñeyāḥ pāpakṛto bhṛśam 60
garbho vātaprakopeṇa dohade cāpamānite
bhavet kubjaḥ kuṇiḥ paṅgurmūko minmina eva ca 61
āhārācāraceṣṭābhiryādṛśībhiḥ samanvitau
strīpuṃsau samupeyātāṃ tayoḥ putro'pi tādṛśaḥ 62
garbhāśayagataṃ śukramārttavaṃ jīvasaṃjñakaḥ
prakṛtiḥ savikārā ca tatsarvaṃ garbhasaṃjñakam 63
kālena varddhito garbho yadyaṅgopāṅgasaṃyutaḥ
bhavettadā sa munibhiḥ śarīrīti nigadyate 64
tasya tvaṅgānyupāṅgāni jñātvā suśrutaśāstrataḥ
mastakādabhidhīyante śiṣyāḥ śṛṇuta yatnataḥ 65
ādyamaṅgaṃ śiraḥ proktaṃ tadupāṅgāni kuntalāḥ
tasyāntarmastuluṅgaṃ ca lalāṭaṃ bhrūyugantathā 66
netradvayaṃ tayorantarvarttete dve kanīnike
dṛṣṭidvayaṃ kṛṣṇagolau śvetabhāgau ca vartmanī 67
pakṣmāṇyapāṅgau śaṅkhau ca karṇau tacchaṣkulīdvayam
pālidvayaṃ kapolau ca nāsikā ca prakīrttitā 68
oṣṭhādharau ca sṛkkiṇyau mukha tālu hanudvayam
dantāśca dantaveṣṭaśca rasanā cibukaṅgalaḥ 69
dvitīyamaṅgaṃ grīvā tu yayā mūrddhā vidhāryate
tṛtīyaṃ bāhuyugalaṃ tadupāṅgānyatha bruve 70
tatropari matau skandhau pragaṇḍau bhavatastvadhaḥ
kaphoṇiyugmaṃ tadadhaṃ prakoṣṭhayugalantathā 71
maṇibandhau tale hastau tayoścāṅgulayo daśa
nakhāśca daśa te sthāpyā daśacchedyāḥprakīrttitāḥ 72
caturthamaṅgaṃ vakṣastu tadupāṅgānyatha bruve
stanau puṃsastathā nāryā viśeṣa ubhayorayam 73
yauvanāgamane nāryāḥ pīvarau bhavataḥ stanau
garbhavatyā prasūtāyāstāveva kṣīrapūritau 74
hṛdayaṃ puṇḍarīkeṇa sadṛśaṃ syādadhomukham
jāgratastadvikasati svapatastu nimīlati 75
āśayastattu jīvasya cetanāsthānamuttamam
atastasmiṃstamovyāpte prāṇinaḥ prasvapanti hi 76
kakṣayorvakṣasaḥ sandhī jatruṇī samudāhṛte
kakṣe ubhe samākhyāte tayoḥ syātāṃ ca vaṅkṣaṇau 77
udaraṃ pañcamañcāṅgaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ pārśvadvayaṃ matam
sapṛṣṭhavaṃśaṃ pṛṣṭhaṃ tu samastaṃ saptamaṃ smṛtam 78
upāṅgāni ca kathyante tāni jānīhi yatnataḥ
śoṇitājjāyate plīhā vāmato hṛdayādadhaḥ 79
raktavāhiśirāṇāṃ sa mūlaṃ khyāto maharṣibhiḥ
hṛdayādvāmato'dhaśca phupphuso raktaphenajaḥ 80
adho dakṣiṇataścāpi hṛdayādyakṛtaḥ sthitiḥ
tattu rañjakapittasya sthānaṃ śoṇitajaṃ matam 81
adhastu dakṣiṇe bhāge hṛdayāt kloma tiṣṭhati
jalavāhiśirāmūlaṃ tṛṣṇā'cchādanakṛnmatam 82
medaḥ śoṇitayoḥ sārādvṛkkayoryugalaṃ bhavet
tau tu puṣṭikarau proktau jaṭharasthasya medasaḥ 83
arddhavyāmena hīnāni yoṣito'ntrāṇi nirdiśet
uktā sārddhāstrayo vyāmāpuṃsāmantrāṇi sūribhiḥ 84
undukaśca kaṭī cāpi trikaṃ vastiśca vaṅkṣaṇau
kaṇḍarāṇāṃ prarohaḥsyānmeḍhro'dhvā vīryamūtrayoḥ 85
sa eva garbhasyādhānaṃ kuryādgarbhāśaye striyāḥ
śaṅkhanābhyākṛtiryonistryāvarttā sā ca kīrttitā 86
tasyāstṛtīye tvāvartte garbhaśayyā pratiṣṭhitā
vṛṣaṇau bhavataḥ sārātkaphāsṛṅmāṃsamedasām 87
vīryavāhiśirādhārau tau matau pauruṣāvahau
gudasya mānaṃ sarvasya sārddhaṃ syāccaturaṅgulam 88
tatra syurvalayastisraḥ śaṅkhāvartanibhāstu tāḥ
pravāhiṇī bhavetpūrvā sārddhāṅgulamitā matā 89
utsarjanī tu tadadhaḥ sā sārddhāṅgulasammitā
tasyādhaḥ saṃvaraṇī syādekāṅgulasamā matā 90
arddhāṅgulapramāṇaṃ tu budhairgudamukhaṃ matam
malotsargasya mārgo'ya pāyurdehe vinirmitaḥ 91
puṃsaḥ prothau smṛtau yau tu tau nitambau ca yoṣitaḥ
tayoḥ kukundare syātāṃ sakthinotvaṅgamaṣṭamam 92
tadupāṅgāni ca brūmo jānunī piṇḍakādvayam
jaṃghe dve ghuṭike pārṣṇī tale ca prapade tathā 93
pādāvaṅgulayastatra daśa tāsāṃ nakhā daśa
atha doṣāḥ pravakṣyante dhātavastadanantaram 94
āhārādergatistasya pariṇāmaśca vakṣyate
ārttavaṃ cātha dhātūnāṃ malāstadupadhātavaḥ 95
āśayāśca kalāścāpi marmāṇyatha ca sandhayaḥ
śirāśca snāyavaścāpi dhamanyaḥ kaṇḍarāstathā 96
randhrāṇi bhūri srotāṃsi jālaiḥ kūrccāśca rajjavaḥ
sevanyaścātha saṅghātāḥ sīmāntāśca tathā tvacaḥ 97
lomāni lomakūpāśca deha etanmayo mataḥ
vāyuḥ pittaṃ kaphaśceti trayo doṣāḥ samāsataḥ 98
vikṛtāvikṛtā dehaṃ ghnanti te varddhayanti ca
te vyāpino'pi hṛnnābhyoradho madhyordhvasaṃśrayāḥ
vayo'horātribhuktānāmantamadhyādigāḥ kramāt 99
dhātavaśca malāścāpi duṣyantyebhiryatastataḥ
vātapittakaphā ete trayo doṣā iti smṛtāḥ 100
te dhātavo'pi vidvadbhirgaditā dehadhāraṇāt 101
doṣadhātumalādīnāṃ netā śīghraḥ samīraṇaḥ
rajoguṇamayaḥ sūkṣmo rūkṣaḥ śīto laghuścalaḥ 102
utsāhocchvāsaniḥśvāsaceṣṭāvegapravarttanaiḥ 103
samyag gatyā ca dhātūnāmandri yāṇāñca pāṭavaiḥ
anugṛhṇātyavikṛto hṛdayendri yacittadhṛk 104
rajoguṇamayaḥ sūkṣmaḥ śīto rūkṣo laghuścalaḥ
kharo mṛduryogavāhī saṃyogādubhayārthakṛt 105
dāhakṛt tejasā yuktaḥ śītakṛtsomasaṃśrayāt
vibhāgakaraṇādvāyuḥ pradhānaṃ doṣasaṃgrahe 106
pakvāśayakaṭīsakthiśrotrāsthisparśanendri yam
sthānaṃ vātasya tatrāpi pakvādhānaṃ viśeṣataḥ 107
udānastadanuprāṇaḥ samāno'pāna eva ca
vyānaścaitāni nāmāni vāyoḥ sthānaprabhedataḥ 108
kaṇṭhe hṛdi tathā'dhastātkoṣṭhavahnermalāśaye
sakale'pi śarīre'sau krameṇa pavano vaset 109
udāno nāma yastūrdhvamupaiti pavanottamaḥ
tena bhāṣitagītādipravṛttiḥ kupitastu saḥ 110
ūrdhvajatrugatān rogānvidadhāti viśeṣataḥ
yo vāyuḥ prāṇanāmā'sau mukhaṃ gacchati dehadhṛk 111
so'nna praveśayatyantaḥ prāṇāṃścāpyavalambate
prāyaśaḥ kurute duṣṭo hikkāśvāsādikān gadān 112
āmapakvāśayacaraḥ samāno vahnisaṅgataḥ
so'nna pacati tajjāṃśca viśeṣānvivinakti hi 113
sa duṣṭo vahnimāndyātisāragulmān karoti hi
pakvāśayālayo'pānaḥ kāle karṣati cāpyayam 114
samīraṇaḥ śakṛnmūtraśukragarbhārttavānyadhaḥ
kruddhastu kurute rogān ghorānvastigudāśrayān 115
śukradoṣapramehāṃśca vyānāpānaprakopajān
kṛtsnadehacaro vyāno rasasaṃvāhanodyataḥ 116
svedā'sṛksrāvaṇaścāpi pañcadhā ceṣṭayatyapi
praspandanañcodvahanaṃ pūraṇañca virecanam 117
dhāraṇañceti pañcaitāśceṣṭāḥ proktāḥ nabhasvataḥ
gatyapakṣepaṇotkṣepanimeṣonmeṣaṇādikāḥ
prāyaḥ sarvāḥ kriyāstasmin pratibaddhāḥ śarīriṇām 118
kruddhaḥ saḥ kurute rogān prāyaśaḥ sarvadehagān
yugapat kupitā ete dehaṃ bhindyurasaṃśayam 119
pittamuṣṇaṃ dra vaṃ pītaṃ nīlaṃ sattvaguṇottaram
saraṃ kaṭu laghu snigdhaṃ tīkṣṇamamlantu pākataḥ 120
pācakaṃ rañjakañcāpi sādhakālocake tathā
bhrājakañceti pittasya nāmāni sthānabhedataḥ 121
agnyāśaye yakṛtplīhnorhṛdaye locanadvaye
tvaci sarvaśarīreṣu pittaṃ nivasati kramāt 122
pācakaṃ pacate bhuktaṃ śeṣāgnibalavarddhanam
rasamūtrapurīṣāṇi virecayati nityaśaḥ 123
ityalamaprakṛticintanena punaḥ prakṛtamanusarati
rañjakaṃ nāma yatpittaṃ tadra saṃ śoṇitaṃ nayet
yattu sādhakasaṃjñaṃ tatkuryādbuddhiṃ dhṛtiṃ smṛtam 124
yadālocakasaṃjñaṃ tadrū pagrahaṇakāraṇam
bhrājakaṃ kāntikāri syāllepābhyaṅgādipācakam 125
śleṣmāśveto guruḥ snigdhaḥ picchilaḥ śītalastathā
tamoguṇādhikaḥ svādurvidagdho lavaṇo bhavet 126
kaphasyaitāni nāmāni kledanaścāvalambanaḥ
rasanaḥ snehanaścāpi śleṣaṇaḥ sthānabhedataḥ 127
āmāśaye'tha hṛdaye kaṇṭhe śirasi sandhiṣu
sthāneṣveṣu manuṣyāṇāṃ śleṣmā tiṣṭhatyanukramāt 128
kledanaḥ kledayatyannamātmaśaktyā'parāṇyapi
anugṛhṇāti ca śleṣmasthānānyudakakarmaṇā 129
rasayuktātmavīryeṇa hṛdayasyāvalambanam
trikasandhāraṇaṃ cāpi vidadhātyavalambanaḥ 130
ubhāvapi tataḥ saumyau tiṣṭhataścāntike yataḥ
yato rasānvijānīto rasanārasanau samau 131
snehanaḥ snehadānena samastendri yatarpaṇaḥ
śleṣaṇaḥ sarvasandhīnāṃ saṃśleṣaṃ vidadhātyasau 132
ete sapta svayaṃ sthitvā dehaṃ dadhati yannṛṇām
rasāsṛṅmāṃsamedo'sthimajjaśukrāṇi dhātavaḥ 133
prīṇanaṃ jīvanaṃ lepaḥ sneho dhāraṇapūraṇe
garbhotpādaśca karmāṇi dhātūnāṃ kathitāni hi 134
gatyartho rasa dhāturyastato'bhavadayaṃ rasaḥ
sadra vaṃ sakalaṃ dehe rasatīti rasaḥ smṛtaḥ 134
samyakpakvasya bhuktasya sāro nigadito rasaḥ
sa tu dra vaḥ sitaḥ śītaḥ svāduḥ snigdhaścalo bhavet 135
sarvadehacarasyāpi rasasya hṛdayaṃ sthalam
samānamarutā pūrvaṃ yadayaṃ hṛdaye dhṛtaḥ 136
āruhya dhamanīrgatvā dhātūn sarvānayaṃ rasaḥ
puṣṇāti tadanu svīyairvyāpnoti ca tanuṃ guṇaiḥ 137
mandavahnividagdhastu kaṭurvā'mlo bhavedra saḥ
sa kuryādbahulān rogān viṣakṛtyaṃ karotyapi 138
yadā raso yakṛdyāti tatra rañjakapitattaḥ
rāgaṃpākaṃ ca samprāpya sa bhavedra ktasaṃjñakaḥ 139
raktaṃ sarvaśarīrasthaṃ jīvasyādhāramuttamam
snigdhaṃ guru calaṃ svādu vidagdhaṃ pittavadbhavet 140
yakṛt plīhā ca raktasya mukhyasthānantayoḥ sthitam
anyatra saṃsthitavatāṃ raktānāṃ poṣakaṃ bhavet 141
śoṇitaṃ svāgninā pakvaṃ vāyunā ca ghanīkṛtam
tadeva māṃsaṃ jānīyāttasya bhedānapi bruve 142
yathā'thamūṣmaṇā yukto vāyuḥ srotāṃsidārayet
anupraviśya piśitaṃ peśīrvibhajate tathā 143
māṃsapeśyaḥ samākhyātā nṛṇāṃ pañca śatāni hi 144
tāsāṃ śatāni catvāri śākhāsu kathitānyatha
koṣṭhe ṣaḍuttarā ṣaṣṭiḥ kathitā munipuṅgavaiḥ
grīvāyā ūrdhvagāstāstu catustriṃśat prakīrttitāḥ 145
strīṇāmapi bhavantyetāḥ kintu viṃśatiruttarāḥ
garbhāśaye garbhamārge yonau ca stanayorapi 146
puṃsāṃ peśyaḥ purastādyāḥ proktā mehanamuṣkajāḥ
strīṇāmāvṛtya tiṣṭhanti phalamantargataṃ hi tāḥ 147
śirāsnāyvasthiparvāṇi sandhayaśca śarīriṇām
peśībhiḥsaṃvṛtānyeva balavanti bhavanti hi 148
yanmāṃsaṃ svāgninā pakvaṃ tanmeda iti kathyate
tadatīva guru snigdhaṃ balakāryatibṛṃhaṇam 149
medo hi sarvabhūtānāmudareṣvasthi saṃsthitam
ata evodare vṛddhiḥ prāyo medasvino bhavet 150
medo yat svāgninā pakvaṃ vāyunā cātiśoṣitam 151
tadasthisaṃjñāṃ labhate sa sāraḥ sarvavigrahe
abhyantaragataiḥ sārairyathā tiṣṭhanti bhūruhāḥ 152
asthisāraistathā dehā dhriyante dehino dhruvam
tasmācciravinaṣṭeṣu tvaṅmāṃseṣu śarīriṇām
asthīni na vinaśyanti sārā etāni sarvathā 153
śalyatantre'sthikhaṇḍānāṃśatatrayamudāhṛtam 154
tānyevātra nigadyante teṣāṃ sthānāni yāni ca
saviṃśatiśataṃ tvasthnāṃ śākhāsu kathitaṃ budhaiḥ 155
pārśvayoḥ śroṇiphalake vakṣaḥpṛṣṭhodareṣu ca
jānīyādbhiṣageteṣu śataṃ saptadaśottaram
grīvāyāmūrdhvagāṃ vidyādasthnāṃ ṣaṣṭiṃtrisaṃyutām 156
etānyasthīni pañcavidhāni bhavanti tāni yathā
taruṇāni kapālāni rucakāni bhavanti hi
valayānīti tāni syurnalakāni ca kāni cit 157
akṣikośa śrutighrāṇagrīvāsu taruṇāni ca 158
śiraḥ śaṅkhakapoleṣu tālvaṃ saprothajānuṣu
kapālāni bhavantyeṣu danteṣu rucakāni ca 159
pārṣṇyoḥ pārśvayuge pṛṣṭhe vakṣojaṭharapāyuṣu
pādayorvalayāni syurnalakāni bruve'dhunā 160
haste pādāṅgulitale kūrcce ca maṇibandhake
bāhūjaṅghādvaye cāpi jānīyānnalakāni tu 161
māṃsānyantrāṇi baddhāni śirābhiḥ snāyubhistathā
asthīnyālambanaṃ kṛtvā na dīryante patanti ca 162
asthi yat svāgninā pakvaṃ tasya sāro bhavedghanaḥ
yaḥ svedavat pṛthagbhūtaḥ sa majjetyabhidhīyate 163
sthūlāsthiṣu viśeṣeṇa majjā tvabhyantare sthitaḥ 164
rasādra ktaṃ tato māṃsaṃ māṃsānmedaḥ prajāyate
medaso'sthi tato majjā majjñaḥ śukrasya sambhavaḥ 165
yātyāmāśayamāhāraḥ pūrvaṃ prāṇānileritaḥ
mādhuryaṃ phenabhāvaṃ ca ṣaḍraso'pi labheta saḥ 166
ṣaṣṭhī pittadharā nāma yā kalā parikīrttitā
āmapakvāśayāntaḥsthā grahaṇī sā'bhidhīyate 167
tatragrahaṇyāmāmāśayapakvāśayamadhyavarttipācakākhyapittādhiṣṭhānenāgninā'hāraḥ pacyate sa kaṭuśca bhavatītyāhagrahaṇyāṃ pacyate koṣṭhe vahninā jāyate kaṭuḥ iti 168
bhaumāpyāgneyavāyavyāḥ pañcoṣmāṇaḥ sanābhasāḥ
pañcāhāraguṇān svān svānpārthivādīn pacantyanu 169
pañca bhūtātmake dehe āhāraḥ pāñcabhautikaḥ
vipakvaḥ pañcadhā samyagguṇānsvānabhivarddhayet iti 170
miṣṭaḥ paṭuśca madhuramamlo'mla pacyate rasaḥ
kaṭutiktakaṣāyāṇāṃ vipāko jāyate kaṭuḥ iti 171
āhārasya rasaḥ sāraḥ sāra hīno maladra vaḥ
śirābhistajjalaṃ nītaṃ vastiṃ mūtratvamāpnuyāt 172
śeṣaṃ kiṭṭañca yattasya tatpurīṣaṃ nigadyate
samānavāyunā nītantattiṣṭhati malāśaye 173
mūtrañcopasthamārgeṇa purīṣaṃ gudamārgataḥ
apānavāyunā kṣiptaṃ bahiryāti śararītaḥ 174
rasastu hṛdayaṃ yāti samānamaruteritaḥ
sa tu vyānena vikṣiptaḥ sarvān dhātūn vivarddhayet 175
kedāreṣu yathā kulyāḥ puṣṇanti vividhauṣadhīḥ
tathā kalevare dhātūn sarvān varddhayate rasaḥ 176
sthūlaḥ sūkṣmastanmalaśca tatra tatra tridhā rasaḥ
svaṃ sthūlaoṃ'śa paraṃ sūkṣmastanmalo yāti tanmalam 177
dhātau rasādau majjānte pratyekaṃ kramato rasaḥ
ahorātrātsvayaṃ pañca sārddhadaṇḍaṃ ca tiṣṭhati 178
svāgnibhiḥ pacyamāneṣu majjānteṣu rasādiṣu
ṣaṭsu dhātuṣu jāyante malāni munayo jaguḥ 179
yathā sahasradhā dhmāte na malaṃ kila kāñcane
tathā rase muhuḥ pakve na malaṃ śukratāṅgate 180
ojaḥ sarvaśarīrasthaṃ snigdhaṃ śītaṃ sthiraṃ sitam
somātmakaṃ śarīrasya balapuṣṭikaraṃ matam 181
guru śītaṃ mṛdu snigdhaṃ sāndraṃ svādu sthiraṃ tathā
prasannaṃ picchilaṃ sūkṣmamojo daśaguṇaṃ smṛtam 182
aṣṭavindupramāṇaṃ tadīṣadra ktaṃ sapītakam
agnīṣomātmakatvena dvirūpaṃ varṇitantu tat 183
ojaśca tejo dhātūnāṃ śukrāntānāṃ paraṃ smṛtam
hṛdayasthamapi vyāpi dehasthitinibandhanam 184
yasya pravṛddhau dehasya tuṣṭipuṣṭibalodayāḥ
yannāśe niyato nāśo yasmiṃstiṣṭhati jīvanam 185
niṣpadyante yato bhāvā vividhā dehasaṃśrayāḥ
utsāhapratibhādhairyalāvaṇyasukumāratāḥ 186
tataḥ sthūlo bhāgo raso māsena puṃsāṃ śukraṃ strīṇāntvārttavaṃ śukrañca bhavati uktañca suśrute evaṃ māsena rasaḥ śukro bhavati strīṇāmārttavañceti cakārāt strīṇāmapi śukraṃ bhavati ata evoktaṃ suśrute yoṣito'pi sravatyeva śukraṃ puṃsaḥ samāgame tatra garbhasya kiñcittuna karotīti na cintyate 187
strīṇāṃ garbhopayogi syādārttavaṃ sarvasammatam
tāsāmapi balaṃ varṇaṃ śukraṃ puṣṭiṃ karoti hi 188
rasādra ktaṃ tato māṃsaṃ māṃsānmedaḥ prajāyate
medaso'sthi tato majjā majjñaḥ śukrasya sambhavaḥ 189
rasaḥ śarīre śabdārccirjalasantānavat tridhā
sañcaratyanurūpo'ya nityameva hi dehinām 190
vājīkariṇya oṣadhyaḥ svaprabhāvaguṇocchrayāt
virecayanti tāḥ śukraṃ virekidra vyavannṛṇām 191
dugdhaṃ māṣāśca bhallāta phalamajjāmalāni ca
janakāni nigadyante recanāni ca retasaḥ 192
bālānāṃ śukramastyeva kintu saukṣmyānna dṛśyate
puṣpāṇāṃ mukule gandho yathā sannapi nāpyate 193
teṣāṃ tadeva tāruṇye puṣṭatvādvyaktimeti hi
kusumānāṃ praphullānāṃ gandhaḥ prādurbhavedyathā 194
romarājyādayaḥ puṃsāṃ nārīṇāmapi yauvane
jāyate'tra ca yo bhedo jñeyo vyākhyānataḥ sa ca 195
vārddhake varddhamānena vāyunā rasaśoṣaṇāt
na tathā dhātuvṛddhiḥ syāttatastatrānilaṃ jayet 196
śukraṃ saumyaṃ sitaṃ snigdhaṃ balapuṣṭikaraṃ smṛtam
garbhabījaṃ vapuḥsāro jīvasyāśraya uttamaḥ 197
jīvo vasati sarvasmindehe tatra viśeṣataḥ
vīrye rakte male yasmin kṣīṇe yāti kṣayaṃ kṣaṇāt 198
sphaṭikābhaṃ dra vaṃ snigdhaṃ madhuraṃ madhugandhi ca
śukramicchanti kecittu tailakṣaudra nibhañca tat 199
yathā payasi sarpistu gūḍhaścekṣau raso yathā
evaṃ hi sakale kāye śukraṃ tiṣṭhati dehinām 200
dvyaṅgule dakṣiṇe pārśve vastidvārasya cāpyadhaḥ
mūtrasrotaḥpathācchukraṃ puruṣasya pravarttate 201
kṛtsnadehasthitaṃ śukraṃ prasannamanasastathā
strīṣu vyāyacchataścāpi harṣāttat sampravarttate 202
śukraṃ kāmena kāminyā darśanāt sparśanādapi
śabdasaṃśravaṇād dhyānāt saṃyogācca pravarttate 203
rasādeva rajaḥ strīṇāṃ māsi māsi tryahaṃ sravet
tadvarṣād dvādaśādūrdhvaṃ yāti pañcāśataḥ kṣayam 204
māsenopacitaṃ kāle dhamanībhyastadārttavam
īṣadvivarṇaṃ kṛṣṇañca vāyuryonimukhaṃ nayet 205
śaśāsṛkpratimaṃ yacca yadvā lākṣārasopamam
tadārttavaṃ praśaṃsanti yadvāso na virañjayet 206
atiriktā guṇā rakte bahnermāṃse tu pārthivāḥ
medasyapāṃ bhuvaścāsthni pṛthivyanilatejasām 207
majjñi śukre ca somasya mūtre ca śikhinoguṇāḥ
bhuvastathā'ttave tvagne rase kṣīre tathā'mbhasaḥ 208
kaphaḥ pittaṃ malaḥ kheṣu prasvedo nakhaloma ca
netraviṭtvakṣu ca sneho dhātūnāṃ kramaśo malāḥ 209
vanitānāṃ prasūtānāṃ dhamanībhyāṃ stanau gatāt
rasādeva hi jāyeta stanyaṃ stanayugāśayam 210
śuddhamāṃsasya yaḥ snehaḥ sā vasā parikīrttitā
medasastāpyamānasya sneho vā kathitā vasā 211
stanyaṃ rajo vasā svedo dantāḥ keśāstathaiva ca
ojaśca saptadhātūnāṃ kramāt saptopadhātavaḥ 212
uroraktāśayastasmādadhaḥ śleṣmāśayaḥ smṛtaḥ
āmāśayastu tadadhastalliṅgaṃ carako'vadat 213
āmāśayādadhaḥ pakvāśayādūrdhvantu yā kalā
grahaṇī nāmikā saiva kathitaḥ pācakāśayaḥ 214
ūrdhvamagnyāśayo nābhermadhyabhāge vyavasthitaḥ
tasyopari tilaṃ jñeyaṃ tadadhaḥ pavanāśayaḥ 215
pakvāśayastutadadhaḥ sa eva tu malāśayaḥ
tadadhaḥ kathito vastiḥ sa hi mūtrāśayo mataḥ 216
kaphā'mapittavātānāmāśayā malamūtrayoḥ
puruṣebhyo'dhikāścānye nārīṇāmāśayāstrayaḥ 217
dharā garbhāśayaḥ proktaḥ pittapakvāśayāntare
stanau pravṛddhau tāveva budhaiḥ stanyāśayau matau 218
snāyubhiśca praticchannān santatāṃśca jarāyuṇā
śleṣmaṇā veṣṭitāṃścāpi kalābhāgāṃstu tānviduḥ 219
dhātvāśayāntare dhātoryaḥ kledastvadhitiṣṭhati
dehoṣmaṇā'bhipakvaśca sā kaletyabhidhīyate 220
ādyā māṃsadharā proktā dvitīyā raktadhāriṇī
medodharā tṛtīyā tu caturthī śleṣmadhāriṇī 221
pañcamī tu malaṃ dhatte ṣaṣṭhī pittadharā matā
retodharā saptamī syāditi sapta kalāḥ smṛtāḥ 222
sannipātaḥ śirāsnāyusandhimāṃsāsthisambhavaḥ
marmāṇi teṣu tiṣṭhanti prāṇāḥ khalu viśeṣataḥ 223
saptottaraśataṃ santi dehe marmāṇi dehinām
tānyekādaśa māṃse syuraṣṭāvasthiṣu santi hi 224
sandhīnāṃ viṃśatistāni snāyūnāṃ saptaviṃśatiḥ
catvāriṃśatattathaikañca śirāmarmāṇi tatra tu 225
dvāviṃśatiḥ sakthiyuge tāvatyeva bhujadvaye
dvādaśorasi kukṣau ca pṛṣṭhadeśe caturdaśa 226
grīvāyāmūrdhvabhāge tu saptatriṃśanmatāni hi
marmāṇi tāni santīha pañcadhā ca bhavanti hi 227
sadyaḥ prāṇaharāṇi syurmarmāṇyekonaviṃśatiḥ
marmadeśāstrayastriṃśat syuḥ kālāntaramārakāḥ 228
catvāriṃśacca catvāri vaikalyaṃ janayanti hi
marmāṣṭakaṃ rujākāri viśalyaghnaṃ trikaṃ matam 229
śṛṅgāṭakānyadhipatiḥ śaṅkhau kaṇṭhaśirā gudam
hṛdayaṃ vastinābhī ca sadyo ghnanti hatāni cet 230
vakṣomarmāṇi sīmantatalakṣiprendra vastayaḥ
vṛhatyau pārśvayoḥ sandhī kaṭīkataruṇe ca ye
nitambāviti caitāni kālāntaraharāṇi tu 231
lohitākṣāṇijānūrvī kūrcā viṭapakūrparāḥ
kukundare kakṣadhare vidhure sakṛkāṭike 232
aṃsāṃsaphalakāpāṅgā nīle manye phaṇe tathā
vaikalyakaraṇānyāhurāvarttau dvau tathaiva ca 233
gulphau dvau maṇibandhau dvau tathā kūrcaśirāṃsi ca
rujākarāṇi jānīyādaṣṭāvetāni buddhimān 234
utkṣepau sthapanī caiva viśalyaghnaṃ trikammatam 235
saptarātrāntare hanyuḥ sadyaḥ prāṇaharāṇi hi
kālāntaraprāṇaharaṃ pakṣe māse ca mārakam 236
sadyaḥ prāṇaharañcānte viddhaṃ kālena mārayet
kālāntaraprāṇaharamante viddhantu duḥkhadam 237
marmāṇyadhiṣṭhāya hi ye vikārā mūrcchanti kāye vividhā narāṇām
prāyeṇa te kṛcchratamā bhavanti vaidyena yatnairapi sādhyamānāḥ 238
atha sandhayaḥ
te dvividhāśceṣṭāvantaḥ sthirāśca
śākhāsu hanvoḥ kaṭyāñca ceṣṭāvanto bhavanti hi
śeṣāstu sandhayaḥ sarve sthirāstajjñairudāhṛtāḥ 239
kathitā dehināṃ dehe sandhayo dve śate daśa
śākhāsu te'ṣṭaṣaṣṭiśca koṣṭhe tvekonaṣaṣṭikā 240
grīvāyā ūrdhvadeśe tu tryaśītiste prakīrttitāḥ
prathamaṃ parigaṇyante teṣu śākhāgatā iha 241
korodūkhalasāmudgāḥ pratarastūṇasevanī
kākatuṇḍaṃ maṇḍalañca śaṅkhāvartto'ṣṭasandhayaḥ 242
asthnāṃ tu sandhayo hyete kevalāḥ samudāhṛtāḥ
peśīsnāyu śirāṇāntu sandhisaṃkhyā na vidyate 243
sandhibandhanakāriṇyo doṣadhātuvahāḥ śirāḥ
nābhyāṃ sarvā nibaddhāstāḥ pratanvanti samantataḥ 244
śarīraṃ sakalañcaitacchirābhiḥ poṣyate sadā
praṇālībhirivārāmāḥ kulyābhiḥ kṣetradhānyavat 245
prasāraṇākuñcanādikriyābhiḥ satataṃ tanau
śirā evopakurvanti tāḥ syuḥ saptaśatāni tu 246
yathā drumadale sākṣād dṛśyante pratatāḥ śirāḥ
tathaiva daihino dehe varttante sakale śirāḥ 247
nābhisthāḥ prāṇināṃ prāṇāḥ prāṇānnābhirupāśritā
śirābhirāvṛtā nābhiścakranābhirivārakaiḥ 248
kriyāṇāmapratīghātamamohaṃ buddhikarmaṇām
karotyanyān guṇāṃścāpi svāḥ śirāḥ pavanaścaran 249
yadā tu kupito vāyuḥ svāḥ śirāḥ pratipadyate
tadā'sya vividhā rogā jāyante vātasambhavāḥ 250
bhrājiṣṇutāmannarucimagnidīptimarogatām
karotyanyān guṇāṃścāpi pittamātmaśirāścarad 251
yadā tu kupitaṃ pittaṃ sevate svavahāḥ śirāḥ
tadā'syavividhā rogā jāyante pittasambhavāḥ 252
snehamaṅgeṣu sandhīnāṃ sthairyaṃ balamarogātām
karotyanyān guṇāṃścāpi valāsaḥsvāḥśirāścaran 253
yadā tu kupitaḥ śleṣmā svāḥ śirāḥ pratipadyate
tadā'sya vividhā rogā jāyante śleṣmasambhavāḥ 254
dhātūnāṃ pūraṇaṃ varṇasparśajñānamasaṃśayam
svaśirāsu caradra ktaṃ kuryāccānyān guṇānapi 255
yadā tu kupitaṃ raktaṃ sevate svavahāḥ śirāḥ
tadā'sya vividhā rogā jāyante raktasambhavāḥ 256
tatrāruṇā vātavahāḥ pūryante vāyunā śirāḥ
pittāduṣṇāśca nīlāśca śītā gauryaḥ sthirāḥ kaphāt
asṛgdharāstu tā raktāḥ syuśca nātyuṣṇaśītalāḥ 257
medasaḥ snehamādāya śirā snāyutvamāpnuyāt
śirāṇāṃ hi mṛduḥ pākaḥ snāyūnāntu tataḥ kharaḥ 258
snāyavo bandhanānisyurdehamāṃsāsthimedasām
sandhīnāmapi yattāstu śirābhyaḥ sudṛḍhāḥ smṛtāḥ 259
nauryathā phalakāstīrṇā bandhanairbahubhiryutā
niyuktā'gādhasalile bhavedbhārasahā bhṛśam 260
evameva śarīresminyāvantaḥ sandhayaḥ smṛtāḥ
snāyubhirbahubhirbaddhāstena bhārasahā narāḥ 261
śatāni nava jāyante śarīre snāyavo nṛṇām
tāsāṃ vivaraṇaṃ brūmaḥ śiṣyāḥ śṛṇuta yatnataḥ 262
śākhāsu ṣaṭśatāni syuḥ koṣṭhe triṃśacchatadvayam
grīvāyā ūrdhvadeśe tu snāyūnāṃ saptatiḥ samṛtā 263
dhamanyo nābhito jātāścaturviṃśatisaṃkhyayā
daśordhvagā daśādhogāḥ śeṣāstiryaggatāḥ smṛtā 264
yathā svabhāvataḥ khāni mṛṇāleṣu viseṣu ca
dhamanīnāṃ tathā khāni raso yairabhitaścaret 265
pañcābhibhūtāstvatha pañcakṛtvaḥ pañcendri yaṃ pañcasu bhābayanti
pañcendri yaṃ pañcasu bhāvayitvā pañcatvamāyānti vināśakāle 266
mahatyaḥ snāyavaḥ proktāḥ kaṇḍarāstāstu ṣoḍaśa
prasāraṇākuñcanayordṛṣṭaṃ tāsāṃ prayojanam 267
catasro hastayostāsāṃ tāvatyaḥ pādayoḥ smṛtāḥ
grīvāyāmapi tāvatyastāvatyaḥ pṛṣṭhasaṅgatāḥ 268
netraśravaṇanāsānāṃ dve dve randhre prakīrttite
mukhamehanapāyūnāmekaikaṃ randhramucyate 269
daśamaṃ mastake proktaṃ randhrāṇīti nṛṇāṃ viduḥ
strīṇāmanyāni ca trīṇi stanayorgarbhavartmani 270
manaḥ prāṇānnapānīyadoṣadhātūpadhātavaḥ
dhātūnāṃ ca malā mūtraṃ malamityādayastanau 271
sañcaranti hi yairmāgaistāni srotāṃsi sañjaguḥ
bahūni tāni saṃkhyāya śakyante naiva bhāṣitum 272
jālāni tu śirāsnāyumāṃsāsthnāmudbhavanti hi
tāni catvāri catvāri sarvāṇyeva ca ṣoḍaśa 273
kūrcā syurhastayordvau tu tāvantau pādayorapi
grīvāyāmeka ekastu meḍhre sarve'pi ṣaṭ smṛtāḥ 274
pṛṣṭhavaṃśasyobhayatra mahatyo māṃsarajjavaḥ
catasro māṃsapeśīnāṃ bandhanaṃ tatprayojanam 275
sevanyaḥ sapta tāsāṃ tu bhaveyuḥ pañca mastake
ekā śephasi jihvāyāmekā vidhyenna tāḥ kvacit 276
caturdaśāsthnāṃ saṅghātāsteṣāṃ trayo gulphajānuvaṃkṣaṇeṣu
etenetarasakthibāhū ca vyākhyātau trikaśirasorekaikam 277
caturdaśaiva sīmantāḥ kathitā munipuṅgavaiḥ
saṅghātāḥ sīvitā yaistu sīmantāste prakīrttitāḥ 278
kṣīrasya pacyamānasya yathā santānikā bhavet
pacyamānasya śukrasya rajasaśca tathā tvacaḥ
pūrvāvabhāsinī tāsāṃ sidhmasthānaṃ ca sā smṛtā 279
dvitīyā lohitā jñeyā tilakālakajanmabhūḥ 280
tṛtīyā tu bhavecchvetā sthānaṃ carmadalasya sā 281
tāmrā caturthī vijñeyā kilāsaśvitrabhūmikā 282
piñcamī vedinī nāmnā pañcabhāgapramāṇikā
visarpakuṣṭhādhiṣṭhānā jñeyā ṣaṣṭhī tu rohiṇī
vikhyātā rohiṇī ṣaṣṭhī granthigaṇḍāpacīsthitiḥ 283
sthūlā tvaksaptamī khyātā vidra dhyādeḥ sthitiśca sā 284
asthno malāni lomāni cāsaṃkhyāni bhavanti hi
santi yāvanti lomāni tāvanto lomakūpakāḥ 285
aṅgapratyaṅganirvṛttiḥ svabhāvādeva jāyate
sanniveśaśca gātrāṇāṃ nātrāste kāraṇāntaram 286
aṅgapratyaṅganirvṛttau ye bhavanyaguṇā guṇāḥ
te te garbhasya vijñeyā dharmādharmanimittajāḥ 287
dantānāṃ patanaṃ janma punaḥ pāte tvasambhavaḥ
taleṣvanudbhavo lomnāmetatsarvaṃ svabhāvataḥ 288
garbhāśaye nipatitaṃ yādṛkśukraṃ tathā'tavam
tādṛgeva dra vībhūtaṃ prathame māsi tiṣṭhati 289
marutpittakaphaistatsthaiḥ pacyamāno dvitīyake
kalalasthamahābhūtasamudāyo ghanībhavet 290
tṛtīye māsi śiraso hastayoḥ pādayostathā
piṇḍakāḥ pañca sidhyanti sūkṣmāṅgāvayavāstanoḥ 291
sarvāṇyaṅgānyupāṅgāni caturthe syuḥ sphuṭāni hi
hṛdayavyaktabhāvena vyajyate cetanā'pi ca 292
tasmāccaturthe garbhastu nānā vastūni vāñchati
tato dvihṛdayā yatsyānnārī dauhṛdinī matā 293
dauhṛdāvajñayā kubjaṃ kuṇiṃ ṣaṇḍhaṃ ca vāmanam
vikṛtākṣamanakṣaṃ vā putraṃ nārī prasūyate 294
yataḥ strī dauhṛdaṃ prāpya vīryavantaṃ cirāyuṣam
putraṃ prasūyate tasmāttasyai vācchitamarpayet 295
indri yārthāṃstu yānyānsā bhoktumicchati garbhiṇī
garbhabādhābhayāttāṃstānbhiṣagāhṛtya dāpayet 296
sā prāptadauhṛdā putraṃ janayettu guṇānvitam
alabdhadauhṛdā garbhe labhetātmani vā bhayam 297
yeṣu yeṣvindri yārtheṣu dauhṛde sā'vamānitā
prasūyate sutaṃ sārtiṃ tasmiṃstasmiṃstadindri ye 298
rājasandarśane yasyā dauhṛdaṃjāyate striyāḥ
arthavantaṃ mahābhāgaṃ kumāraṃ sā prasūyate 299
dukūlapaṭṭakauśeyabhūṣaṇādiṣu dauhṛdāt
alaṅkāraiṣiṇaṃ putraṃ lalitaṃ sā prasūyate 300
āśrame saṃyatātmānaṃ dharmaśīlaṃ prasūyate
devatāpratimāyāṃ tu prasūte pārṣadopamam 301
darśane vyālajātīnāṃ hiṃsāśīlaṃ prasūyate
raktākṣaṃ lomaśaṃ śūraṃ mahiṣāmiṣadauhṛdāt 302
vārāhamāṃse svapnāluṃ śūraṃ sañjanayetsutam
mṛgamāṃse tu jaṅghālaṃ vikrāntaṃ vanacāriṇam 303
ato'nukteṣu yā nārī dauhṛdaṃ vidadhāti hi
śarīrācāraśīlaiḥ sā samānaṃ janayiṣyati 304
pañcame mānasaṃ ṣaṣṭhe buddhiścātiprabuddhyte
sarvāṇyaṅgānyupāṅgāni bhṛśaṃ vyaktāni saptame 305
ojo'ṣṭame sañcarati mātā putrau muhuḥ kramāt
tena taumlānamuditau syātāṃ jāto na jīvati 306
na jīvatyaṣṭame jātastatraujo na sthiraṃ yataḥ
tathā nerṛtyabhāgatvāddāpayettadvali tataḥ 307
navame daśame māsi nārī bālaṃ prasūyate
ekādaśe dvādaśe vā tato'nyatra vikārataḥ 308
śiro bhavati cāṅgasya pūrvamityāha śaunakaḥ
śirasyevopajāyante pradhānānīndri yāṇi yat 309
hṛdayaṃ jāyate pūrvaṃ kṛtavīryo'vadanmuniḥ
buddheśca manasaścāpi yatastatsthānamīritam 310
pārāśarya iti prāha pūrvaṃ nābhisamudbhavaḥ
prāṇo yatra sthito dehaṃ varddhayatyūṣmasaṃyutaḥ 311
pāṇipādaṃ bhavetpūrvaṃ mārkaṇḍeyamunermatam
dehinaḥ sakalāśceṣṭāḥ pāṇipādāśrayā yataḥ 312
prathamaṃ jāyate koṣṭhaṃ tataḥ sarvāṃgasambhavaḥ
etattuṃ kathayāmāsa gautamo munipuṃgavaḥ 313
sarvāṇyaṅgānyupāṅgāni yugapatsambhavanti hi
sūkṣmatvānnopalabhyante mataṃ dhanvantareridam 314
āmrasyāṇuphale bhavanti yugapanmāṃsāsthimajjādayo
lakṣyante na pṛthakpṛthaktanutayā puṣṭāsta eva sphuṭāḥ
evaṃ garbhasamudbhave tvavayavāḥ sarve bhavantyekadā
lakṣyāḥ sūkṣmatayā na te prakaṭatāmāyānti vṛddhiṃ gatāḥ 315
atha śarīre pitṛjamātṛjarasajātmajā bhāgā ucyante tatra
keśāḥśmaśru ca lomāni nakhā dantāḥ śirāstathā
dhamanyaḥ snāyavaḥ śukrametāni pitṛjāni hi 316
māṃsāsṛṅmajjamedāṃsi yakṛtplīhāntranābhayaḥ
hṛdayaṃ ca gudaṃ cāpi bhavantyetāni mātṛtaḥ 317
śarīropacayo varṇo balaṃ dehasthitistathā
rasādetāni jāyante bhiṣajo munayo jaguḥ 318
jñānaṃ vijñānamāyuśca sukhaduḥkhādikaṃ tathā
indri yāṇi ca sarvāṇi bhavantyetāni cātmanaḥ 319
agnīṣomau mahī vāyurnabhaḥ sattvaṃ rajastamaḥ
pañcendri yāṇi bhūtātmā garbhaṃ sañjīvayanti hi 320
garbhasya nābhināḍyā tu nāḍī rasavahā striyāḥ
saṃlagnā tena garbhasya vṛddhirbhavati nityaśaḥ 321
niḥśvāsocchvāsasaṃkṣobhasvapnāṃśānso'dhigacchati
māturniḥśvasitocchvāsasaṃkṣobhasvapnasaṃbhavān 322
garbhasya nābhimadhye tu jyotiḥsthānaṃ dhruvaṃ smṛtam
tadādhamati vātaśca dehastenāsya vardhate 323
ūṣmaṇā sahitāścāpi dārayatyasya mārutaḥ
ūrdhvaṃ tiryagadhastācca srotāṃsi tu yathā tathā 324
dṛṣṭiśca rāmakūpāśca na vardhante kadā ca na
dhruvāṇyetāni martyānāmiti dhanvantarermatam 325
śarīre kṣīyamāṇe'pi vardhete dvāvimau sadā
svabhāvaṃ prakṛtiṃ kṛtvā nakhakeśāviti sthitiḥ 326
cetanānāmadhiṣṭhānaṃ mano dehaśca sendri yaḥ
keśalomanakhāgrānnamaladra vaguṇairvinā 327
vātālpatvādayogācca vāyoḥ pakvāśayasya ca
vātamūtrapurīṣāṇi garbhastho na vimuñcati 328
jarāyuṇā mukhe channe kaṇṭhe ca kaphaveṣṭite
vāyormārganirodhācca na garbhasthaḥ praroditi 329
garbhiṇī prathamādahnaḥ prahṛṣṭā bhūṣitā śuciḥ
bhavecchuklāmbaradharā guruviprārcane ratā 330
bhojyaṃ tu madhuraprāyaṃ snigdhaṃ hṛdyaṃ dra vaṃ laghu
saskṛtaṃ dīpanīyaṃ tu nityamevopayojayet 331
garbhiṇī na tu kurvīta vyāyāmamapatarpaṇam
vyavāyaṃ ca na seveta na kuryādatitarpaṇam 332
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ śokaṃ yānasyārohaṇaṃ tathā
raktamokṣaṃ vegarodhaṃ na kuryādutkaṭāsanam 333
doṣābhighātairgarbhiṇyā yo yo bhāgaḥ prapīḍyate
sa sa bhāgaḥ śiśostasya garbhasthasya prapīḍyate 334
malināṃ vikṛtākārāṃ hīnāṅgīṃ na spṛśetstriyam
na jighredapi durgandhaṃ na paśyennayanāpriyam 335
vacāṃsi nāpi śṛṇuyātkarṇayorapriyāṇi ca
nānnaṃ paryuṣitaṃ śuṣkaṃ bhuñjīta kvathitaṃ na ca 336
caityaśmaśānavṛkṣāṃśca bhāvāṃścāpyayaśaskarān
bahirniṣkramaṇaṃ krodhaṃ śūnyāgāraṃ ca varjayet 337
noccairbrūyānna tatkuryādyena garbho vinaśyati
tailābhyaṅgodvartanaṃ ca nātyarthaṃ kārayedapi 338
nāmṛdvāstaraṇaṃ kuryānnātyuccaṃ śayanāsanam
etāṃstu niyamānsarvānyatnātkurvīta garbhiṇī 339
navame daśame māsi nārī garbhaṃ prasūyate
ekādaśe dvādaśe vā tato'nyatra vikārataḥ 340
aṣṭahastāyataṃ cāru caturhastaviśālakam
prācīdvāramudagdvāraṃ vidadhyātsūtikāgṛham 341
jāte hi śithile kukṣau mukte hṛdayabandhane
saśūle jaghane nārī vijñeyā prasavotsukā 342
āsannaprasavāyāstu kaṭīpṛṣṭhaṃ tu savyatham
bhavenmuhuḥ pravṛttiśca mūtrasya ca malasya ca 343
tailenābhyaktagātrāntāṃ saṃsnātāmuṣṇavāriṇā
yavāgūṃ pāyayetkoṣṇāṃ mātrayā ghṛtasaṃyutām 344
kṛtopadhāne mṛduni vistīrṇe śayane śanaiḥ
abhugnasakthī cottānā nārī tiṣṭhedvyathā'nvitā 345
catasro'śakanīyāśca srāvaṇe kuśalā hitāḥ
vṛddhāḥ paricareyustāḥ samyakchinnanakhāḥ striyaḥ 346
apatyamārgaṃ tailena samabhyajya samantataḥ
ekā tu tāsu subhage pravāhasveti tāṃ vadet 347
avyathā mā pravāhiṣṭhāḥ prāvahethā vyathā yadi
pravāhethāḥ śanaiḥ pūrvaṃpragāḍhaṃ ca tataḥ param 348
tato gāḍhatamaṃ garbhe yonidvāramupāgate
aparāsahito garbho yāvatpatati bhūtale 349
mūkaṃ vā badhiraṃ kubjaṃ śvāsakāsakṣayānvitam
sūte srastatanuṃ bālamakāle tu pravāhaṇāt 350
iti śrīmiśralaṭakana tanaya śrīmanmiśrabhāva viracite
bhāvaprakāśe garbhaprakaraṇaṃ tṛtīyam 3
<1.1.4>
atha caturthaṃ bālaprakaraṇam 4
atha bāle samutpanne vidadhīta vidhiṃ tathā
yathaiva kulavṛddhastrīvyavahāraparamparā 1
prasūtā hitamāhāraṃ vihāraṃ ca samācaret
vyāyāmaṃ maithunaṃ krodhaṃ śītasevāṃ vivarjayet 2
mithyācārātsūtikāyā yo vyādhirupajāyate
sa kṛcchrasādhyo'sādhyo vā bhavettatpathyamācaret 3
sarvataḥ pariśuddhā syātsnigdhapathyā'lpabhojanā
svedābhyaṅgaparā nityaṃ bhavenmāsamatandri tā 4
prasūtā sārdhamāsānte dṛṣṭe vā punarārttave
sūtikānāmahīnā syāditi dhanvantarermatam 5
vyupadra vāṃ viśuddhāṃ ca vijñāya varavarṇinīm
ūrdhvaṃ caturbhyo māsebhyo niyamaṃ parihārayet 6
rasaprasādo madhuraḥ pakvāhāranimittajaḥ
kṛtsnāddehātstanau prāptaḥ stanyamityabhidhīyate 7
stanyaṃ trirātrātstrīṇāṃ vā catūrātrādanantaram
pravarttayanti vivṛtā dhamanyo hṛdaye sthitāḥ 8
payaḥ putrasya saṃsparśāddarśanātsmaraṇādapi
grahaṇādapyurojasya śukravatsampravarttate
sneho nirantarastasya pravāhe heturucyate 9
avātsalyādbhayācchokātkrodhādatyapatarpaṇāt
strīṇāṃ stanyaṃ bhavetsvalpaṃ garbhāntaravidhāraṇāt 10
śāliṣaṣṭikagodhūmānmāṃsakṣudra jhaṣānapi
kālaśākamalābūṃ ca nārikelaṃ kaserukam 11
śṛṅgāṭakaṃ varīṃ cāpi vidārīkandameva ca
laśunaṃ dugdhavṛddhayai strī seveta sumanā bhavet 12
kalamasya taṇḍulānāṃ kalkaṃ yā kṣīrapeṣitaṃ pibati
sā bhavati bhṛśaṃ taruṇī kṣīrabhareṇaiva tuṅgakucayugalā 13
kalamaḥ kalivikhyāto jāyate sa bṛhaddhrade
kāśmīradeśa evokto mahātaṇḍulasaṃjñakaḥ 14
vidārikandasya rasaṃ pibetstanyasya vṛddhaye
taccūrṇaṃ tasya vṛrddhythaṃ pibedvā kṣīrasaṃyutam 15
dhātryā gurubhirāhārairvihārairdoṣalaistathā
dehe doṣāḥ prakupyanti tataḥ stanyaṃ praduṣyati 16
mithyā'hāravihāriṇyā duṣṭā vātādayaḥ striyāḥ
dūṣayanti payastena śarīraṃ vyādhayaḥ śiśoḥ 17
kaṣāyaṃ salilaplāvi stanyaṃ mārutadūṣitam
pittādamlaṃ ca kaṭukaṃ rājyo'mbhasi tu pītikāḥ 18
kaphaduṣṭaṃ tu yattoye nimajjati ca picchilam
dvandvajaṃ tu dviliṅgaṃ syāttriliṅgaṃ sānnipātikam 19
dhātrī kṣīraviśurddhythaṃ mudgayūṣarasāśinī
bhārṅgīdāruvacāḥ piṣṭvā pibetsā'tiviṣāstathā 20
pāṭhāmūrvā'bdabhūnimbadāruśuṇṭhīkaliṅgakaiḥ
sārivāmatsyapittā'khyai kvāthaḥ stanyaviśodhanaḥ 21
paṭolanimbāsanadārupāṭhā mūrvāṃ guḍūcīṃ kaṭurohiṇīṃ ca
sanāgarāṃ ca kvathitāṃ ca toye dhātrī pibetstanyaviśuddhihetoḥ 22
nīre syanyaṃ yadeki svādavivarṇamatantumat
pāṇḍuraṃ tanu śītaṃ ca taddugdhaṃ śuṃddhamādiśet 23
pītāya yadi bālasya vidadhyādupamātaram
suvicārya guṇāndoṣānkuryāddhātrīṃ tadedṛśīm 24
savarṇāṃ madhyavayasāṃ sacchīlāṃ muditāṃ sadā
śuddhadugdhāṃ bahukṣīrāṃ savatsāmativatsalām 25
svādhīnāmalpasantuṣṭāṃ kulīnāṃ sajjanātmajām
kaitavena parityaktāṃ nijaputraśadṛśaṃ śiśau 26
śokākulākṣu dhārtā ca śrāntā vyādhimatī sadā
atyuccā nitarāṃ nīcā sthūlātīva bhṛśaṃ kṛśā 27
garbhiṇī jvariṇī cāpi lambonnatapayodharā
ajīrṇabhojinī cāpi tathā pathyavivarjitā 28
āsaktā kṣudra kāryeṣu duḥkhārtā cañcalā'pi ca
etāsāṃ stanyapānena śiśurbhavati sāmayaḥ 29
tatra mātā praśastāṅgī cāruvastrā puromukhī
upaviśyāsane samyagdakṣiṇaṃ stanamambunā 30
prakṣālyeṣatparisrāvya mantrābhyāmabhimantritam
udaṅmukhaṃ śiśuṃ kroḍe śanaiḥ sandhāya pāyayet 31
asrāvitaṃ stanaṃ bālaḥ pibanstanyena bhūyasā
pūrṇasrotā vamīkāsaśvāsairbhavati pīḍitaḥ 32
kṣīranīranidhiste'stu stanayoḥ kṣīrapūrakaḥ
sadaiva subhago bālo bhavatveṣa mahābalaḥ 33
payo'mṛtasamaṃ pītvā kumāraste śubhānane
dīrghamāyuravāpnotu devāḥ prāpyāmṛtaṃ yathā 34
kṣīrasātmyatayā kṣīramājaṃ gavyamathāpi vā
dadyādāstanyaparyāpterbālebhyo vīkṣya mātrayā 35
yathoktavidhinā bālaṃ māsi ṣaṣṭhe'ṣṭame'pi ca
annaṃ samprāśayetkiñcittatastadvardhayetkramāt 36
bālamaṅke sukhaṃ dadhyānna cainaṃ tarjayetkvacit
sahasā bodhaye nnaiva nāyogyamupaveśayet 37
nākṛṣya sthāpayetkroḍe na kṣipraṃ śayane kṣipet
rodayenna kvacitkārye vidhimāvaśyakaṃ vinā 38
taccittamanuvartteta taṃ sadaivānumodayet
vāratapataḍidvṛṣṭidhūmānalajalāditaḥ
nimnoccasthānataścāpi rakṣedbālaṃ prayatnataḥ 39
abhyaṅgodvarttana snānaṃ netrayorañjanaṃ tathā
vasanaṃ mṛdu yattacca tathā mṛdvanulepanam
janmaprabhṛti pathyāni bālasyaitāni sarvathā 40
kavalaḥ pañcamādvarṣādaṣṭamānnasyakarma ca
virekaḥ ṣoḍaśādvarṣādviṃśateścaiva maithunam 41
vayastu trividhaṃ bālyaṃ madhyamaṃ vārdhakaṃ tathā
ūnaṣoḍaśavarṣastu naro bālo nigadyate 42
trividhaḥ so'pi dugdhāśī dugdhānnāśī tathā'nnabhuk
dugdhāśī varṣaparyantaṃ dugdhānnāśī śaraddvayam 43
taduttaraṃ syādannāśī evaṃ bālastridhā mataḥ
madhye ṣoḍaśasaptatyormadhyamaḥ kathito budhaiḥ 44
caturdhā madhyamaṃ vṛddhiyuvapūrṇakṣayānvitam
bhavedāviṃśatervṛddhiryuvā tvāntriṃśato mataḥ 45
catvāriṃśatsamā yāvattiṣṭhedvīryādipūritaḥ
tataḥ krameṇa kṣīṇaḥ syādyāvadbhavati saptatiḥ 46
tatastu saptaterūrdhvaṃ kṣīṇadhāturasādikaḥ
kṣīyamāṇendri yabalaḥ kṣīṇaretā dine dine 47
balīpalitakhālityayuktaḥ karmasu cākṣamaḥ
kāsaśvāsādibhiḥ kliṣṭo vṛddho bhavati mānavaḥ 48
bālye vivardhate śleṣmā pittaṃ syānmadhyame'dhikam
vārdhake varddhate vāyurvicāryaitadupakramet 49
bālyaṃ vṛddhiśchavirmedhā tvagdṛṣṭiḥ śukravikramau
buddhiḥ karmendri yaṃ ceto jīvitaṃ daśato hraset 50
sapta prakṛtayo nṝṇāṃ vātātpittātkaphāttathā
saṃsargātsannipātācca bhavanti bhiṣajāṃ mate 51
śukraśoṇitasaṃyoge yo doṣastūtkaṭo bhavet
prakṛtirjāyate tena tasyā lakṣaṇamucyate 52
śukrāsṛggarbhiṇībhojyaceṣṭāgarbhāśayārttiṣu
yaḥ syāddoṣo'dhikastena prakṛtiḥ saptadhoditā 53
jāgarūko'lpakeśaśca sphuṭitāṃghrikaraḥ kṛśaḥ
śīghrago bahuvāgrūkṣaḥ svapne viyati gacchati
evaṃvidhaḥ sa vijñeyo vātaprakṛtiko naraḥ 54
pittaprakṛtiko loko yādṛśo'tha nigadyate
akālapalito gauraḥ krodhī svedī ca buddhimān 55
bahubhuktāmranetraśca svapne jyotīṃṣi paśyati
evaṃvidho bhavedyastu pittaprakṛtiko naraḥ 56
śyāmakeśaḥ kṣamī sthūlo bahuvīryo mahābalaḥ
svapne jalāśayālokī śleṣmaprakṛtiko naraḥ 57
dṛśyate prakṛtau yatra rūpaṃ doṣadvayasya tu
dvisaṃsargeṇa jānīyātsarvaliṅgaistridoṣajam 58
vibhutvādāśukāritvādvalitvādanyakopanāt
svātantryādbahurogatvāddoṣāṇāṃ prabalo'nilaḥ 59
prāyo'ta eva pavanādhyuṣitā manuṣyā doṣātmakāḥ sphuṭitadhūsarakeśagātrāḥ
śītadviṣaścaladhṛtismṛtibuddhiceṣṭāsauhārdadṛṣṭigatayo'tibahupralāpāḥ 60
alpapittabalajīvitanidrāḥ sannasaktacalajarjaravācaḥ
nāstikā bahubhujaḥ savilāsā gītahāsyamṛgayākalilolāḥ 61
madhurāmlapaṭūṣṇasātmyakāṃkṣāḥ kṛśadīrghākṛtayaḥ saśabdayātāḥ
na dṛḍhā na jitendri yā na cāryā na ca kāntādayitā bahuprajā vā 62
netrāṇi caiṣāṃ kharadhūsarāṇi vṛttānyacārūṇi mṛtopamāni
unmīlitānīva bhavanti supte śailadrumāṃste gaganaṃ ca yānti 63
adhanyā matsarādhmātāḥ stenā prodbaddhapiṇḍikāḥ
śvaśṛgāloṣṭragṛdhrākhukākānūkāśca vātikāḥ 64
pittaṃ vahnirvahnijaṃ vā yadasmātpittodri ktastīkṣṇatṛṣṇābubhukṣaḥ
gauroṣṇāṅgastāmrahastāṅghrivaktraḥ śūro mānī piṅgakeśo'lparomā 65
dayitamālyavilepanamaṇḍanaḥ sucaritaḥ śucirāśritavatsalaḥ
vibhavasāhasabuddhibalānvito bhavati bhīṣu gatirdviṣatāmapi 66
medhāvī praśithilasandhibandhamāṃso nārīṇāmanabhimato'lpaśukrakāmaḥ
āvāsaḥ palitataraṃganīlikānāṃ bhuktānnaṃ madhurakaṣāyatiktaśītam 67
dharmadveṣī svedanaḥ pūtigandhirbhūryuccārakrodhapānāśanerṣyaḥ
suptaṃ paśyetkarṇikārān palāśān digdāholkāvidyudarkānalāṃśca 68
tanūni piṅgāni calāni caiṣāṃ tanvalpapakṣmāṇi himapriyāṇi
krodhena madyena raveśca bhāsā rāgaṃ vrajantyāśu vilocanāni 69
madhyāyuṣo madhyabalāḥ paṇḍitāḥ kleśabhīravaḥ
vyāghrarkṣakapimārjāravṛkānūkāśca paittikāḥ 70
śleṣmā somaṃ śleṣmalastena saumyo gūḍhasnigdhaśliṣṭasandhyasthimāṃsaḥ
kṣuttṛḍduḥkhakleśadharmairatapto buddhyā yuktaḥ sāttvikaḥ satyasandhaḥ 71
priyaṃgudūrvāśarakāṇḍadarbhagorocanāpadmasuvarṇavarṇaḥ
pralambabāhuḥ pṛthupīnavakṣā mahālalāṭo ghananīlakeśaḥ 72
mṛdvaṅgaḥ samasuvibhaktacārudeho bahvojoratirasaśukraputrabhṛtyaḥ
dharmātmā vadati na niṣṭhuraṃ ca jātu pracchannaṃ vahati dṛḍhaṃ ciraṃ ca vairam 73
samadadviradendra tulyayāto jaladāmbhodhimṛdaṅgasiṃhaghoṣaḥ
smṛtimānabhiyogavānvinīto na ca bālye'pyatirodano na lolaḥ 74
tiktaṃ kaṣāyaṃ kaṭukoṣṇarūkṣamalpaṃ sa bhuṃkte balavāṃstathā'pi
raktāntasusnigdhaviśāladīrghasuvyaktaśuklāsitapakṣmalākṣaḥ 75
alpavyāhārakrodhapānāśanerṣyaḥ prājyāyurvitto dīrghadarśī vadānyaḥ
śrāddho gambhīraḥ sthūlalakṣyaḥ kṣamāvānāryonidrā lurdīrghasūtraḥ kṛtajñaḥ 76
ṛjurvipaścitsubhagaḥ salajjo bhakto gurūṇāṃ sthirasauhṛdaśca
svapne sapadmānsavihaṅgamālāṃstoyāśayānpaśyati toyadāṃśca 77
brahmarudre ndra varuṇatārkṣyahaṃsagajādhipaiḥ
śleṣmaprakṛtayastulyāstathā siṃhāśvagovṛṣaiḥ 78
viṣajāto yathā kīṭo na viṣeṇa prabādhyate
tadvatprakṛtayo martyaṃ śaknuvanti na bādhitum 79
prakopo vā'nyabhāvo vā śamo vā nopajāyate
prakṛtīnāṃ svabhāvena jāyate tu gatāyuṣaḥ 80
iti śrīmiśralaṭakana tanaya śrīmanmiśrabhāva viracite
bhāvaprakāśe bālaprakaraṇaṃ caturtham 4
atha pañcamaṃ dinacaryādiprakaraṇam 5
bhūmideśastridhā'nūpo jāṃgalo miśralakṣaṇaḥ 1
nadīpalvalaśailāḍhyaḥ phullotpalakulairyutaḥ
haṃsasārasakāraṃḍacakravākādisevitaḥ 2
śaśavārāhamahiṣarururohikulākulaḥ
prabhūtadrumapuṣpāḍhyo nīlaśasyaphalānvitaḥ 3
anekaśālikedārakadalīkṣuvibhūṣitaḥ
anūpadeśo jñātavyo vātaśleṣmāmayārttimān 4
ākāśasama uccaśca svalpapānīyapādapaḥ
śamīkarīrabilvārkapīlukarkaṃdhusaṅkulaḥ 5
hariṇaiṇarkṣapṛṣatagokarṇakharasaṅkulaḥ
susvāduphalavāndeśo vātalo jāṅgalaḥ smṛtaḥ 6
bahūdakanago'nūpaḥ kaphamārutarogavān
jāṅgalo'lpāmbuśākhī ca pittāsṛṅmārutottaraḥ 7
saṃsṛṣṭalakṣaṇo yastu deśaḥ sādhāraṇo mataḥ
samāḥ sadhāraṇe yasmācchītavarṣoṣṇamārutāḥ
samatā tena doṣāṇāṃ tasmātsādhāraṇo varaḥ 8
ucite vartamānasya nāsti durdeśajaṃ bhayam
āhārasvapnaceṣṭā'dau taddeśasya kṛte sati 9
yasya deśasya yo jantustajjaṃ tasyauṣadhaṃ hitam
deśādanyatra vasatastattulyaguṇamauṣadham 10
sve deśenicitā doṣā anyasminkopamāgatāḥ
balavantastathā na syurjalajāḥ sthalajāstathā 11
mānavo yena vidhinā svasthastiṣṭhati sarvadā
tameva kārayedvaidyo yataḥ svāsthyaṃ sadepsitam 12
dinacaryāṃ niśācaryāmṛtucaryāṃ yathoditām
ācaranpuruṣaḥ svasthaḥ sadā tiṣṭhati nānyathā 13
samadoṣaḥ samāgniśca samadhātumalakriyaḥ
prasannātmendri yamanāḥ svastha ityabhidhīyate 14
brāhme muhūrte budhyeta svastho rakṣā'thamāyuṣaḥ
tatra sarvāghaśāntyarthaṃ smareddhi madhusūdanam 15
dadhyājyādarśasiddhārthaṃ bilvagorocanasrajām
darśanaṃ sparśanaṃ kāryaṃ prabuddhena śubhāvaham 16
svamānanaṃ ghṛte paśyed yadīccheccirajīvitam
āyuṣyamuṣasi proktaṃ malādīnāṃ visarjanam
tadantrakūjanādhmānodaragauravavāraṇam 17
āṭopaśūlau parikarttikā ca saṅgaḥ purīṣasya tathordhvavātaḥ
purīṣamāsyādathavā nireti purīṣavege'bhihate narasya 18
vātamūtrapurīṣāṇāṃ saṅgo dhmānaṃ klamo rujā
jaṭhare vātajāścānyerogāḥ syurvātanigrahāt 19
bastimehanayoḥ śūlaṃ mūtrakṛcchraṃ śirorujā
vināmo vaṅkṣaṇānāhaḥ syāliṅgaṃ mūtranigrahe 20
na vegito'nyakāryaḥ syānna vegānīrayedbalāt
kāmaśokabhayakrodhānmanovegānvidhārayet 21
gudādimalamārgāṇāṃ śaucaṃ kāntibalapradam
pavitrakaramākhyātamalakṣmīkalipāpahṛt 22
prakṣālanaṃ mataṃ pāṇyoḥ pādayoḥ śuddhikāraṇam
malaśramaharaṃ vṛṣyaṃ cakṣuṣyaṃ rājasāpaham 23
bhakṣayeddantapavanaṃ dvādaśāṅgulamāyatam
kaniṣṭhikā'gravatsthūlamṛjvagranthiṃ tathā'vraṇam 24
ekaikaṃ gharṣayeddantaṃ mṛdunā kūrcakena tu
dantaśodhanacūrṇena dantamāṃsānyabādhayan 25
kṣaudra trikaṭukāktena tailasindhubhavena vā
cūrṇena tejovatyāśca dantānnityaṃ viśodhayet 26
madhuko madhure śreṣṭhaḥ karañjaḥ kaṭuke tathā
nimbaḥ syāttiktake śreṣṭhaḥ kaṣāye khadirastathā 27
samayaṃ tu samālokya doṣaṃ ca prakṛtiṃ tathā
yathocitai rasairvīryairyuktaṃ dra vyaṃ prayojayet 28
tenāsya mukhavairasyadantajihvā'syajā gadāḥ
rucivaiśadyalaghutā na bhavanti bhavanti ca 29
arke vīryaṃ vaṭe dīptiḥ karañje vijayo bhavet
plakṣe caivārthasampattirvaidaryāṃ madhurodhvaniḥ 30
khadire mukhasaugandhyaṃ bilve tu vipulaṃ dhanam
udumbare tu vāksiddhirāmre tvārogyameva ca 31
kadambe tu dhṛtirmedhā campake ca dṛḍhā matiḥ
śirīṣe kīrttisaubhāgyamāyurārogyameva ca 32
apāmārge dhṛtirmedhā prajñāśaktistathā'sane
dāḍimyāṃ sundarākāraḥ kakubhe kuṭaje tathā 33
jātītagaramandārairduḥsvapnaṃ ca vinaśyati 34
gurvākastālahintālau ketakaśca bṛhattṛṇaḥ
kharjūraṃ nārikelaṃ ca saptaite tṛṇarājakāḥ 35
tṛṇarājasamutpannaṃ yaḥ kuryāddantadhāvanam
naraścāṇḍālayoniḥ syādyāvadgaṅgāṃ na paśyati 36
na khādedgalatālvoṣṭhajihvādantagadeṣu tat
mukhasya pāke śothe ca śvāsakāsavamīṣu ca 37
durbalo'jīrṇabhuktaśca hikkāmūrcchāmadānvitaḥ
śirorujārtastṛṣitaḥ śrāntaḥ pānaklamānvitaḥ 38
arditaḥ karṇaśūlī ca netrarogī navajvarī
varjayeddantakāṣṭhaṃ tu hṛdāmayayuto'pi ca 39
jihvānirlekhanaṃ haimaṃ rājataṃ tāmrajaṃ tathā
pāṭitaṃ mṛdu tatkāṣṭhaṃ mṛdupatramayaṃ tathā 40
daśāṅgulaṃ mṛdu snigdhaṃ tena jihvāṃ likhetsukham
tajjihvāmalavairasyadurgandhajaḍatāharam 41
gaṇḍūṣamapi kurvīta śītena payasā muhuḥ
kaphatṛṣṇāmalaharaṃ mukhāntaḥśuddhikārakam 42
sukhoṣṇodakagaṇḍūṣaḥ kaphārucimalāpahaḥ
dantajāḍyaharaścāpi mukhalāghavakārakaḥ 43
viṣamūrcchāmadārttānāṃ śoṣiṇāṃ raktapittinām
kupitākṣimalakṣīṇarūkṣāṇāṃ sa na śasyate 44
mukhaprakṣālanaṃ śītapayasā raktapittajit
mukhasya piḍikāśoṣanīlikāvyaṅganāśanam 45
kuryādvā'pi kaduṣṇena payasā'syaviśodhanam
kaphavātaharaṃ snigdhaṃ mukhaśoṣavināśanam 46
kaṭutailādi nasyārthe nityābhyāsena yojayet
prātaḥ śleṣmaṇi madhyāhne pitte sāyaṃ samīraṇe 47
sugandhavadanāḥ snigdhaniḥsvanā vimalendri yāḥ
nirbalīpalitavyaṅgā bhaveyurnasyaśīlinaḥ 48
sauvīramañjanaṃ nityaṃ hitamakṣṇostato bhajet
locane bhavatastena manojñe sūkṣmadarśane 49
sroto'ñjanaṃ mataṃ śreṣṭhaṃ viśuddhaṃ sindhusambhavam
dṛṣṭeḥ kaṇḍūmalaharaṃ dāhakledarujāpaham 50
akṣṇo rūpāvahaṃ caiva sahate mārutātapau
netre rogā na jāyante tasmādañjanamācaret 51
rātrau jāgaritaḥ śrāntaśchardito bhuktavāṃstathā
jvarāturaḥ śiraḥ snāto nākṣṇorañjanamācaret 52
pañcarātrānnakhaśmaśrukeśaromāṇi karttayet
keśaśmaśrunakhādīnāṃ karttanaṃ samprasādhanam
pauṣṭikaṃ dhanyamāyuṣyaṃ śaucakāntikaraṃ param 53
utpāṭayettu lomāni nāsāyā na kadācana
tadutpāṭanato dṛṣṭerdaurbalyaṃ tvarayā bhavet 54
keśapāśe prakurvīta prasādhanyā prasādhanam
keśaprasādhanaṃ keśyaṃ rajojantumalāpaham 55
ādarśālokanaṃ proktaṃ maṅgalyaṃ kāntikāram
pauṣṭikaṃ balyamāyuṣyaṃ pāpālakṣmīvināśanam 56
lāghavaṃ karmasamarthyaṃ vibhaktaghanagātratā
doṣakṣayo'gnivṛddhiśca vyāyāmādupajāyate 57
vyāyāmadṛḍhagātrasya vyādhirnāsti kadācana
viruddhaṃ vā vidagdhaṃ vā bhuktaṃ śīghraṃ vipacyate 58
bhavanti śīghraṃ naitasya dehe śithilatādayaḥ
na caivaṃ sahasā'kramya jarā samadhirohati 59
na cāsti sadṛśaṃ tena kiñcitsthaulyāpakarṣakam
sa sadā guṇamādhatte balināṃ snigdhabhojinām 60
vasante śītasamaye sutarāṃ sa hito mataḥ
anyadā'pi ca karttavyo balārdhena yathā balam 61
hṛdayastho yadā vāyurvaktraṃ śīghraṃ prapadyate
mukhaṃ ca śoṣaṃ labhate tadbalārdhasya lakṣaṇam 62
kiṃ vā lalāṭe nāsāyāṃ gātrasandhiṣu kakṣayoḥ
yadā sañjāyate svedo balārdhaṃ tu tadādiśet 63
bhuktavānkṛtasambhogaḥ kāsī śvāsī kṛśaḥ kṣayī
raktapittī kṣatī śoṣī na taṃ kuryātkadācana 64
ativyāyāmataḥ kāso jvaraśchardiṃ śramaḥ klamaḥ
tṛṣṇā kṣayaḥ pratamako raktapittaṃ ca jāyate 65
abhyaṅgaṃ kārayennityaṃ sarveṣvaṅgeṣu puṣṭidam
śiraḥ śravaṇapādeṣu taṃ viśeṣeṇa śīlayet 66
sārṣapaṃ gandhatailaṃ ca yattailaṃ puṣpavāsitam
anyadra vyayutaṃ tailaṃ na duṣyati kadācana 67
abhyaṅgo vātakaphahṛcchramaśāntibalaṃ sukham
nidrā varṇamṛdutvāyuṣkurute dehapuṣṭikṛt 68
abhyaṅgaḥ śīlito mūrdhni sakalendri yatarpakaḥ
dṛṣṭituṣṭikaro hanti śirobhūmigatāngadān 69
keśānāṃ bahutāṃ dārḍhyaṃ mṛdutāṃ dīrghatāṃ tathā
kṛṣṇatāṃ kurute kuryācchirasaḥpūrṇatāmapi 70
na karṇarogā na malaṃ na ca manyāhanugrahaḥ
noccaiḥ śrutirna bādhiryaṃ syānnityaṃ karṇapūraṇāt 71
rasādyaiḥ pūraṇaṃ karṇe bhojanātprākpraśasyate
tailādyaiḥ pūraṇaṃ karṇe bhāskare'stamupāgate 72
pādābhyaṅgaśca tatsthairyanidrā dṛṣṭiprasādakṛt
pādasuptiśramastambhasaṅkocasphuṭanapraṇut 73
vyāyāmakṣuṇṇavapuṣaṃ padbhyāṃ sammarditaṃ tathā
vyādhayo nopasarpanti vainateyamivoragāḥ 74
lomakūpaśirājāladhamanībhiḥ kalevare
tarpayedbalamādhatte snehayukte'vagāhane 75
adbhiḥ saṃsiktamūlānāṃ tarūṇāṃ pallavādayaḥ
varddhante hi tathā nṝṇāṃ snehasaṃsiktadhātavaḥ 76
navajvarī ajīrṇī ca nābhyaktavyaḥ kathañcana
tathā virikto vāntaśca nirūḍho yaśca mānavaḥ 77
pūrvayoḥ kṛcchratā vyādherasādhyatvamathāpi vā
śeṣāṇāṃ ca tviha proktā vahnisādādayo gadāḥ 78
udvarttanaṃ kaphaharaṃ medoghnaṃ śukradaṃ param
balyaṃ śoṇitakṛccāpi tvakprasādamṛdutvakṛt 79
mukhalepādṛḍhaṃ cakṣuḥ pīno gaṇḍastathā'nanam
kāntamavyaṅgapiḍakaṃ bhavetkamalasannibham 80
dīpanaṃvṛṣyamāyuṣyaṃ snānamojobalapradam
kaṇḍūmalaśramasvedatandrā tṛḍdāhapāpmanut 81
bāhyaiśca sekaiḥ śītādyairūṣmā'ntaryāti pīḍitaḥ
narasya snānamātrasya dīpyate tena pāvakaḥ 82
śītena payasā snānaṃ raktapittapraśāntikṛt
tadevoṣṇena toyena balyaṃ vātakaphāpaham 83
śiraḥ snānamacakṣuṣyamatyuṣṇenāgbunā sadā
vātaśleṣmaprakope tu hitaṃ tacca prakīrttitam 84
aśītenāmbhasā snānaṃ payaḥpānaṃ navāḥstriyaḥ
etadvo mānavāḥ pathyaṃ snigdhamalpaṃ ca bhojanam 85
yaḥ sadā'malakaiḥ snānaṃ karoti sa viniścitam
balīpalitanirmukto jīvedvarṣaśataṃ naraḥ 86
snānaṃ jvare'tisāre ca netrakarṇānilārtiṣu
ādhmānapīnasājīrṇabhuktavatsu ca garhitam 87
snānasyānantaraṃ samyagvastreṇāṅgasya mārjanam
kāntipradaṃ śarīrasya kaṇḍūtvagdoṣanāśanam 88
kauśeyaurṇikavastraṃ ca raktavastraṃ tathaiva ca
vātaśleṣmaharaṃ tattu śītakāle vidhārayet 89
medhyaṃ suśītaṃ pittaghnaṃ kaṣāyaṃ vastramucyate
taddhārayeduṣṇakāle tatrāpi laghu śasyate 90
śuklaṃ tu śubhadaṃ vastraṃ śītātapanivāraṇam
na coṣṇaṃ na ca vā śītaṃ tattuvarṣāsu dhārayet 91
yaśasyaṃ kāmyamāyuṣyaṃ śrīmadānandavardhanam
tvacyaṃ vaśīkaraṃ rucyaṃ navanirmalamambaram 92
kadā'pi na janaiḥ sadbhirdhāryaṃ malinamambaram
tattu kaṇḍūkṛmikaraṃ glānyalakṣmīkaraṃ param 93
kuṅkumaṃ candanaṃ cāpi kṛṣṇāguru ca miśritam
uṣṇaṃ vātakaphadhvaṃsi śītakāle tadiṣyate 94
candanaṃ ghanasāreṇa bālakena ca miśritam
sugandhi paramaṃ śītamuṣṇakāle praśasyate 95
candanaṃ ghusṛṇopetaṃ mṛganābhisamāyutam
na coṣṇaṃ na ca vā śītaṃ varṣākāle tadiṣyate 96
anulepastṛṣāmūrcchādurgandhasvedadāhajit
saubhāgyatejastvagvarṇaprītyaujobalavardhanaḥ
sa snānānarhalokānāmamulepo'pi no hitaḥ 97
sugandhipuṣpapatrāṇāṃ dhāraṇaṃ kāntikārakam
pāparakṣograhaharaṃ kāmadaṃ śrīvivarddhanam 98
bhūṣaṇairbhūṣayedaṅgaṃ yathāyogyaṃ vidhānataḥ
śucisaubhāgyasantoṣadāyakaṃ kāñcanaṃ smṛtam 99
grahadṛṣṭiharaṃ puṣṭikaraṃ duḥsvapnanāśanam
pāpadaurbhāgyaśamanaṃ ratnābharaṇadhāraṇam 100
māṇikyaṃ taraṇeḥ sujātamamalaṃ muktāphalaṃ śītago
rmāheyasya ca vidrumo nigaditaḥ saumyasya gārutmatam
devejyasya ca puṣparāgamasurācāryasya vajraṃ śane
rnīlaṃ nirmalamanyayośca gadite gomedavaidūryake 101
vāsaḥśṛṅgāraratnānāṃ dhāraṇaṃ prītibardhakam
rakṣoghnamarthyamojasyaṃ saubhāgyakaramuttamam 102
satataṃ siddhamantrasya mahauṣadhyāstathaiva ca
rocanāsarṣapādīnāṃ maṅgalyānāṃ ca dhāraṇam 103
āyurlakṣmīkaraṃ rakṣoharaṃ maṅgaladaṃ śubham
hiṃsnādibhayavidhvaṃsi vaśīkaraṇakāraṇam 104
tato bhojanavelāyāṃ kuryānmaṅgalyadarśanam
tasya pradarśanaṃ nityamāyurdharmavivardhanam 105
loke'sminmaṅgalānyaṣṭau brāhmaṇo gaurhutāśanaḥ
puṣpasraksarpirāditya āpo rājā tathā'ṣṭamaḥ 106
pādukādhāraṇaṃ kuryātpūrvaṃ bhojanataḥ param
pādarogaharaṃ vṛṣyaṃ cakṣuṣyaṃ cāyuṣo hitam 107
śarīre jāyate nityaṃ vāñchā nṝṇāṃ caturvidhaḥ
bubhukṣā ca pipāsā ca suṣupsā ca ratispṛhā 108
bhojanecchāvighātātsyādaṅgamardo'ruciḥ śramaḥ
tandrā locanadaurbalya dhātudāho balakṣayaḥ 109
vighātena pipāsāyāḥ śoṣaḥ kaṇṭhāsyayorbhavet
śravaṇasyāvarodhaśca raktaśoṣo hṛdi vyathā 110
nidrā vighātato jṛmbhā śirolocanagauravam
aṅgamardastathā tandrā syādannāpāka eva ca 111
bubhukṣito na yo'śnāti tasyāhārendhanakṣayāt
mandībhavati kāyāgniryathā cāgnirnirindhanaḥ 112
āhāraṃ pacati śikhī doṣānāhāravarjitaḥ
paced doṣakṣaye dhātūnprāṇāndhātukṣaye'pi ca 113
āhāraḥ prīṇanaḥ sadyo balakṛd dehadhāraṇaḥ
smṛtyāyuḥśaktivarṇaujaḥsattvaśobhāvivardhanaḥ 114
yathoktaguṇasampannaṃ naraḥ seveta bhojanam
vicārya doṣakālādīnkālayorubhayorapi 115
sāyaṃ prātarmanuṣyāṇāmaśanaṃ śrutibodhitam
nāntarā bhojanaṃ kuryādagnihotrasamo vidhiḥ 116
yāmamadhye na bhoktavyaṃ yāmayugmaṃ na laṅghayet
yāmamadhye rasotpattiryāmayugmādbalakṣayaḥ 117
kṣutsambhavati pakveṣu rasadoṣamaleṣu ca
kāle vā yadi vā'kāle so'nnakāla udāhṛtaḥ 118
udgāraśuddhirutsāho vegotsargo yathocitaḥ
laghutā kṣutpipāsā ca jīrṇāhārasya lakṣaṇam 119
āhāraṃ tu rahaḥ kuryānnirhāramapi sarvadā
ubhābhyāṃ lakṣmyupetaḥ syātprakāśe hīyate śriyā 120
āhāranirhāravihārayogāḥ sadaiva sadbhirvijane vidheyāḥ 121
pitṛmātṛsuhṛdvaidyapākakṛddhaṃsabarhiṇām
sārasasya cakorasya bhojane dṛṣṭiruttamā 122
dīnahīnakṣudhā'ttānāṃ pāpapāṣaṇḍarogiṇām
kukkuṭādeḥ śuno dṛṣṭirbhojane naiva śobhanā 123
doṣahṛd dṛṣṭidaṃ pathyaṃ haimaṃ bhojanabhājanam
raupyaṃ bhavati cakṣuṣyaṃ pittahṛtkaphavātakṛt 124
kāṃsyaṃ buddhipradaṃ rucyaṃ raktapittaprasādanam
paittalaṃ vātakṛdrū kṣamuṣṇaṃ kṛmikaphapraṇut 125
āyase kācapātre ca bhojanaṃ siddhikārakam
śothapāṇḍuharaṃ balyaṃ kāmalā'pahamuttamam 126
śaileye mṛṇmaye pātre bhojanaṃ śrīnivāraṇam
dārūdbhave viśeṣeṇa rucidaṃ śleṣmakāri ca
pātraṃ patramayaṃ rucyaṃ dīpanaṃ viṣapāpanut 127
jalapātraṃ tu tāmrasya tadabhāve mṛdo hitam 128
pavitraṃ śītalaṃ pātraṃ racitaṃ sphaṭikena yat
kācena racitaṃ tadvattathā vaidūryasambhavam 129
bhojanāgre sadā pathyaṃ lavaṇārdra kabhakṣaṇam
agnisandīpanaṃ rucyaṃ jihvākaṇṭhaviśodhanam 130
annaṃ brahmā raso viṣṇurbhoktā devo maheśvaraḥ
iti sañcintya bhuñjānaṃ dṛṣṭidoṣo na bādhate 131
añjanāgarbhasambhūtaṃ kumāraṃ brahmacāriṇam
dṛṣṭidoṣavināśāya hanumantaṃ smarāmyaham 132
aśnīyāttanmanā bhūtvā pūrvaṃ tu madhuraṃ rasam
madhye'mlalavaṇau paścātkaṭutiktakaṣāyakān 133
phalānyādau samaśnīyāddāḍimādīni buddhimān
vinā mocaphalaṃ tadvadvarjanīyā ca karkaṭī 134
mṛṇālabisaśālūkakandekṣuprabhṛtīnapi
pūrvameva hi bhojyāni na tu bhuktvā kadācana 135
guru piṣṭamayaṃ dra vyaṃ taṇḍulānpṛthukānapi
na jātu bhuktavānkhādenmātrāṃ khādedbubhukṣitaḥ 136
ghṛtapūrvaṃ samaśnīyātkaṭhinaṃ prāk tato mṛdu
ante punardra vāśī tu balādro geṇa muñcati 137
yadyatsvādutaraṃ tattadvidadhyāduttarottaram
bhuktvā yatprārthyate bhūyastaduktaṃ svādu bhojanam 138
saumanasyaṃ balaṃ puṣṭimutsāhaṃ vṛddhimāyuṣaḥ
svādu sañjanayatyannamasvādu ca viparyayam 139
atyuṣṇānnaṃ balaṃ hanti śītaṃ śuṣkaṃ ca durjaram
atiklinnaṃ glānikaraṃ yuktiyuktaṃ hi bhojanam 140
atidrutāśitāhāre guṇāndoṣānna vindati
bhojyaṃ śītamahṛdyaṃ ca syādvilambitamaśnataḥ 141
mandānalo naro dra vyaṃ mātrāguru vivarjayet
svabhāvataśca guru yattathā saṃskārato guru 142
mātrāgurustu mudgādirmāṣādiḥ prakṛterguruḥ
saṃskāraguru piṣṭānnaṃ proktamityupalakṣaṇam 143
āhāraṃ ṣaḍvidhaṃ cūṣyaṃ peyaṃ lehyaṃ tathaiva ca
bhojyaṃ bhakṣyaṃ tathā carvyaṃ guru vidyādyathottaram 144
gurūṇāmardhasauhityaṃ laghūnāṃ tṛptiriṣyate
dra vo dra vottaraścāpi na mātrāgururiṣyate 145
viśuṣkamannamabhyastaṃ na pākaṃ sādhu gacchati 146
śuṣkaṃ niruddhaṃ viṣṭambhi vahnivyāpādakṛdbhavet 147
na bhuktvā na radaiśchittvā na niśāyāṃ na vā bahūn
na jalāntaritānadbhiḥ saktūnadyānna kevalān 148
punardānaṃ pṛthakpānaṃ sāmiṣaṃ payasā niśi
sadantacchedamuṣṇaṃ ca sapta saktuṣu varjayet 149
saktūnāmāśu jīryeta mṛdutvādavalehikā 150
yathākāle'timātraṃ yat tadbhavedviṣamāśanam
bahustokamakāle vā jñeyaṃ tadviṣamāśanam 151
ālasyagauravāṭopasādāṃśca kurute'dhikam
hīnamātraṃ tanoḥkārśyaṃ karoti ca balakṣayam 152
aprāptakāle bhuñjāno hyasamarthatanurnaraḥ
tāṃstān vyādhīnavāpnoti maraṇaṃ cādhigacchati 153
kāletīteśnato jantorvāyunopahate'nale
kṛcchrādvipacyate bhuktaṃ na syādbhoktuṃ punaḥ spṛhā 154
kukṣerbhāgadvayaṃ bhojyaistṛtīye vāri pūrayet
vāyoḥ sañcāraṇārthāya caturthamavaśeṣayet 155
rasenānnasya rasanā prathamenopatarpitā
na tathā svādamāpnoti tataḥ śodhyāmbunāntarā 156
atyambupānānna vipacyate'nnamanambupānācca sa eva doṣaḥ
tasmānnaro vahnivivardhanāya muhurmuhurvāri pibedabhūri 157
bhuktasyādau jalaṃ pītaṃ kārśyamandāgnidoṣakṛt
madhye'gnidīpanaṃ śreṣṭhamante sthaulyakaphapradam 158
samasthūlakṛśā bhuktamadhyāntaprathamāmbupāḥ 159
tṛṣitastu na cāśnīyātkṣudhito na pibejjalam
tṛṣitastu bhavedgulmī kṣudhitastu jalodarī 160
aśnīyāttanmanā bhūtvā pūrvaṃ tu madhuraṃ rasam
madhye'mlalavaṇau paścātkaṭutiktakaṣāyakān 161
dugdhaṃ svādurasaṃ snigdhamojasyaṃ dhātuvardhanam
vātapittaharaṃ vṛṣyaṃ śleṣmalaṃ guru śītalam 162
vidāhīnyannapānāni yāni bhuṃṅkte hi mānavaḥ
tadvidāhapraśāntyarthaṃ bhojanānte payaḥ pibet 163
kuryātkṣīrāntamāhāraṃ na dadhyantaṃ kadācana
lavaṇāmlakaṭūṣṇāni vidāhīnyatti yāni tu
taddoṣaṃ hartumāhāraṃ madhureṇa samāpayet 164
balavacchatruvināśanena śatruhantuḥ kṣīṇatā ca dṛśyate tathā ca
nāśanātpratyanīkasya svayaṃ ca kṣīyate yathā
vahnisantaptalohasya taptatāṃ nāśayejjalam 165
nanu bhojanāvasānasamaye prayuktāḥ kaṭutiktakaṣāyarasāḥ kaphaṃ
śamayiṣyanti vātasya vṛddhiṃ vidhāsyantīti cetai tanna
kaṭvādīnāṃ kṣīṇaśaktitvāt tathā ca
yadekaṃ nāśayeddoṣaṃ tadanyaṃ vardhayetkutaḥ
nāśane hyekadoṣasya yatastatkṣīṇaśaktikam 166
jagdhāḥ sarve'pi gacchanti balino vaśyatāṃ rasāḥ
yathā prakupitā doṣā vaśaṃ yānti balīyasaḥ 167
evaṃ bhuktvā samācāmedrū kṣagrahaṇapūrvakam
bhojane dantalagnāni nirhṛtyācamanaṃ caret 168
dantāntaragataṃ cānnaṃ śodhanenāharecchanaiḥ
kuryādanirhṛtaṃ taddhi mukhasyāniṣṭagandhatām 169
dantalagnamanirhāryaṃ lepaṃ manyeta dantavat
na tatra bahuśaḥ kuryādyatnaṃ nirharaṇaṃ prati 170
ācamya jalayuktābhyāṃ pāṇibhyāṃ cakṣuṣī spṛśet
bhuktvā pāṇitalaṃ ghṛṣṭvā cakṣuṣoryadi dīyate
acireṇaiva tadvāri timirāṇi vyapohati 171
bhuktvā ca saṃsmarennityamagastyādīnsukhāvahān 172
viṣṇurātmā tathaivānnaṃ pariṇāmaśca vai yathā
satyena tena madbhuktaṃ jīryatvannamidaṃ tathā 173
agastiragnirvaḍavānalaśca bhuktaṃ mamānnaṃ jarayantvaśeṣam
sukhaṃ ca me tatpariṇāmasambhavaṃ yacchantvarogaṃ mama cāstu deham 174
aṅgārakamagastiṃ ca pāvakaṃ sūryamaśvinau
pañcaitānsaṃsmarennityaṃ bhuktaṃ tasyāśu jīryati 175
ityuccārya svahastena parimārjya tathodaram
anāyāsapradāyīni kuryātkarmāṇyatandri taḥ 176
jīrṇe'nne vardhate vāyurvidagdhe pittamedhate
bhuktamātre kaphaścāpi kramo'ya bhojanopari 177
dhūmenāpohya hṛdyairvā kaṣāyakaṭutiktakaiḥ
pūgaiḥ karpūrakastūrīlavaṃgasumanaḥ phalaiḥ 178
phalaiḥ kaṭukaṣāyairvā mukhavaiśadyakāribhiḥ
tāmbūlapatrasahitaiḥ sugandhairvā vicakṣaṇaḥ 179
ratau suptotthite snāte bhuktevānte ca saṃgare
sabhāyāṃ viduṣāṃ rājñāṃ kuryāttāmbūlacarvaṇam 180
tāmbūlamuktaṃ tīkṣṇoṣṇaṃ rocanaṃ tuvaraṃ saram
tiktaṃ kṣāroṣaṇaṃ kāmaraktapittakaraṃ laghu 181
vaśyaṃ śleṣmāsyadaurgandhyamalavātaśramāpaham
mukhavaiśadyasaugandhyakāntisauṣṭhavakārakam 182
hanudantamaladhvaṃsi jihvendri yaviśodhanam
mukhaprasekaśamanaṃ galāmayavināśanam 183
navaṃ tadeva madhuraṃ kaṣāyānurasaṃ guru
balāsajananaṃprāyaḥ patraśākaguṇaṃ smṛtam 184
vaṅgadeśodbhavaṃ parṇaṃ paraṃ kaṭurasaṃ saram
pācanaṃ pittajanakamuṣṇaṃ kaphaharaṃ smṛtam 185
parṇaṃ purāṇamakaṭu kṣullakaṃ tanu pāṇḍuram
viśeṣādguṇavadvedyamanvaddhīnaguṇaṃ smṛtam 186
pūgaṃ guru himaṃ rūkṣaṃ kaṣāyaṃ kaphapittanut
mohanaṃ dīpanaṃ rucyamāsyavairasyanāśanam 187
pūgaṃ syāt dṛḍhamadhyaṃ yatsvinnaṃ vā'pi tridoṣanut
sarasaṅgurvabhiṣyandi tad bhṛśaṃ vahnināśanam 188
khadiraṃ kaphapittaghnaścūrṇaṃ vātabalāsanut
saṃyogatastridoṣaghnaṃ saumanasyaṃ karoti ca 189
mukhavaiśadyasaugandhyakāntisauṣṭhavakārakam
prabhāte pūgamadhikaṃ madhyāhne khadiraṃ tathā
niśāsu cūrṇamadhikaṃ tāmbūlaṃ bhakṣayetsadā 190
āyuragre yaśo mūle lakṣmīrmadhye vyavasthitā
tasmādagraṃ tathā mūlaṃ madhyaṃ parṇasya varjayet 191
parṇamūle bhavedvyādhiḥ parṇāgre pāpasambhavaḥ
parṇamadhyaṃ haratyāyuḥ śirā buddhi vināśinī 192
ādyaṃ viṣopamaṃ pītaṃ dvitīyaṃ bhedi durjaram
tṛtīyādanupātavyaṃ sudhātulyaṃ rasāyanam 193
tāmbūlaṃ nātiseveta na virikto bubhukṣitaḥ 194
dehadṛkkeśadantāgniśrotravarṇabalakṣayaḥ
śoṣaḥ pittānilāsraṃ syādatitāmbūlacarvaṇāt 195
tāmbūlaṃ na hitaṃ dantadurbalekṣaṇarogiṇām
viṣamūrcchāmadārttānāṃ kṣayiṇāṃ raktapittinām 196
bhuktvā śatapadaṃ gacchecchanaistena tu jāyate
annasaṃghātaśaithilyaṃ grīvājānukaṭīṣu ca 197
bhuktvopaviśatastandrā śayānasya tu puṣṭatā
āyuścraṃkamamāṇasya mṛtyurdhāvati dhāvataḥ 198
śvāsānaṣṭau samuttānastān dviḥpārśve tu dakṣiṇe
tatastaddviguṇānvāme paścātsvapyādyathāsukham 199
vāmadiśāyāmanalo nābherūrdhve'sti jantūnām
tasmāttu vāmapārśve śayīta bhuktaprapākārtham 200
tridoṣaśamanī ṣaṭvā tūlī vātakaphāpahā
bhūśayyā vṛṃhaṇī vṛṣyā kāṣṭhapaṭṭī tu vātalā 201
bhūśayyā vātalā'tīvarūkṣā pittāsranāśinī
suśayyāśayanaṃ hṛdyaṃ puṣṭinidrā dhṛtipradam
śramānilaharaṃ vṛṣyaṃ viparītamato'nyathā 202
saṃvāhanaṃ māṃsaraktatvakprasādakaraṃ param
prītinidrā karaṃ vṛṣyaṃ kaphavātaśramāpaham 203
pravātaṃ raukṣyavaivarṇyastambhakṛddāhapittanut
svedamūrcchāpipāsāghnamapravātamato'nyathā 204
sukhaṃ pravātaṃ seveta grīṣme śaradi cāntarā
nirvātamāyuṣe sevyamārogyāya ca sarvadā 205
pūrvā'nilo guruḥ soṣṇaḥ snigdhaḥ pittāsnadūṣakaḥ
vidāhī vātalaḥ śrāntikaphaśoṣavatāṃ hitaḥ 206
svāduḥ paṭurabhiṣyandī tvagdoṣārśoviṣakṛmīn
sannipātaṃ jvaraṃ śvāsamāmavātaṃ ca kopayet 207
dakṣiṇaḥ pavanaḥ svāduḥ pittaraktaharo laghuḥ
vīryeṇa śītalo balyaścakṣuṣyo na tu vātalaḥ 208
paścimaḥ pavanastīkṣṇaḥ śoṣaṇo balahṛllaghuḥ
medaḥ pittakaphadhvaṃsī prabhañjanavivardhanaḥ 209
uttaro mārutaḥ śītaḥ snigdho doṣaprakopakṛt
kledanaḥ prakṛtisthānāṃ balado madhuro mṛduḥ 210
āgneyo dāhakṛdrū kṣo nairṛto na vidāhakṛt
vāyavyastu bhavettikta eśānaḥ kaṭukaḥ smṛtaḥ 211
viṣvagvāyuranāyuṣyaḥ prāṇināṃ bahurogakṛt
atastaṃ naiva seveta sevitaḥ syānna śarmaṇe 212
vyajanasyānilo dāhasvedamūrcchāśramāpahaḥ
tālavṛntabhavo vātastridoṣaśamako mataḥ 213
vaṃśabyajanajastūṣṇo raktapittaprakopaṇaḥ
cāmaro vastrasambhūto māyūro vetrajastathā 214
pūte doṣajitā vātāḥ snigdhāḥ hṛdyāḥ supūjitāḥ 215
divā svāpaṃ na kurvīta yatosau syātkaphāvahaḥ
grīṣmavarjyeṣu kāleṣu divāsvapno niṣidhyate 216
ucito hi divāsvapno nityaṃ yeṣāṃ śarīriṇām
vātādayaḥ prakupyanti teṣāmasvapatāṃ divā 217
vyāyāmapramadā'dhvavāhanaratān klāntānatīsāriṇaḥ
śūlaśvāsavatastṛṣāparigatān hikkāmarutpīḍitān
kṣīṇān kṣīṇakaphāñchiśūnmadahatānvṛddhān rasājīrṇino
rātrau jāgaritānnarānniraśanānkāmaṃ divā svāpayet 218
divā vā yadi vā rātrau nidrā sātmīkṛtā tu yaiḥ
na teṣāṃ svapatāṃ doṣo jāgratāṃ copajāyate 219
bhojanānantaraṃ nidrā vātaṃ harati pittahṛt
kaphaṃ karoti vapuṣaḥ puṣṭiṃ saukhyaṃ tanoti hi 220
śayanaṃ pittanāśāya vātanāśāya mardanam
vamanaṃ kaphanāśāya jvaranāśāya laṅghanam 221
āsīnaṃ ghūrṇitaṃ yattu nābhiṣyandi na rūkṣaṇam 222
śabdān sparśāṃśca rūpāṇi rasāngandhānmanaḥpriyān
bhuktavānapi seveta tenānnaṃ sādhu tiṣṭhati 223
śabdaḥ sparśastathā rūpaṃ raso gandho jugupsitaḥ
bhuktamaprayataṃ cānnamatihāsyaṃ ca vāmayet 224
śayanaṃ cāśanaṃ cāti na bhajenna dra vādhikam
nāgnyātapau na plavanaṃ na yānaṃ nāpi vāhanam 225
vyāyāmaṃ ca vyavāyaṃ ca dhāvanaṃ yānameva ca
yuṃddhaṃ gītaṃ ca pāṭhaṃ ca muhūrttaṃ bhuktavāṃstyajet 226
atyambupānādviṣamāśanācca sandhāraṇāt svapnaviparyayācca
kāle'pi sātmyaṃ laghu cāpi bhuktamannaṃ na pākaṃ bhajate narasya 227
īrṣyābhaya krodhasamanvitena lubdhena rugdainyanipīḍitena
vidveṣayuktena ca sevyamānamannaṃ na samyakparipākameti 228
ajīrṇe bhujyate yattu tadadhyaśanamucyate 229
prāgbhukte cānale mande dvirahni na samāharet 230
pūrva bhukte vidagdhe'nne bhuñjāno hanti pāvakam 231
sāyamāśe tvajīrṇe tu prātarbhuktaṃ viṣopamam 232
bhavedyadi prātarajīrṇaśaṅkā tadā'bhayāṃ nāgarasaindhavābhyām
vicūrṇitāṃ śītajalena bhuktvā bhuñjīta cānnaṃ mitamannakāle 233
āyuḥkṣayabhayādvidvānnāhni seveta kāminīm
avaśo yadi seveta tadā grīṣmavasantayoḥ 234
āsyā varṇakaphasthaulyasaukumāryasukhapradā
adhvā varṇakaphasthaulyasaukumāryavināśanaḥ 235
yattu caṃkramaṇaṃ nātidehapīḍākaraṃ bhavet
tadāyurbalamedhā'gnipradamindri yabodhanam 236
uṣṇīṣaṃ kāntikṛtkeśyaṃ rajovātakaphāpaham
laghu yacchasyate tasmād guru pittākṣirogakṛt 237
upānaddhāraṇaṃ netryamāyuṣyaṃ pādarogahṛt
sukhapracāramojasyaṃ vṛṣyaṃ ca parikīrtitam 238
pādābhyāmanupānadbhyāṃ sadā caṃkramaṇaṃ nṛṇām
anārogyamanāyuṣyamindri yaghnamadṛṣṭidam 239
chatrasya dhāraṇaṃ varṣātapavātarajo'paham
himaghnaṃ hitamakṣṇośca maṅgalyamapi kīrttitam 240
sattvotsāhavalasthairyadhairyatejovivardhanam
avaṣṭambhakaraṃ cāpi bhayaghnaṃ daṇḍadhāraṇam 241
ūrdhvācchādanasaṃyuktā śibikā sarvavallabhā
tasyāmārohaṇaṃ nṝṇāṃ tridoṣaśamakaṃ matam 242
vātaśleṣmagadārttānāmahitā bhramakṛttariḥ
pittānilakaro hastī lakṣmyāyuḥpuṣṭivarddhanaḥ 243
ghoṭakārohaṇaṃ vātapittāgniśramakṛnmatam
medovarṇakaphaghnaṃ ca hitaṃ tadvalināṃ param 244
ātapaḥ svedamūrcchā'srapittatṛṣṇāklamaśramān
dāhaṃ vivarṇatāṃ kuryādetāṃśchāyā vyapohati 245
vṛṣṭirvṛṣyā himā balyā nidrā 'lasyavidhāyinī
bhayāvahā mohakarī kuheli kaphavātalā 246
agnirvātakaphastambhaśītavepathunāśanaḥ
āmābhiṣyandaśamano raktapittaprakopaṇaḥ 247
sadyaḥ śleṣmakaro dhūmo netrayorahito bhṛśam
śirogauravakṛccāpi vātapittaṃ ca kopayet 248
maitrī sadbhiḥ samaṃ kuryātsnehaṃ satsu tu sarvathā
saṃsargaṃ sādhubhiḥ kuryādasatsaṅgaṃ parityajet 249
seveta devabhūdevavṛddhavaidyanṛpātithīn
vimukhānnārthinaḥ kuryānnāvamanyeta kānapi 250
gurūṇāṃ sannidhau tiṣṭhetsadaiva vinayānvitaḥ
pādaprasāraṇādīni tatra naiva samācaret 251
apakārapare'pi syādupakāraparaḥ pumān
ātmavatsakalānpaśyedvairiṇo dūrato vaset 252
na kañcidātmanaḥ śatruṃ nātmānaṃ kasyacidri pum
prakāśayennāpamānaṃ na ca niḥsnehatāṃ prabhoḥ 253
nātmānamudake paśyenna nagnaḥ praviśejjalam
tathā nājñātagāmbhīryaṃ na hiṃsraprāṇisevitam 254
kāle hitaṃ mitaṃ satyaṃ saṃvādi madhuraṃ vadet
bhuñjīta madhuraprāyaṃ snigdhaṃ kāle hitaṃ mitam 255
