na rātrau dadhi bhuñjīta na ca nirlavaṇaṃ tathā
nāmudgasūpaṃ nākṣaudraṃ na cāpyaghṛtaśarkaram 256
janasyāśayamālakṣya yo yathā parituṣyati
taṃ tathaivānuvarteta parārādhanapaṇḍitaḥ 257
naikaḥ sukhī na sarvatra viśvasto na ca śaṅkitaḥ
nodyamevirametkvāpi hetāvīrṣyetphale na tu 258
vegānna dhārayejjātu manovegānvidhārayet
na pīḍayedindri yāṇi na caitānyatilālayet 259
varṣā'tapādiṣu cchatrī daṇḍī rātrau bhayeṣu ca
sopānatkastanuṃ rakṣedvicaredyugamātradṛk 260
nadīṃ tarenna bāhubhyāṃ nāgniskandhamabhivrajet
sandigdhanāvaṃ vṛkṣaṃ ca nārohedduṣṭayānakam 261
nāsaṃvṛtamukhaḥ kuryātsabhāyāṃ ca vicakṣaṇaḥ
kāsaṃ hāsaṃ tathodgāraṃ jṛmbhaṇaṃ kṣavathuṃ tathā 262
nāsikāṃ na vikuṣṇīyānnāsītotkaṭakaḥ kvacit
nordhvajānuściraṃ tiṣṭhenna nakhena likhedbhuvam 263
sammārjanīrajo naiva dehe dadyātkadācana
na nakhena tṛṇaṃ chindyānnocchiṣṭo brāhmaṇaṃ spṛśet 264
noparaktaṃ na codyantaṃ nāstaṃ yātaṃ divākaram
sarvathā na samīkṣeta na jale pratibimbitam 265
nekṣeta satataṃ sūkṣmaṃ dīptāmedhyāpriyāṇi ca
paurandaraṃ dhanurnaiva darśayetkamapi kvacit 266
necchedbalavatā yuddhaṃ na bhāraṃ śirasā vahet
gātraṃ na vādayetkeśānhastena dhunuyānna ca 267
na gacchetpūjyayormadhye dampatyorantareṇa ca
riporannaṃ na bhuñjīta gaṇikā'nnamapi kvacit 268
pratibhūrna bhavetkvāpi na ca sākṣī vṛthā bhavet 269
sthāgaṃ na dhārayejjātu dyūtaṃ dūrātparityajet 270
viśvāsaṃ nācaretstrīṇāṃ tāḥ svataṃtrā na cārayet
rakṣaṇīyāḥ sadā yatnādyauvane tu viśeṣataḥ 271
na bhinne śayane svapyānnānekavivare'pi ca
naiko devālaye naiva rātrau tarutale'pi ca 272
evaṃ dināni gamayetsadācāraparaḥ sadā
tato rātriprayuktāni kuryātkarmāṇi mānavaḥ 273
ityācāraṃ samāsena bhāṣitaṃ yaḥ samācaret
sa viṃdatyāyurārogyaṃ prītiṃ dharmaṃ dhanaṃ yaśaḥ 274
etāni pañca karmāṇi sandhyāyāṃ varjayed budhaḥ
āhāraṃ maithunaṃ nidrāṃ saṃpāṭhaṃ gatimadhvani 275
bhojanājjāyate vyādhirmaithunādgarbhavaikṛtiḥ
nidra yā niḥsvatā pāṭhādāyurhānirgaterbhayam 276
jyotsnā śītā smarānandapradā tṛṭpittadāhahṛt
tato hīnaguṇaḥ kuryādavaśyāyo'nilaṃ kapham 277
tamo bhayāvahaṃ mohadiṅmohajanakaṃ bhavet
pittahṛtkaphahṛtkāmavardhanaṃ klamakṛcca tat 278
rātrau ca bhojanaṃ kuryātprathamapraharāntare
kiñcidūnaṃ samaśnīyād durjaraṃ tatra varjayet 279
śarīre jāyate nityaṃ dehinaḥ surataspṛhā
avyavāyānmehamedovṛddhiḥ śithilatā tanoḥ 280
bāleti gīyate nārī yāvadvarṣāṇi ṣoḍaśa
tatastu taruṇī jñeyā dvātriṃśadvatsarāvadhi 281
tadūrdhvamadhiruḍhā syātpañcāśadvatsarāvadhi
vṛddhā tatparato jñeyā suratotsavavarjitā 282
nidāghaśaradorvālā hitā viṣayiṇāṃ matā
taruṇī śītasamaye prauḍhā varṣāvasantayoḥ 283
nityaṃ bālā sevyamānā nityaṃ vardhayate valam
taruṇī hrāsayecchaktiṃ prauḍhodbhāvayate jarām 284
sadyo māṃsaṃ navaṃ cānnaṃ vālā strī kṣīrabhojanam
ghṛtamuṣṇodake snānaṃ sadyaḥ prāṇakarāṇiṣaṭ 285
pūtimāṃsaṃ striyo vṛddhā bālā'kastaruṇaṃ dadhi
prabhāte maithunaṃ nidrā sadyaḥ prāṇaharāṇi ṣaṭ 286
vṛddho'pi taruṇīṃ gatvā taruṇatvamavāpnuyāt
vayodhikāṃ striyaṃ gatvā taruṇaḥ sthavirāyate 287
āyuṣmanto mandajarā vapurvarṇabalānvitāḥ
sthiropacitamāṃsāśca bhavanti strīṣu saṃyatāḥ 288
seveta kāmataḥ kāmaṃ balādvājīkṛto hime
prakāmaṃ tu niṣeveta maithunaṃ śiśirāgame 289
tryahādvasantaśaradoḥ pakṣād vṛṣṭinidāghayoḥ 290
tribhistribhirahobhirhi sameyātpramadāṃ naraḥ
sarveṣvṛtuṣu gharme tu pakṣātpakṣād vrajed budhaḥ 291
śīte rātrau divā grīṣme vasante tu divā niśi
varṣāsu vāridadhvāne śaratsu sarasaḥ smaraḥ 292
upeyātpuruṣo nārīṃ saṃdhyayorna ca parvasu
gosarge cārddharātre ca tathā madhyadine'pi ca 293
vihāraṃ bhāryayā kuryād deśe'tiśayasaṃvṛte
ramye śrāvyāṅganāgāne sugandhe sukhamārute 294
deśe gurujanāsanne vivṛte'titrapākare
śrūyamāṇe vyathāhetuvacane na rameta nā 295
snātacandanaliptāṅgaḥ sugandhaḥ sumano'nvitaḥ
bhuktavṛṣyaḥ suvasanaḥ suveṣaḥ samalaṃkṛtaḥ 296
tāmbūlavadanaḥ patnyāmanurakto'dhikasmaraḥ
putrārthī puruṣo nārīmupeyācchayane śubhe 297
atyāśito'dhṛtiḥ kṣudvānsavyathāṅgaḥ pipāsitaḥ
bālo vṛddho'nyavegārttastyajedro gī ca maithunam 298
bhāryāṃ rūpaguṇopetāṃ tulyaśīlāṃ kulodbhavām
atikāmo'bhikāmāṃ tu hṛṣṭo hṛṣṭāmalaṃkṛtām
seveta pramadāṃ yuktyā vājīkaraṇavṛṃhitaḥ 299
rajasvalāmakāmāṃ ca malināmapriyāṃ tathā 300
varṇabṛddhāṃ vayovṛddhāṃ tathā vyādhiprapīḍitām
hīnāṅgīṃ garbhiṇīṃ dveṣyāṃ yonirogasamanvitām 301
sagotrāṃ gurupatnīṃ ca tathā pravrajitāmapi
nādhigacchet pumānnārīṃ bhūrivaiguṇyaśaṅkayā 302
rajasvalāṃ gatavato narasyāsaṃyatātmanaḥ
dṛṣṭyāyustejasāṃ hāniradharmaśca tato bhavet 303
liginīṃ gurupatnīṃ ca sagotrāmatha parvasu
vṛddhāṃ ca sandhyayoścāpi gacchato jīvanakṣayaḥ 304
garbhiṇyāṃ garbhapīḍā syādvyādhitāyāṃ balakṣayaḥ 305
hīnāṅgīṃ malināṃ dveṣyāṃ kṣāmāṃ vandhyāmasaṃvṛte
deśe'bhigacchato retaḥ kṣīṇaṃ mlānaṃ mano bhavet 306
kṣudhitaḥ kṣubdhacittaśca madhyāhne tṛṣito'balaḥ
sthitasya hāniṃ śukrasya vāyoḥ kopaṃ ca vindati 307
vyādhitasya rujā plīhā mūrcchā mṛtyuśca jāyate
pratyūṣe cārdharātre ca vātapitte prakpyataḥ 308
tiryagyonāvayonau vā duṣṭayonau tathaiva ca
upadaṃśastathā vāyoḥ kopaḥ śukrasukhakṣayaḥ 309
uccārite mūtrite ca retasaśca vidhāraṇe
uttāne ca bhavecchīghraṃ śukrāśmaryāstu sambhavaḥ 310
sarvametattyajettasmādyato lokadvayāhitam
śukraṃ tūpasthitaṃ mohānna sandhāryaṃ kadā cana 311
snānaṃ saśarkaraṃ kṣīraṃ bhakṣyamaikṣavasaṃskṛtam
vāto māṃsarasaḥ svapnaḥ suratānte hitā amī 312
śūlakāsajvaraśvāsakārśyapāṇḍvāmayakṣayāḥ
ativyavāyājjāyante rogāścākṣepakādayaḥ 313
rotrau jāgaraṇaṃ rūkṣaṃ kaphadoṣaviṣārtijit
nidrā tu sevitākāle dhātusāmyamatandri tām 314
puṣṭiṃ varṇaṃ balotsāhaṃ vahnidīptiṃ karoti hi 315
yo leḍhi śayanasamaye madhumiśraṃ bījapūradalacūrṇam
sa tu lajjākaravātaprasaranirodhātsukhaṃ svapiti 316
savituḥ samudayakāle prasṛtīḥ salilasya yaḥ pibedaṣṭau
rogajarāparimukto jīvedvatsaraśataṃ sāgram 317
arśaḥśothagrahaṇyo jvarajaṭharajarākuṣṭhamedovikārāḥ
mūtrāghātāsrapittaśravaṇagalaśiraḥśroṇiśūlākṣirogāḥ
ye cānye vātapittakṣatajakaphakṛtā vyādhayaḥ santi janto
stāṃstānabhyāsayogādapaharati payaḥ pītamante niśāyāḥ 318
vigataghananiśīthe prātarutthāya nityaṃ pibati khalu naro yo ghrāṇarandhreṇa vāri
sa bhavati matipūrṇaścakṣuṣā tārkṣyatulyo valipalitavihīnaḥ sarvarogairvimuktaḥ 319
pātavyaṃ nāsayā nīraṃ prasṛtitrayamātrayā 320
vyaṅgavalīpalitaghnaṃpīnasavaisvaryakāsaśothaharam
rajanīkṣaye'mbunasyaṃ rasāyanaṃ dṛṣṭisañjananam 321
snehe pīte kṣate śuddhāvādhmāne stimitodare
hikkāyāṃ kaphavātotthe vyādhau tadvāri vārayet 322
kṣayakopaśamā yasmindoṣāṇāṃ sambhavanti hi
ṛtuṣaṭkaṃ tadākhyātaṃ raveḥ rāśiṣu saṃkramāt 323
grīṣmo meṣabṛṣau proktaḥ prāvṛṇmithunakarkaṭau
siṃhakanye smṛtā varṣā tulāvṛścikayoḥ śarat 324
dhanurgrāhau ca hemanto vasantaḥ kumbhamīnayoḥ 325
śiśiraḥ puṣpasamayo grīṣmo varṣā śaraddhimāḥ
māghādimāsayugmaṃ syurṛtavaḥ ṣaṭ kramādamī 326
gaṅgāyā dakṣiṇe deśe vṛṣṭerbahulabhāvataḥ
ubhau munibhirākhyātau prāvṛḍvarṣābhidhāvṛtū 327
tisyāevottaredeśe himapracurabhāvataḥ
etāvubhau samākhyātau hemantaśiśirāvṛtū 328
uttarāyaṇamādyaistaiḥ paraiḥ syāddakṣiṇāyanam
ādyamuṣṇaṃ balaharaṃ tato'nyadbaladaṃ himam 329
hemantaṃ śītalaḥ snigdhaḥ svādurjaṭharavahnikṛt
śiśiraḥ śītalo'tīva rūkṣo vātāgnivardhanaḥ 330
vasanto madhuraḥ snigdhaḥ śleṣmavṛddhikaraśca saḥ
grīṣmo rūkṣo'tikaṭukaḥ pittakṛtkaphanāśanaḥ 331
varṣāḥ śītā vidāhinyo vahnimāndyānilapradāḥ
śaraduṣṇā pittakartrī nṛṇāṃ madhyabalāvahā 332
cayaprakopopaśamā vāyorgrīṣmādiṣu triṣu
varṣā'diṣu ca pittasya śleṣmaṇaḥ śiśirādiṣu 333
cīyate laghurūkṣābhiroṣadhībhiḥ samīraṇaḥ
tadvidhastadvidhe dehe kālasyauṣṇyānna kupyati 334
adbhiramlavipākābhiroṣadhībhiśca tādṛśam
pittaṃ yāti cayaṃ kopaṃ na tu kālasya śaityataḥ 335
cīyate snigdhaśītābhirudakauṣadhibhiḥ kaphaḥ
tulye ca kāle dehe ca skannatvānna prakupyati 336
hime yāti śamaṃ pittaṃ vāyuḥ śleṣmā ca cīyate
sa vāyuḥ śiśire kopaṃ yātyevopacitaḥ kaphaḥ 337
hemante sañcitaḥ śleṣmā śiśire tvaticīyate
śītasnigdhagurudra vyaiḥ śaityaskanno na kupyati 338
iti kālasvabhāvo'yamāhārādivaśātpunaḥ
cayādīn yānti sadyo'pi doṣāḥ kāle viśeṣataḥ 339
cayakopamaśamāndoṣā vihārāhārasevanaiḥ
samānairyāntyakāle'pi viparītairviparyayam 340
svasthānasthasya doṣasya vṛddhiḥ syātstabdhakoṣatā
pītāvabhāsatā vahnimandatā cāṅgagauravam 341
ālasyaṃ cayahetau tu dveṣaśca cayalakṣaṇam
sañcaye 'pahṛtā doṣā labhante nottarāṃ gatim 342
te tūttarāsu gatiṣu bhavanti balavattarāḥ 343
varṣāsuprabalo vāyustasmānmiṣṭādayastrayaḥ
rasāḥ sevyā viśeṣeṇa pavanasyopaśāntaye 344
bhavedvarṣāsu vapuṣaḥ klinnatvaṃ yadviśeṣataḥ
tatkledaśāntaye sevyā api kaṭvādayastrayaḥ 345
svedanaṃ mardanaṃ sevyaṃ dadhyuṣṇaṃjāṅgalāmiṣam
śālayo yavagodhūmā jalaṃ kaupaṃ jalaṃ cyutam 346
na bhajetpūrvapavanaṃ vṛṣṭiṃ gharmaṃ himaṃ śramam
nadītīraṃ divāsvapnaṃ rūkṣaṃ nityaṃ ca maithunam 347
sarpiḥ svādukaṣāyatiktakarasā yacchītalaṃ yallaghu
kṣīraṃ svacchasitaikṣavaḥ paṭurasaḥ svalpaṃ palaṃ jāṅgalam
godhūmā yavamudgaśālisahitā nādeyamaṃśūdakaṃ
candra ścandanamindurādirajanī mālyaṃ paṭo nirmalaḥ 348
divise'kakarairjuṣṭaṃ niśi śītakarāṃśubhiḥ
jñeyamaṃśūdakaṃ nāma snigdhaṃ doṣatrayāpaham
viśrāmaḥ suhṛdāṃ gaṇeṣu madhurā vācaḥ saraḥ krīḍanaṃ
pittānāṃ ca virecanaṃ balavato yuktaṃ śirāmokṣaṇam
etāṃyatra ghanāvasānasamayepathyāni muñceddadhivyāyāmāmlakaṭūṣṇatīkṣṇa divasasvapnaṃ himañcātapam 349
ikṣavaḥ śālayo mudgāḥ saro'mbha kvathitaṃ payaḥ
śaradyetāni pathyāni pradoṣe cenduraśmayaḥ 350
prātarbhojanamamlamiṣṭalavaṇānabhyaṅgagharmaśramān
godhūmaikṣavaśālimāṣapiśitaṃ piṣṭaṃ navānnaṃ tilān
kastūrīṃ varakuṅkumāguruyutāmuṣṇāmbuśaucaṃ tathau snigdhaṃ strīṣu sukhaṃ gurūṣṇavasanaṃ seveta hemantake 351
śiśire śītamadhikaṃ raukṣyaṃ cādānakālajam
viśeṣatastatastatra hemantasya mato vidhiḥ 352
vāntiṃ nasyamathābhayāṃ ca madhunā vyāyāmamudvarttanaṃ
saṃseveta madhau kaphaghnakavalaṃ śūlyaṃ palaṃ jāṅgalam
godhūmānbahuśālibhedasahitānmudgānyavānṣaṣṭikāṃ
llepañcandanakuṅkumāgurukṛtaṃ rūkṣaṃ kaṭūṣṇaṃ laghu 353
miṣṭamamlaṃ dadhisnigdhaṃ divāsvapnaṃ ca durjaram
avaśyāyamapi prājño vasante parivarjayet 354
svādusnigdhahimaṃ laghu dra vamayaṃ dra vyaṃ rasālāṃ sitāṃ
saktuṃkṣīramajāṅgalāni sitayā śāli rasaṃ māṃsajam
śītāṃśuṃ śayanaṃ divā malayajaṃ śītaṃ payaḥ pānakaṃ
sevetoṣṇadine tyajettu kaṭukakṣārāmlagharmaśramān 355
ṝtuṣveṣu ya etaistu vidhibhirvarttate naraḥ
doṣānṛtukṛtānnaiva labhate sa kadācana 356
iti śrīlaṭakana tanaya śrīmanmiśrabhāva viracite bhāvaprakāśe
<1.1.5>
dinacaryādiprakaraṇaṃ pañcamam 5
atha ṣaṣṭhaṃ miśraprakaraṇam 6
rogastu doṣavaiṣamyaṃ doṣasāmyamarogatā
rogā duḥkhasya dātāro jvaraprabhṛtayo hi te 1
te ca svābhāvikāḥ kecitkecidāgantavaḥ smṛtāḥ
mānasāḥ kecidākhyātāḥ kathitāḥ ke'pi kāyikāḥ 2
karmajāḥ kathitāḥ keciddoṣajāḥ santi cāpare
karmadoṣodbhavāścānye vyādhayastrividhāḥ smṛtāḥ 3
karmakṣayātkarmakṛtā doṣajāḥ svasvabheṣajaiḥ
karmadoṣodbhavā yānti karmadoṣakṣayāt kṣayam 4
sādhyā yāpyā asādhyāśca vyādhayastrividhāḥ smṛtāḥ
sukhasādhyaḥ kaṣṭasādhyo dvividhaḥ sādhya ucyate 5
yāpanīyaṃ tu taṃ vidyātkriyā dhārayate hi yam
kriyāyāṃ tu nivṛttāyāṃ sadyo yaśca vinaśyati 6
prāptā kriyā dhārayati sukhinaṃ yāpyamāturam
prapatiṣyadivāgāraṃ stambho yatnena yojitaḥ 7
sādhyā yāpyatvamāyānti yāpyāścāsādhyatāṃ tathā
ghnanti prāṇānasādhyāstu narāṇāmakriyāvatām 8
rogārambhakadoṣasya prakopādupajāyate
yo'nyo vikāraḥ sa budhairupadra va ihoditaḥ 9
rogiṇo maraṇaṃ yasmādavaśyambhāvi lakṣyate
tallakṣaṇamariṣṭaṃ syādri ṣṭaṃ cāpi taducyate 10
yā kriyā vyādhiharaṇī sā cikitsā nigadyate
doṣadhātumalānāṃ yā sāmyakṛtsaiva rogṛhṛt 11
yābhiḥ kriyābhirjāyante śarīre dhātavaḥ samāḥ
sā cikitsā vikārāṇāṃ karma tadbhiṣajāṃ matam 12
yā hyudīrṇaṃ śamayati nānyaṃ vyādhiṃ karoti ca
sā kriyā na tu yā vyādhiṃ haratyanyamudīrayet 13
jātamātraścikitsyaḥ syānnopekṣyo'lpatayā gadaḥ
vahniśatruviṣaistulyaḥ svalpo'pi vikarotyasau 14
rogamādau parīkṣeta tato'nantaramauṣadham
tataḥ karma bhiṣak paścājjñānapūrvaṃ samācaret 15
yastu rogamavijñāya karmāṇyārabhate bhiṣak
apyauṣadhavidhānajñastasya siddhiryadṛcchayā 16
bheṣajaṃ kevalaṃ kartuṃ yo jānāti na cāmayam
vaidyakarma sa cetkuryādvadhamarhati rājataḥ 17
yastu kevalarogajño bheṣajeṣvavicakṣaṇaḥ
taṃ vaidyaṃ prāpya rogī syādyathā naurnāvikaṃ vinā 18
yastu kevalaśāstrajñaḥ kriyāsvakuśalo bhiṣak
sa muhyatyāturaṃ prāpya tathā bhīrurivāhavam 19
yastu rogaviśeṣajñaḥ sarvabhaiṣajyakovidaḥ
deśa kālavibhāgajñastasya siddhirna saṃśayaḥ 20
ādāvante rujāṃ jñāne prayateta cikitsakaḥ
bheṣajānāṃ vidhānena tataḥ kuryāccikitsatam 21
vikāranāmākuśalo na jihrīyātkadācana
na hi sarvavikārāṇāṃ nāmato'sti dhruvā sthitiḥ 22
nāsti rogo vinā doṣairyasmāttasmāccikitsakaḥ
anuktamapi doṣāṇāṃ liṅgairvyādhimupācaret 23
ye na kurvantyasādhyānāṃ cikitsāṃ te bhiṣagvarāḥ
ato vaidyaiḥ śramaḥ kāryaḥ sādhyāsādhyaparīkṣaṇe 24
śīte śītapratīkāramuṣṇe tūṣṇanivāraṇam
kṛtvā kuryātkriyāṃ prāptāṃ kriyākālaṃ na hāpayet 25
aprāpte vā kriyākāle prāpte vā na kriyā kṛtā
kriyāhīnā'tiriktā ca sādhyeṣvapi na sidhyati 26
vikāre'lpe mahatkarma kriyā laghvī garīyasi
dvayametadakauśalyaṃ kauśalyaṃ yuktakarmatā 27
kriyāyāstu guṇālābhe kriyāmanyāṃ prayojayet
pūrvasyāṃ śāntavegāyāṃ na kriyāsaṅkaro hitaḥ 28
kriyābhistulyarūpābhirna kriyāsaṅkaro hitaḥ
tābhistu bhinnarūpābhiḥ sāṅkaryaṃ naiva duṣyati 29
na caikāntena nirdiṣṭe śāstre niviśate budhaḥ
svayamapyatra bhiṣajā tarkaṇīyaṃ cikitsatā 30
utpadyate ca sā'vasthā doṣakālabalaṃ prati
yasyāṃ kāryamakāryaṃ syātkarma kāryaṃ vivarjitam 31
kvacidarthaḥ kvacinmaitrī kvaciddharmaḥ kvacidyaśaḥ
karmābhyāsaḥ kvacicceti cikitsā nāsti niṣphalā 32
āyurvedoditāṃ yuktiṃ kurvāṇāśca hitāya ye
puṇyāyurvṛddhisaṃyuktā nīrogāśca bhavanti te 33
naiva kurvīta lobhena cikitsāpuṇyavikrayam
īśvarāṇāṃ vasumatāṃ lipsetārthaṃ tu vṛttaye 34
cikitsitaṃ śarīraṃ yo na niṣkrīṇāti durmatiḥ
sa yatkaroti sukṛtaṃ sarvaṃ tadbhiṣagaśnute 35
na deśo manujairhīno na manuṣyā nirāmayāḥ
tataḥ sarvatra vaidyānāṃ susiddhā eva vṛttayaḥ 36
rogī dūto bhiṣagdīrghamāyurdra vyaṃ susevakaḥ
sadauṣadhaṃ cikitsāyā ityaṅgāni budhā jaguḥ 37
rogo yasyāstirogī sa sacikitsyastu yādṛśaḥ
yādṛśaścācikitsyo'pi vakṣyamāṇo niśamyatām 38
nijaprakṛtivarṇābhyāṃ yuktaḥ sattvena cakṣuṣā
cikitsyo bhiṣajāṃ rogī vaidyabhakto jitendri yaḥ 39
āyuṣmānsattvavānsādhyo dra vyavānmitravānapi
cikitsyo bhiṣajāṃ rogī vaidyavākyakṛdāstikaḥ 40
caṇḍaḥ sāhasiko bhīruḥ kṛtaghno vyagra eva ca
śokākulo mumūrṣuśca vihīnaḥ karaṇaiścaḥ yaḥ 41
vairī vaidyavidagdhaśca śraddhāhīnaśca śaṅkitaḥ
bhiṣajāmavidheyaḥ syurnopakramyā bhiṣagvidhāḥ 42
etānupācaran vaidyo vahūn doṣānavāpnuyāt 43
yaścikitsakamānetuṃ yāti dūtaḥ sa kathyate
sa ca yādṛk samucitastādṛgatra nigadyate 44
dūtāḥ sujātayo'vyaṅgāḥ paṭavo nirmalāmbarāḥ
sukhino'śvavṛṣārūḍhāḥ śubhrapuṣpaphalairyutāḥ 45
sajātayaḥ suceṣṭāśca sajīvadiśi saṅgatāḥ
bhiṣajaṃ samaye prāptā rogiṇaḥ sukhahetave 46
vaidyāhvānāya dūtasya gacchato rogiṇaḥ kṛte
na śubhaṃ saumyaśakunaṃ pradīptaṃ tu sukhāvaham 47
tathāhi riktahasto na paśyetturājānaṃ bhiṣajaṃ gurum
daivajñaṃ devatāṃ mitraṃ phalena phalamādiśet 48
cikitsāṃ kurute yastu sa cikitsaka ucyate
sa ca yādṛksamīcīnastādṛśo'pi nigadyate 49
tattvādhigataśāstrārtho dṛṣṭakarmā svayaṃkṛtī
laghuhastaḥ śuciḥ śūraḥ sajjopaskarabheṣajaḥ 50
pratyutpannamatirdhīmān vyavasāthī priyaṃvadaḥ
satyadharmaparo yaśca vaidya īdṛk praśasyate 51
kucailaḥ karkaśaḥ stabdho grāmīṇaḥ svayamāgataḥ
pañca vaidyā na pūjyante dhanvantarisamā yadi 52
vyādhestattvaparijñānaṃ vedanāyāśca nigrahaḥ
etadvaidyasya vaidyatvaṃ na vaidyaḥ prabhurāyuṣaḥ 53
bhiṣagādau parīkṣeta rugṇasyāyuḥ prayatnataḥ
tata āyuṣi vistīrṇe cikitsā saphalā bhavet 54
saumyā dṛṣṭirbhavedyasya śrotraṃ vaktraṃ tathaiva ca
svādu gandhaṃ vijānāti sa sādhyo nātra saṃśayaḥ 55
pāṇipādau ca yasyoṣṇau dāhaḥ svalpataro bhavet
jihvātu komalā yasya sa rogī na vinaśyati 56
svedahīno jvaro yasya śvāso nāsikayā caret
kaṇṭhaśca kaphahīnaḥ syātsa rogī jīvati dhruvam 57
yasya nidrā sukhena syāccharīraṃ dyutimadbhavet
indri yāṇi prasannāni sa rogī naiva naśyati 58
śarīraśīlayoryasya prakṛtervikṛtirbhavet
tadariṣṭaṃ samāsena vyāsataśca nibodha me 59
śṛṇoti vividhāñchabdānviparītāñchṛṇoti ca
yo na śṛṇoti cākasmāttaṃ vadanti gatāyuṣam 60
yastūṣṇamiva gṛhṇāti śītamuṣṇaṃ ca śītavat
uṣṇagātro'timātraṃ yo bhṛśaṃ śītena kampate 61
prahāraṃ naiva jānāti yo'ṅgacchedamathāpi vā
pāṃśunevāvakīrṇāni yaśca gātrāṇi manyate 62
varṇānyatā vā rājyo vā yasya gātre bhavanti hi
snātānuliptaṃ yaṃ cāpi bhajante nīlamākṣikāḥ 63
viparītena gṛhṇāti rasānyaścopayojitān
yo vā rasānna saṃvetti taṃ gatāsuṃ pracakṣate 64
sugandhaṃ vetti durgandhaṃ durgandhaṃ ca sugandhavat
gṛhṇāti yo'nyathā gandhaṃ śānte dīpe nirāmayaḥ 65
rātrau sūryaṃ jvalantaṃ vā divā vā candra varcasam
divā jyotīṃṣi yaścāpi jvalitānīva paśyati 66
vidyutvato'sitānmeghān gagane nirghane ghanān
vimānayānaprāsādairyaśca saṅkulamambaram 67
yaścānilaṃ mūrttimantamantarikṣe'valokate
dhūmanīhāravāsobhirāvṛtāmiva medinīm 68
pradīptamiva yo lokaṃ yo vāplutamivāmbhasā
bhūmimaṣṭāpadākārāṃ lekhābhiryaśca paśyati 69
yo na paśyati ṝkṣāṇi yaśca devīmarundhatīm
dhruvamākāśagaṅgāṃ ca taṃ vadanti gatāyuṣam 70
ādarśe'mbuni gharme vā chāyāṃ yaśca na paśyati
paśyatyekāṅgahīnāṃ vā vikṛtāṃ vā'nyasatvajām 71
śvakākakaṅkagṛdhrāṇāṃ pretānāṃ yakṣarakṣasām
āturo labhate mṛtyuṃ svastho vyādhimavāpnuyāt 72
hrīśriyau naśyato yasya teja ojaḥ smṛtiḥ prabhā
akasmācca bhajante yaṃ sa gatāsurasaṃśayam 73
yasyādharauṣṭhaḥ patitaḥ kṣiptaścordhvaṃ tathottaraḥ
ubhau vā jāmbavābhāsau durlabhaṃ tasya jīvitam 74
āraktā daśanā yasya śyāvā vā syuḥ patanti vā
khañjanapratibhā vāpi taṃ gatāyuṣamādiśet 75
kṛṣṇā tathā'nuliptā ca jihvā śūnyā ca yasya vai
karkaśā vā bhavedyasya so'cirādvijahātyasūn 76
kuṭilā sphuṭitā vā'pi śuṣkā vā yasya nāsikā
avasphūrjati bhagnā vā sa na jīvati mānavaḥ 77
saṃkṣipte viṣame stabdhe rūkṣe sāsre ca locane
syātāṃ pariśrute yasya sa gatāyurnaro dhruvam 78
keśā sīmantino yasya saṃkṣipte vinate bhruvau
luṭhanti cākṣipakṣmāṇi so'cirādyāti mṛtyave 79
nāharatyannamāsyasthaṃ na dhārayati yaḥ śiraḥ
ekāgradṛṣṭirmūḍhātmā sadyaḥ prāṇānvimuñcati 80
utthāpyamāno bahuśaḥ saṃmohaṃ yo'dhigacchati
balavāndurbalo vāpi taṃ pakvaṃ bhiṣagādiśet 81
nidrā nirantaraṃ yasya yo jāgartti ca sarvadā
muhyedvā vaktukāmaśca pratyākhyeyaḥ sa jānatā 82
uttarauṣṭhaṃ ca yo lihyādutkarāṃśca karotiḥ yaḥ
pretairvā bhāṣate sāyaṃ pretarūpaṃ tamādiśet 83
khebhyaśca romakūpebhyo yasya raktaṃ pravartate
puruṣasyāviṣārtasya sa sadyo jīvitaṃ tyajet 84
samyak cikitsyamānasya vikāro yo'bhivardhate
prakṣīṇabalamāṃsasya lakṣaṇaṃ tadgatāyuṣaḥ 85
bhūtāḥ pretāḥ piśācāśca rakṣāṃsi vividhāni ca
maraṇābhimukhaṃ jantumupasṛtya ca nityaśaḥ 86
tāni bheṣajavīryāṇi pratighnanti jighāṃsayā
tasmānmoghāḥ kriyāḥ sarvā bhavantyeva gatāyuṣaḥ 87
sarve dra vyamapekṣante rogiprabhṛtayo yataḥ
vinā vittaṃ na bhaiṣajyaṃ cikitsā'ṅga tato dhanam 88
snigdho'jugupsurbalavānyukto vyādhitarakṣaṇe
vaidyavākyakṛdaśrānto yujyate paricārakaḥ 89
vaidyo vyādhiṃ haredyena taddra vyaṃ proktamauṣadham
tadyādṛśamavaśyaṃ syādro gaghnaṃ tādṛśaṃ bruve 90
praśastadeśe sañjātaṃ praśaste'hani coddhṛtam
alpamātraṃ bahuguṇaṃ gandhavarṇarasānvitam 91
doṣaghnamaglānikaramadhikaṃ na vikāri yat
samīkṣya kāle dattaṃ ca bheṣajaṃ syād guṇāvaham 92
āgneyā vindhyaśailādyā saumyo himagiriḥ smṛtaḥ
atastadauṣadhāni syuranurūpāṇi hetubhiḥ 93
anyeṣvapi prarohanti vaneṣūpavaneṣu ca
gṛhṇīyāttāni sumanāḥ śuciḥ prātaḥ suvāsare 94
ādityasammukho maunī namaskṛtya śivaṃ hṛdi
sādhāraṇadharādra vyaṃ gṛhṇīyāduttarāśritam 95
valmīkakutsitānūpaśmaśānoṣaramārgajāḥ
jantuvahnihimavyāptā nauṣadhyaḥ kāryasādhikāḥ 96
śaradyakhilakāryārthaṃ grāhyaṃsarasamauṣadham
virekavamanārthaṃ tu vasantānte samāharet 97
atisthūlajaṭā yāḥ syustāsāṃ grāhyāstvaco dhruvam
gṛhṇīyātsūkṣmamūlāni sakalānyapi buddhimān 98
mahānti yeṣāṃ mūlāni kāṣṭhagarbhāṇi sarvataḥ
teṣāṃ tu valkalaṃ grāhyaṃ hrasvamūlāni sarvaśaḥ 99
nyagrodhādestvaco grāhyā sāraḥsyādvījakāditaḥ
tālīsādeśca patrāṇi phalaṃ syāt triphalāditaḥ 100
kvacinmūlaṃ kvacitkandaḥ kvacitpatraṃ kvacitphalam
kvacitpuṣpaṃ kvacitsarvaṃ kvacitsāraḥ kvacittvacaḥ 101
citrakaṃ sūraṇaṃ nimbo vāsā ca triphalā kramāt
dhātakī kaṇṭakārī ca khadiraḥ kṣīrapādapaḥ 102
kvacinnimbasya gṛhṇīyātpatrābhāve tvacāmapi
bālaṃ phalaṃ tu bilvasya pakvamāragvadhasya ca 103
aṅge'nukte jaṭā grāhyā bhāge'nukte'khilaṃ samam
pātre'nukte mṛdaḥ pātraṃ kāle'nukte tvaharmukham 104
navānyeva hi yojyāni dra vyāṇyakhilakarmasu
vinā viḍaṅgakṛṣṇābhyāṃ guḍadhānyājyamākṣikaiḥ 105
purāṇantu praśastaṃ syāttāmbūlaṃ kāñjikaṃ tathā
śuṣkaṃ navīnadra vyaṃ tu yojyaṃ sakalakarmasu 106
ārdrāntu dviguṇaṃ yuñjyādeṣa sarvatra niścayaḥ
guḍūcī kuṭajo vāsā kūṣmāṇḍaśca śatāvarī 107
aśvagandhā sahacaraḥ śatapuṣpā prasāriṇī
prayoktavyāḥ sadaivārdrādviguṇaṃ naiva kārayet 108
vāsānimbapaṭolaketakabalākūṣmāṇḍakendīvarī
varṣābhūkuṭajāśca kandasahitā sā pūtigandhā'mṛtā
endrī nāgabalā kuruṇṭakapuracchatrā'mṛtāḥ sarvadā
sārdrā eva tu na kvacid dviguṇitāḥ kāryeṣu yojyā budhaiḥ 109
ghṛtaṃ tailaṃ ca pānīyaṃ kaṣāyaṃ vyañjanādikam
paktvā śītīkṛtaṃ coṣṇaṃ tatsarvaṃ syādviṣopamam 110
sūkṣmāsthimāṃsalā pathyā sarvakarmaṇi pūjitā
kṣiptā'mbhasi nimajjedyāḥ bhallātakyastathottamāḥ 111
varāhamūrddhavatkando vārāhīkandasaṃjñakaḥ
sauvarcalaṃ tu kācābhaṃ saindhavaṃ sphaṭikaprabham 112
suvarṇacchavikaṃ jñeyaṃ svarṇamākṣikamuttamam
oḍrapuṣpapratīkāśā manohvā cottamā matā 113
śreṣṭhaṃ śilājatu jñeyaṃ prakṣiptaṃ na viśīryate
toyapūrṇe kāṃsyapātre pratānena vivardhate 114
karpūrastuvaraḥ snigdhaḥ elā sūkṣphalā varā
śvetacandanamatyantaṃ sugandhi gurupūjitam 115
raktacandanamatyantaṃ lohitaṃ pravaraṃ matam
kākatuṇḍanibhaḥ snigdho guruḥ śreṣṭho gururmataḥ 116
sugandhi laghu rūkṣaṃ ca suradāru varaṃ matam
saralaṃ snigdhamatyarthaṃ sugandhi ca guṇāvaham 117
atipītā praśastā tu jñeyā dāruniśā budhaiḥ
jātīphalaṃ guru snigdhaṃ samaṃ śubhrāntaraṃ varam 118
mṛdvīkā sottamā jñeyā yā syādgostanasannibhā
karamardaphalākārā madhyamā sā prakīrttitā 119
khaṇḍaṃ tu vimalaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ candra kāntasamaprabham
gavyājyasadṛśaṃ rucyaṃ gandhaṃ madhu varaṃ matam 120
śālīnāṃ lohitaḥ śāli ṣaṣṭikeṣu ca ṣaṣṭikaḥ
śūkadhānyeṣvapi yavo godhūmaḥ pravaro mataḥ 121
śimbīdhānye varo mudgo masūraścāḍhakī tathā
raseṣu madhuraḥ śreṣṭho lavaṇeṣu ca saindhavaḥ 122
dāḍimāmalakaṃ drā kṣā kharjūraṃ ca parūṣakam
rājādanaṃ mātuluṃgaṃ phalavargeṣu śasyate 123
patraśākeṣu vāstūkaṃ jīvantī potikā varā
paṭolaṃ phalaśākeṣu kandaśākeṣu sūraṇam 124
eṇaḥ kuraṅgo hariṇo jāṅgaleṣu praśasyate
pakṣiṇāṃ tittirirlāvo varo matsyeṣu rohitaḥ 125
hariṇastāmravarṇaḥ syādeṇaḥ kṛṣṇatayā mataḥ
kuraṅgastāmra uddiṣṭo hariṇākṛtiko mahān 126
jaleṣu divyaṃ dugdheṣu gavyamājyeṣu gobhavam
taileṣu tilajaṃ tailamaikṣaveṣu sitā hitā 127
śimvīṣu māṣāngrīṣmartau lavaṇeṣvauṣaraṃ tyajet
phaleṣu lakucaṃ śāke sārṣapaṃ na hitaṃ matam 128
gomāṃsaṃ grāmyamāṃseṣu na hitaṃ mahiṣīvasā
meṣīpayaḥ kusumbhasya tailaṃ tyājyaṃ ca phāṇitam 129
matsyamānūpamāṃsaṃ ca dugdhayuktaṃ vivarjayet
kapotaṃ sarṣapasnehabharjitaṃ parivarjayet 130
matsyānikṣorvikāreṇa tathā kṣaudre ṇa varjayet
saktūnmāṃsapayoyuktānuṣṇairdadhi vivarjayet 131
uṣṇairnabho'mbunā kṣaudraṃ pāyasaṃ kṛsarānvitam
rambhāphalaṃ tyajettakradadhibilvaphalānvitam 132
daśāhamuṣitaṃ sarpiḥ kāṃsye madhughṛtaṃ samam
kṛtānnaṃ ca kaṣāyaṃ ca punaruṣṇīkṛtaṃ tyajet 133
ekatra bahumāṃsāni virudhyante parasparam
madhu sarpirvasā tailaṃ pānīyaṃ vā payastathā 134
lavaṇaṃ saindhavaṃ proktaṃcandanaṃ raktacandanam
cūrṇalehāsavasnehāḥ sādhyā dhavalacandanaiḥ
kaṣāyalepayoḥ prāyo yujyate raktacandanam 135
antaḥ sammārjane jñeyā hyajamodā yavānikā
bahiḥ sammārjane saiva vijñātavyā'jamodikā 136
payaḥ sarpiḥ prayogeṣu gavyameva hi gṛhyate
śakṛdra so gomayako mūtraṃ gomūtramucyate 137
citrakābhāvatodantī kṣāraḥ śikharijo'thavā
abhāve dhanvayāsasya prekṣepyātu durālabhā 138
tagarasyāpyabhāve tu kuṣṭhaṃ dadyādbhiṣagvaraḥ
mūrvā'bhāve tvaco grāhyā jiṅginīprabhavā budhaiḥ 139
ahiṃsrayā abhāve tu mānakandaḥ prakīrttitaḥ
lakṣmaṇāyā abhāve tu nīlakaṇṭhaśikhā matā 140
vakulābhāvato deyaṃ kahlārotpalapaṅkajam
nīlotpalasyābhāve tu kumudaṃ deyamiṣyate 141
jātīpuṣpaṃ na yatrāsti lavaṃgaṃ tatra dīyate
arkaparṇādipayaso hyabhāve tadra so mataḥ 142
pauṣkarā bhāvataḥ kuṣṭhaṃ tathā lāṅgalyabhāvataḥ
sthauṇeyakasyābhāve tu miṣagbhirdīyate gadaḥ 143
cavikāgajapippalyau piplīmūlavatsmṛtau 144
abhāve somarājyāstu prapunnāḍaphalaṃ matam
yadi na syāddāruniśā tadā deyā niśā budhaiḥ 145
rasāñjanasyābhāve tu dārvīkvāthaḥ prayujyate
saurāṣṭryabhāvato deyā sphaṭikā tadguṇā janaiḥ 146
tālīsapatrakābhāve svarṇatālī praśasyate
bhārṅgyabhāve tu tālīsaṃ kaṇṭakārījaṭā'thavā 147
rucakābhāvato dadyāllavaṇaṃ pāṃśupūrvakam
abhāve madhuyaṣṭyāstu dhātakīṃ ca prayojayet 148
amlavetasakābhāve cukraṃ dātavyamiṣyate
drā kṣā yadi na labhyeta pradeyaṃ kāśmarīphalam 149
tayorabhāve kusumaṃ madhūkasya mataṃ budhaiḥ
lavaṅgakusumaṃ deyaṃ nakhasyābhāvataḥ punaḥ 150
kastūryabhāve kaṅkolaṃ kṣepaṇīyaṃ vidurbudhāḥ
kaṅkolasyāpyabhāve tu jātīpuṣpaṃ pradīyate 151
sugandhimustakaṃ deyaṃ karpūrābhāvato budhaiḥ
karpūrābhāvato deyaṃ granthiparṇaṃ viśeṣataḥ 152
kuṅkumābhāvato dadyātkusumbhakusumaṃ navam
śrīkhaṇḍacandanābhāve karpūraṃ deyamiṣyate 153
abhāve tvetayorvaidyaḥ prakṣipedra ktacandanam
raktacandanakābhāve navośīraṃ vidurbudhāḥ 154
mustā cātiviṣābhāve śivā'bhāve śivā matā
abhāve nāgapuṣpasya padmakeśaramiṣyate 155
medājīvakakākolīṛddhidvandve'pi vā'sati
darīvidāryaśvagandhāvārāhīśca kramāt kṣipet 156
vārāhyāśca tathā'bhāve carmakārāluko mataḥ
vārāhīkandasaṃjñastu paścime gṛṣṭisaṃjñakaḥ 157
vārīhīkanda evānyaścarmakārāluko mataḥ
anūpasambhave deśe varāha iva lomavān 158
bhallātakāsahatve tu raktacandanamiṣyate
bhallātābhāvataścitraṃ nalaścekṣorabhāvataḥ 159
suvarṇābhāvataḥ svarṇamākṣikaṃ prakṣiped budhaḥ
śvetaṃ tu mākṣikaṃ jñeyaṃ budhai rajatavad dhruvam 160
mākṣikasyāpyabhāve tu pradadyātsvarṇagairikam
suvarṇamatha vā raupyaṃ mṛtaṃ yatra na labhyate 161
tatra kāntena karmāṇi bhiṣakkuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ
kāntābhāve tīkṣṇalohaṃ yojayedvaidyasattamaḥ 162
abhāve mauktikasyāpi muktāśuktiṃ prayojayet
madhu yatra na labhyeta tatra jīrṇaguḍo mataḥ 163
matsyaṇḍyabhāvato dadyurbhiṣajaḥ sitaśarkarām
asambhave sitāyāstu budhaiḥ khaṇḍaṃ prayujyate 164
kṣīrābhāve raso maudgo māsūro vā pradīyate
atra proktāni vastūni yāni teṣu ca teṣu ca 165
yojyamekatarābhāve paraṃ vaidyena jānatā
rasavīryavipākādyaiḥ samaṃ dra vyaṃ vicintya ca 166
yuñjyāttadvidhamanyacca dra vyāṇāṃ tu rasādivit
yoge yadapradhānaṃ syāttasya pratinidhirmataḥ 167
yattu pradhānaṃ tasyāpi sadṛśaṃ naiva gṛhyate
vyādherayuktaṃ yaddra vyaṃ gaṇoktamapi tattyajet
anuktamapi yuktaṃ yadyojayettadra sādivit 168
dra vye raso guṇo vīryaṃ vipākaḥ śaktireva ca
padārthāḥ pañca tiṣṭhanti svaṃ svaṃ kurvanti karma ca 169
rasāḥ svādvamlalavaṇatiktoṣaṇakaṣāyakāḥ
ṣaḍ dra vyamāśritāste ca yathāpūrvaṃ balāvahāḥ 170
tatrādyā mārutaṃ ghnanti trayastiktādayaḥ kapham
kaṣāyatiktamadhurāḥ pittamanye tu kurvate 171
ye rasā vātaśamanā bhavanti yadi teṣu vai
raukṣyalāghavaśaityāni na te hanyuḥ samīraṇam 172
ye rasā pittaśamanā bhavanti yadi teṣu vai
tīkṣṇoṣṇalaghutāścaiva naite tatkarmakāriṇaḥ 173
ye rasāḥ śleṣmaśamanā bhavanti yadi teṣu vai
snehagauravaśaityāni na te hanyuḥ kaphaṃ tadā 174
madhuro hi rasaḥ śīto dhātustanyabalapradaḥ
cakṣuṣyo vātapittaghnaḥ kuryātsthaulyamalakṛmīn 175
viṣaghnaḥ picchilaścāpi snigdhaḥ prītyāyuṣorhitaḥ
bālabṛddhakṣatakṣīṇavarṇakeśendri yaujasām 176
praśasto bṛṃhaṇaḥ kaṇṭhyo guruḥ sandhānakṛnmataḥ 177
so'tiyukto jvaraśvāsagalagaṇḍārbudakṛmīn
sthaulyāgnimāndyamehāṃśca kuryānmedaḥkaphāmayān 178
raso'mla pācano rucyaḥ pittaśleṣmāsrado laghuḥ
lekhitoṣṇo bahiḥ śītaḥ kledanaḥ pavanāpahaḥ 179
snigdhastīkṣṇaḥ saraḥ śukravibandhānāhadṛṣṭihā
harṣaṇo romadantānāmakṣibhrūvinikocanaḥ 180
so'tiyukto bhramaṃ kuryāttṛḍdāhatimirajvarān
kaṇḍūpāṇḍutvavīsarpaśothavisphoṭakuṣṭhakṛt 181
lavaṇaḥ śodhano rucyaḥ pācanaḥ kaphapittadaḥ
puṃstvavātaharaḥ kāyaśaithilyamṛdutākaraḥ
balaghna āsyajaladaḥ kapolagaladāhakṛt 182
so'ti yukto 'kṣipākāsrapitta koṭhakṣatādikṛt
valīpalitakhālityakuṣṭhavīsarpatṛṭpradaḥ 183
kaṭuruṣṇaśca tīkṣṇaśca viśado vātapittakṛt
śleṣmahṛllaghurāgneyaḥ kṛmikaṇḍūviṣāpahaḥ 184
śukrastanyaharaścāpi medaḥsthaulyāpakarṣaṇaḥ
aśrudo nāsikā'syākṣijihvā'grodvejako mataḥ 185
dīpanaḥ pācano rucyo nāsikāśoṣaṇo bhṛśam
kledamedovasāmajjaśakṛnmūtropaśoṣaṇaḥ 186
srotaḥ prakāśako rūkṣaḥ medhyo varcovibandhakṛt 187
so'tiyukto bhrāntidāhamukhatālvoṣṭhaśoṣakṛt
kaṇṭhādipīḍāmūrcchā'ntardāhado balakāntihṛt 188
tiktaḥ śītastṛṣāmūrcchājvarapittakaphāñjayet
kṛmikuṣṭhaviṣotkledadāharaktagadāpahaḥ 189
rucyaḥ svayamarociṣṇuḥ kaṇṭhastanyaviśodhanaḥ
vātalo'gnikaro nāsāśoṣaṇo rūkṣaṇo laghuḥ 190
so'tiyuktaḥ śiraḥśūlamanyāstambhaśramārttikṛt
kampamūrcchātṛṣākārī balaśukrakṣayapradaḥ 191
kaṣāyo ropaṇo grāhī stambhanaḥ śodhanastathā
lekhanaḥ pīḍanaḥ saumyaḥ śoṣaṇo vātakopanaḥ 192
kaphaśoṇitapittaghno rūkṣaḥ śīto laghurmataḥ
tvakprasādhana āmasya stambhano viśado mataḥ
jihvāyāṃ jāḍyakṛtkaṇṭhasrotasāṃ ca vibandhakṛt 193
so'tiyukto grahādhmānahṛtpīḍākṣepaṇādikṛt 194
madhuraṃ śleṣmalaṃprāyo jīrṇaśāliyavādṛte
mudgādgodhūmataḥ kṣaudrā tsitāyā jāṅgalāmiṣāt 195
amlaṃ pittakaraṃ prāyo vinā dhātrīṃ ca dāḍimīm
lavaṇaṃ prāyaśo dveṣi netrayoḥ saindhavaṃ vinā 196
prāyaḥ kaṭu tathā tiktamavṛṣyaṃ vātakopanam
śuṇṭhī kṛṣṇārasonāni paṭolamamṛtāṃ vinā 197
pippalīnāgaraṃ vṛṣyaṃ kaṭu cāvṛṣyamucyate
prāyaśaḥ stambhanaṃ proktaṃ kaṣāyamabhayāṃ vinā 198
sāmānyenātra nirdiṣṭā guṇāḥ ṣaḍrasasambhavāḥ
rasānāṃ yogatastu syādanya eva guṇodayaḥ 199
saṃyogādviṣatāṃ yāti samamājyena mākṣiṃkam
amṛtatvaṃ viṣaṃ yāti sarpadaṣṭasya vai yathā 200
laghurgurustathā snigdho rūkṣastīkṣṇa iti kramāt
nabhobhūvārivātānāṃ vahnerete guṇāḥsmṛtāḥ 201
laghu pathyaṃ paraṃ proktaṃ kaphaghnaṃ śīghrapāki ca 202
garu vātaharaṃ puṣṭiśleṣmakṛccirapāki ca
snigdhaṃ vātaharaṃ śleṣmakāri vṛṣyaṃ balāvaham
rūkṣaṃ samīraṇakaraṃ paraṃ kaphaharaṃ matam 203
tīkṣṇaṃ pittakaraṃ prāyo lekhanaṃ kaphavātahṛt
suśrute tu guṇā ete viṃśatistānbruve śṛṇu 204
gururlaghuḥ snigdharūkṣau tīkṣṇaḥ ślakṣṇaḥ sthiraḥ saraḥ
picchilo viśadaḥ śīta uṣṇaśca mṛdukarkaśau
sthūlaḥ sūkṣmo dra vaḥ śuṣka āśurmandaḥ smṛtā guṇāḥ 205
ślakṣṇaḥ snehaṃ vinā'pi syātkaṭhino'pi hi cikkaṇaḥ 206
sthiro vātamalastambhī sarasteṣāṃ pravartakaḥ
picchilastantulo balyaḥ sandhānaḥ śleṣmalo guruḥ 207
kledacchedakaraḥ khyāto viśado vraṇaropaṇaḥ
śītastu hlādanaḥstambhī mūrcchātṛṭasvedadāhanut
uṣṇo bhavati śītasya viparītaśca pācanaḥ 208
sthūlaḥ sthaulyakaro dehe srotasāmavarodhakṛt 209
dehasya sūkṣmacchidre ṣu viśedyatsūkṣmamucyate
dra vaḥ kledakaro vyāpī śuṣkastadviparītakaḥ 210
āśurāśukaro dehe dhāvatyambhasi tailavat
mandaḥ sakalakāryeṣu śithilo'lpo'pi kathyate 211
pacennāmaṃ vahnikṛdyaddīpanaṃ tadyathā misiḥ 212
pacatyāmaṃ na vahniṃ ca kuryādyattaddhi pācanam
nāgakeśaravadvidyāccitrodīpanapācanaḥ 213
naśodhayati yaddoṣānsamānnodīrayatyapi
samīkaroti viṣamāñchamanaṃ tadyathā'mṛtā 214
kṛtvā pākaṃ malānāṃ ca bhitvā bandhamadho nayet
taccānulomanaṃ jñeyaṃ yathā proktā harītakī 215
paktavyaṃ yadapaktvaiva śliṣṭaṃ koṣṭhe malādikam
nayatyadhaḥ sraṃsanaṃ tadyathā syātkṛtamālakam 216
malādikamabaddhaṃ yadbaddhaṃ vā piṇḍitaṃ malaiḥ
bhitvā'dha pātayati yadbhedanaṃ kaṭukī yathā 217
vipakvaṃ yadapakvaṃ vā malādi dra vatāṃ nayet
recayatyapi tajjñeyaṃ recanaṃ trivṛtā yathā 218
apakvaṃ pittaśleṣmānnaṃ balādūrdhvaṃ nayettu yat
vamanaṃ taddhi vijñeyaṃ madanasya phalaṃ yathā 219
sthānādvahirnayedūrdhvamadho vā malasañcayam
dehasaṃśodhanaṃ tatsyād devadālīphalaṃ yathā 220
dīpanaṃ pācanaṃ yatsyāduṣṇatvāddra vaśoṣakam
grāhi tacca yathā śuṇṭhī jīrakaṃ gajapippalī 221
raukṣyācchaityātkaṣāyatvāllaghupākācca yadbhavet
vātakṛtstambhanaṃ tatsyādyathā vatsakaṭuṇṭukau 222
śliṣṭānkaphādikāndoṣānunmūlayati yadvalāt
chedanaṃ tadyathā kṣārā maricāni śilājatu 223
dhātūnmalān vā dehasya viśodhyollekhayecca yat
lekhanaṃ tadyathā kṣaudraṃ nīramuṣṇaṃ vacā yavāḥ 224
yasmād dra vyādbhavetstrīṣu harṣo vājīkaraṃ hi tat
yathā'śvagandhāmusalī śarkarā ca śatāvarī 225
yasmācchukrasya vṛddhiḥ syācchukralaṃ hi taducyate
yathā nāgabalā'dyā syurbījaṃ ca kapikacchujam 226
dugdhaṃ māṣāśca bhallātaphalamajjāmalāni ca
etāni janakāni syū recakāni ca retasaḥ 227
pravartinī strī śukrasya recanaṃ bṛhatīphalam
jātīphalaṃ stambhakaṃ syātkāliṅgaṃ kṣayakāri ca 228
rasāyanantu tajjñeyaṃ yajjarāvyādhināśanam
yathā harītakī dantī gugguluśca śilājatu 229
pūrvaṃ vyāpyākhilaṃ kāyaṃ tataḥ pākañca gacchati
vyavāyi tad yathā bhaṅgā phenañcāhi samudbhavam 230
sandhibandhāṃstu śithilānyatkaroti vikāśi tat
viśoṣyaujaśca dhātubhyo yathā kramukakodra vau 231
buddhiṃ lumpati yad dra vyaṃ madakāri taducyate
tamoguṇapradhānañca yathā madyaṃ surā'dikam 232
vyavāyi ca vikāśi syācchleṣmacchedi madāvaham
āgneyaṃ jīvitaharaṃ yogavāhi smṛtaṃ viṣam 233
nijavīryyeṇa yad dra vyaṃ srotobhyo doṣasañcayam
nirasyati pramāthi syāttadyathā maricaṃ vacā 234
paicchilyādgauravād dra vyaṃ ruddhvā rasavahāḥ śirāḥ
dhatte yadgauravaṃ tatsyādabhiṣyandi yathā dadhi 235
vidāhi dra vyamudgāramamlaṃ kuryāttathā tṛṣām
hṛdi dāhaṃ ca janayetpākaṃ gacchati taccirāt 236
gṛhṇāti yogavāhi dra vyaṃ saṃsargivastuguṇān
pacyamānaṃ yathaitanmadhujalatailājyasūtalohādiḥ 237
uṣṇaśītaguṇotkarṣād budhaivīryaṃ dvidhā smṛtam
yatsarvamagniṣomīyaṃ dṛśyate bhuvanatrayam 238
uṣṇaṃ vātakaphau hanyāt pittaṃ tu tanute jarām
śītaṃ vātakaphātaṅkānkurute pittahṛtparam 239
tatroṣṇaṃ bhramatṛḍglānisvedadāhāśupākatām
śamañca vātakaphayo karoti śiśiraṃ punaḥ
hlādanaṃ jīvanaṃ stambhaṃ prasādaṃ raktapittayoḥ 240
jāṭhareṇāgninā yogādyadudeti rasāntaram
rasānāṃ pariṇāmānte sa vipāka iti smṛtaḥ 241
miṣṭaḥ paṭuśca madhuramamlo'mla pacyate rasaḥ
kaṭutiktakaṣāyāṇāṃ pākaḥ syātprāyaśaḥ kaṭuḥ 242
śleṣmakṛnmadhuraḥ pāko vātapittaharo mataḥ
amlastu kurute pittaṃ vātaśleṣmagadāpahaḥ 243
kaṭuḥ karoti pavanaṃ kaphaṃ pittañca nāśayet
viśeṣa evaṃ rasato vipākānāṃ nidarśitaḥ 244
rasādisāmye yatkarma viśiṣṭaṃ tatprabhāvajam
dantī rasādyaistulyāpi citrakasya virecanī 245
madhūkasya ca mṛdvīkā ghṛtaṃ kṣīrasya dīpanam
prabhāvastu yathā dhātrī lakucasya rasādibhiḥ 246
samā'pi kurute doṣatritayasya vināśanam 247
kvacittukevalaṃ dra vyaṃ karma kuryātprabhāvataḥ
jvaraṃ hanti śirobaddhā sahadevījaṭā yathā 248
viruddhaguṇasaṃyoge bhūyasā'lpa hi jīyate
rasaṃ vipākastau vīryaṃ prabhāvastānvyapohati 249
iti miśraprakaraṇe miśravargaḥ prathamaḥ samāptaḥ 1
bhāvaprakāśapūrvakhaṇḍaṃ
<1.2>
<1.2.1>
bhāva prakāśaḥ
pūrvakhaṇḍe dvitīyo bhāgaḥ
tatra saptamaṃ paribhāṣādiprakaraṇam 7
na mānena vinā yuktirdra vyāṇāṃ jāyate kvacit
ataḥ prayogakāryārthaṃ mānamatrocyate mayā 1
carakasya mataṃ vaidyairādyairyasmānmataṃ tataḥ
vihāya sarvamānāni māgadhaṃ mānamucyate 2
trasareṇurbudhaiḥ proktastriśatā paramāṇubhiḥ
trasareṇustu paryāyanāmnā vaṃśī nigadyate 3
jālāntaragataiḥ sūryakarairvaṃśī vilokyate
ṣaḍvaṃśībhirmarīciḥ syāttābhiḥ ṣaḍbhiśca rājikā 4
tisṛbhī rājikābhiścna sarṣapaḥ procyate budhaiḥ
yavo'ṣṭasarṣapaiḥ prokto guñjā syāttaccatuṣṭayam 5
ṣaḍbhistu raktikābhiḥ syānmāṣako hemadhānakau
māṣaiścaturbhiḥ śāṇaḥ syāddharaṇaḥ sa nigadyate 6
ṭaṅkaḥ sa eva kathitastaddvayaṃ kola ucyate
kṣudra ko vaṭakaścaiva dra ṅkṣaṇaḥ sa nigadyate 7
koladvayantuṃ karṣaḥ syātsa proktaḥ pāṇimānikā
akṣaḥ picuḥ pāṇitalaṃ kiñcitpāṇiśca tindukam 8
viḍālapadakaṃ caiva tathā ṣoḍaśikā matā
karamadhyo haṃsapadaṃ suvarṇaṃ kavalagrahaḥ 9
udumbarañca paryāyaiḥ karṣameva nigadyate
syātkarṣābhyāmarddhapalaṃ śuktiraṣṭamikā tathā 10
śuktibhyāñca palaṃ jñeyaṃ muṣṭirāmraṃ caturthikā
prakuñcaḥ ṣoḍaśī bilvaṃ palamevātra kīttaryate 11
palābhyāṃ prasṛtirjñeyā prasṛtañca nigadyate
prasṛtibhyāmañjali syātkuḍavo'ddhaśarāvakaḥ 12
aṣṭamānañca sa jñeyaḥ kuḍavābhyāñca mānikā
śarāvo'ṣṭapalaṃ tadvajjñeyamatra vicakṣaṇaiḥ 13
śarāvābhyāṃ bhavetprasthaścatuḥ prasthastathā'ḍhakaḥ
bhājanaṃ kāṃsyapātraṃ ca catuḥṣaṣṭipalaśca saḥ 14
caturbhirāḍhakaidro rṇaḥ! kalaśo nalvaṇo'maṇaḥ
unmānañcaghaṭo rāśidro rṇa!paryāyasaṃjñitaḥ 15
dro ṇābhyāṃ śūrpakumbhau ca catuḥṣaṣṭiśarāvakaḥ
śūrpābhyāñca bhaved dro ṇī vāho goṇī ca sā smṛtā 16
dro ṇīcatuṣṭayaṃ khārī kathitā sūkṣmabuddhibhiḥ
catuḥsahasrapalikā ṣaṇṇavatyadhikā ca sā 17
palānāṃ dvisahasrañca bhāra ekaḥ prakīrttitaḥ
tulāpalaśataṃ jñeyaṃ sarvatraivaiṣa niścayaḥ 18
māṣaṭaṅkākṣabilvāni kuḍavaprasthamāḍhakam
rāśirgoṇī khāriketi yathottaracaturguṇam 19
guñjā'dimānamārabhya yāvatsyātkuḍavasthitiḥ
dra vārdra śuṣkadra vyāṇāṃ tāvanmānaṃ samaṃ matam 20
prasthādimānamārabhya dviguṇaṃ tad dra vārdra yoḥ
mānaṃ tathā tulāyāstu dviguṇaṃ na kvacitsmṛtam 21
mṛdvṛkṣaveṇulohāderbhāṇḍaṃ yaccaturaṅgulam
vistīrṇañca tathoccañca tanmānaṃ kuḍavaṃ vadet 22
iti māgadhamānaṃ samāptam
yato mandāgnayo hrasvā hīnasattvā narāḥ kalau
atastu mātrā tadyogyā procyate sujñasaṃmatā 23
yavo dvādaśabhirgaurasarṣapaiḥ procyate budhaiḥ
yavadvayena guñjā syāttriguñjo valla ucyate 24
māṣo guñjābhiraṣṭābhiḥ saptabhirvā bhavetkvacit
caturbhirmāṣakaiḥ śāṇaḥ sa niṣkaṣṭaṅka eva ca 25
gadyāṇo māṣakaiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ karṣaḥ syāddaśamāṣakaiḥ
catuṣkarṣaiḥ palaṃ proktaṃ daśaśāṇamitaṃ budhaiḥ 26
catuṣpalaiśca kuḍavaḥ prasthādyāḥ pūrvavanmatāḥ
sthitirnāstyeva mātrāyāḥ kālamagniṃ vayo balam 27
prakṛtiṃ doṣadeśau ca dṛṣṭvā mātrāṃ prakalpayet
nālpaṃ hantyauṣadhaṃ vyādhiṃ yathā'mbho'lpa mahānalaḥ
atimātraṃ ca doṣāya yathā śasye bahūdakam 28
iti kāliṅgamānaṃ samāptam
iti śrīmiśralaṭakana tanaya śrīmiśrabhāva viracite bhāvaprakāśe paribhāṣādiprakaraṇe
prathamaṃ mānaparibhāṣākhyaṃ prakaraṇaṃ samāptam 1
<1.2.2>
atha dvitīyaṃ bheṣajavidhānaprakaraṇam 2
svarasaśca tathā kalkaḥ kvāthaśca himaphāṇṭakau
jñeyāḥ kaṣāyāḥ pañcaite laghavaḥ syuryathottaram 1
ahatāttatkṣaṇākṛṣṭād dra vyātkṣuṇṇātsamudbhavet
vastraniṣpīḍito yaśca svaraso rasa ucyate 2
kuḍavaṃ cūrṇitaṃ dra vyaṃ kṣiptañca dviguṇe jale
ahorātraṃ sthitaṃ tasmādbhavedvā rasa uttamaḥ 3
ādāya śuṣkadra vyaṃ vā svarasānāmasaṃbhave
jale'ṣṭaguṇite sādhyaṃ pādaśiṣṭaṃ ca gṛhyate 4
svarasya gurutvācca palamarddhaṃ prayojayet
niśoṣitaṃ cāgnisiddhaṃ palamātraṃ rasaṃ pibet 5
sitāmadhuguḍakṣārāñjīrakaṃ lavaṇaṃ tathā
ghṛtaṃ tailañca cūrṇādīn kolamātrān rase kṣipet 6
kaṇḍitaṃ taṇḍulapalaṃ jale'ṣṭaguṇite kṣipet
bhāvayitvā jalaṃ grāhyaṃ deyaṃ sarvatra karmasu 7
kṣuṇṇaṃ dra vyapalaṃ samyak ṣaḍbhirnīrapalaiḥ plutam
niśoṣitaṃ himaḥ sa syāttathā śītakaṣāyakaḥ
tasya mānaṃ mataṃ pāne paladvayamitaṃ budhaiḥ 8
jale catuṣpale śīte kṣuṇṇaṃ dra vyapalaṃ kṣipet
mṛtpātre manthayet samyak tasmācca dvipalaṃ pibet 9
kṣuṇṇe dra vyapale samyagjalamuṣṇaṃ vinikṣipet
mṛtpātre kuḍavonmānaṃ tatastu srāvayetpaṭāt 10
sa syāccūrṇadra vaḥ phāṇṭastanmānaṃ dvipalonmitam
kṣaudraṃ sitāguḍādīṃstu karṣamātrānvinikṣipet 11
dra vyamādra raṃ! śilāpiṣṭaṃ śuṣkaṃ vā sajalaṃ bhavet
tadeva kalko vijñeyastanmānaṃ karṣasammitam 12
kalke madhughṛtaṃ tailaṃ deyaṃ dbiguṇamātrayā
sitāṃ guḍaṃ samaṃ dadyād dra vo deyaścaturguṇaḥ 13
atyantaśuṣkaṃ yad dra vyaṃ supiṣṭaṃ vastragālitam
tatsyāccūrṇaṃ rajaḥ kṣodastanmātrā karṣasammitā 14
cūrṇe guḍaḥ samo deyaḥ śarkarā dviguṇā matā
cūrṇeṣu bharjitaṃ hiṅgu deyaṃ notkledakṛdbhavet 15
liheccūrṇaṃ dra vaiḥ sarvairghṛtādyairdviguṇonmitaiḥ
pibeccaturguṇairevaṃ cūrṇamāloḍitaṃ dra vaiḥ 16
cūrṇāvalehaguṭikākalkānāmanupānakam
pittavātakaphātaṅke tridvyekapalamāharet 17
yathā tailaṃ jale kṣiptaṃ kṣaṇenaiva visarpati
anupānabalādaṅge tathā sarpati bheṣajam 18
dra veṇa yāvatā samyak cūrṇaṃ sarvaṃ plutaṃ bhavet
bhāvanāyāḥ pramāṇaṃ tu cūrṇe proktaṃ bhiṣagvaraiḥ 19
puṭapākasya kalkasya svaraso gṛhyate yataḥ
atastu puṭapākānāṃ yuktiratrocyate mayā 20
puṭapākasya pāko'ya lepasyāṅgāravarṇatā
lepaśca dvyaṅgulaṃ sthūlaṃ kuryād dvyaṅgulamātrakam 21
kāśmarīvaṭajambvādipatrairveṣṭanamuttamam 22
palamātro raso grāhyaḥ karṣamānaṃ madhu kṣipet
kalkacūrṇadra vādyāstu deyāḥ kolamitā budhaiḥ 23
aṣṭamenāṃśaśeṣeṇa caturthenārddhakena vā
athavā kvathanenaiva siddhamuṣṇodakaṃ bhavet 24
śleṣmāmavātamedoghnaṃ vastiśodhanadīpanam
kāsaśvāsajvarān hanti pītamuṣṇodakaṃ niśi 25
kṣīramaṣṭaguṇaṃ dra vyātkṣīrānnīraṃ caturguṇam
kṣīrāvaśeṣaṃ tatpītaṃ śūlamāmodbhavaṃ jayet 26
pānīyaṃ ṣoḍaśaguṇaṃ kṣuṇṇe dra vyapale kṣipet
mṛtpātre kvāthayed grāhyamaṣṭamāṃśāvaśeṣitam 27
karṣādau tu palaṃ yāvad dadyātṣoḍaśikaṃ jalam
tatastu kuḍavaṃ yāvattoyamaṣṭaguṇaṃ bhavet
caturguṇamataścordhvaṃ yāvat prasthādikaṃ jalam 28
tajjalaṃ pāyayeddhīmānkoṣṇaṃ mṛdvagnisādhitam
śṛtaḥ kvāthaḥ kaṣāyaśca niryūhaḥ sa nigadyate 29
mātrottamā palena syāttribhirakṣaistu madhyamā
jaghanyā ca palārddhena snehakvāthauṣadheṣu ca 30
kvāthyadra vyapale vāri dviraṣṭaguṇamiṣyate
caturbhāgāvaśiṣṭantu peyaṃ jalacatuṣṭayam 31
dīptānalaṃ mahākāyaṃ pāyayedañjali jalam
anye tvarddhaṃ parityajya prasṛtiṃ tu cikitsakāḥ 32
kvāthatyāgamanicchantastvaṣṭabhāgāvaśeṣitam
pāramparyopadeśena vṛddhavaidyāḥ paladvayam 33
kvāthe kṣipetsitāmaṃśaiścaturthāṣṭamaṣoḍaśaiḥ
vātapittakaphātaṅke viparītaṃ laghu smṛtam 34
jīrakaṃ guggulaṃ kṣāraṃ lavaṇaṃ ca śilājatu
hiṅgu trikaṭukañcaiva kvāthe śāṇonmitaṃ kṣipet 35
kṣīraṃ ghṛtaṃ guḍaṃ tailaṃ mūtraṃ cānyad dra vaṃ tathā
kalkaṃ cūrṇādikaṃ kvāthe nikṣipet karṣasammitam 36
tatropaviśya viśrāntaḥ prasannavadanekṣaṇaḥ
auṣadhaṃ hemarajatamṛdbhājanaparisthitam 37
pibet prasannahṛdayaḥ pītvā pātramadhomukham
vidhāyācamya salilaṃ tāmbūlādyupayojayet 38
kvāthāderyatpunaḥ pākād ghanatvaṃ sā rasakriyā
so'valehaśca lehaśca tanmātrā syātpalonmitā 39
sitā caturguṇā kāryā cūrṇācca dviguṇo guḍaḥ
dra vaṃ caturguṇaṃ dadyāditi sarvatra niścayaḥ 40
supakve tantumattvaṃ syādavalehe'psu majjanam
sthiratvaṃ pīḍite mudrā gandhavarṇarasodbhavaḥ 41
dugdhamikṣurasaṃ yūṣaṃ pañcamūlakaṣāyakam
vāsākvāthaṃ yathāyogyamanupānaṃ praśasyate 42
vaṭakā atha kathyante tannāma guṭikā vaṭī
modako vaṭikā piṇḍī guḍo varttistathocyate 43
lehavatsādhyate vahnau guḍo vā śarkarā'thavā
guggullurvā kṣipettatra cūrṇaṃ tannirmitā vaṭī 44
kuryādavahnisiddhena kvacid guggulunā vaṭīm
dra veṇa madhunā vā'pi guṭikāṃ kārayed budhaḥ 45
sitā caturguṇā deyā vaṭīṣu dviguṇo guḍaḥ
cūrṇe cūrṇasamaḥ kāryo guggulurmadhu tatsamam 46
dra vaṃ tu dviguṇaṃ deyaṃ modakeṣu bhiṣagvaraiḥ 47
karṣapramāṇaṃ tanmātrā balaṃ dṛṣṭvā prayujyate 48
kalkāccaturguṇīkṛtya ghṛtaṃ vā tailameva ca
caturguṇadra ve sādhyaṃ tasya mātrā palonmitā 49
nikṣipya kvāthayettoyaṃ kvāthyadra vyāccaturguṇam
pādaśiṣṭaṃ gṛhītvā tu snehaṃ tenaiva sādhayet 50
caturguṇaṃ mṛdudra vye kaṭhine'ṣṭaguṇaṃ jalam
mṛdvādikvāthyasaṃghāte dadyādaṣṭaguṇaṃ payaḥ
atyanta kaṭhine dra vye nīraṃ ṣoḍaśikaṃ matam 51
prasthāditaḥ kṣipennīraṃ khārīṃ yāvaccaturguṇam 52
ambukvātharasairyatra pṛthak snehasya sādhanam
kalkasyāṃśaṃ tatra dadyāccaturtha ṣaṣṭhamaṣṭamam 53
dugdhedadhni rase takre kalko deyo'ṣṭamāṃśakaḥ
kalkācca samyak pākārthaṃ toyamatra caturguṇam 54
dra vāṇi yatra sneheṣu pañcādīni bhavanti hi
tatra snehasamānyāhuryathāpūrvaṃ caturguṇam 55
dra vyeṇa kevalenaiva snehapāko bhaved yadi
tatrāmbupiṣṭaḥ kalkaḥ syājjalaṃ cātra caturguṇam 56
kvāthena kevalenaiva pāko yatroditaḥ kvacit
kvāthyadra vyasya kalko'pi tatra snehe prayujyate 57
kalkahīnastu yaḥ snehaḥ sa sādhyaḥ kevale dra ve 58
puṣpakalkastu yaḥ snehastatra toyaṃ caturguṇam
snehāt snehāṣṭamāṃśaśca puṣpakalkaḥ prayujyate 59
varttivatsnehakalkaḥ syād yadā'ṅgulyā vivarttitaḥ
śabdahīno'gninikṣiptaḥsnehaḥ siddho bhavettadā 60
yadā phenodgamastaile phenaśāntiśca sarpiṣi
varṇagandharasotpattiḥ snehaḥ siddho bhavettadā 61
snehapākastridhā prokto mṛdurmadhyaḥ kharastathā
īṣatsarasakalkastu snehapāko mṛdurbhavet 62
madhyapākasya siddhiśca kalke nīrasakomale
īṣatkaṭhinakalkaśca snehapāko bhavetkharaḥ 63
tadūrdhvaṃ dagdhapākaḥ syāddāhakṛnniṣprayojanaḥ
āmapākaśca nirvīryo vahnimāndyakaro guruḥ 64
nasyārthaṃ syānmṛduḥ pāko madhyamaḥ sarvakarmasu
abhyaṅgārthaḥ kharaḥ prokto yuñjyādevaṃ yathocitam 65
ghṛtatailaguḍādīṃśca sādhayennaikavāsare
prakurvuntyuṣitāstvete viśeṣād guṇasañcayam 66
dra veṣu cirakālasthaṃ dra vyaṃ yatsandhitaṃ bhavet
āsavāriṣṭabhedaistu procyate bheṣajocitam 67
yadapakvauṣadhāmbubhyāṃ siddhaṃ madyaṃ sa āsavaḥ
ariṣṭaḥ kvāthasādhyaḥ syāttayormānaṃ palonmitam 68
anuktamānāriṣṭeṣu dra vād dro ṇaṃ guḍāttulām
kṣaudraṃ kṣiped guḍādarddhaṃ prakṣepaṃ daśamāṃśikam 69
jñeyaḥ śītarasaḥ sīghurapakvamadhuradra vaiḥ
siddhaḥ pakvarasaḥ sīdhuḥ sampakvamadhuradra vaiḥ 70
paripakvānnasandhānātsamutpannāṃ surāṃ jaguḥ
surāmaṇḍaḥ prasannā syāttataḥ kādamvarī ghanā 71
tadadho jagalo jñeyo medako jagalādghanaḥ
vakkaso hṛtasāraḥ syātsurābījaṃ tu kiṇvakam 72
yattālakharjūrarasaiḥ sandhitā sā hi vāruṇī 73
kandamūlaphalādīni sasnehalavaṇāni ca
yatra dra ve'bhiṣūyante tacchuktamabhidhīyate 74
vinaṣṭamamlatāṃ yātaṃ madyaṃ vā madhuradra vaḥ
vinaṣṭaḥ sandhito yastu taccukramabhidhīyate 75
guḍāmbunā satailena kandaśākaphalaistathā
saṃdhitaṃ cāmlatāṃ yātaṃ guḍacukraṃ pracakṣyate
evameva hi śuktaṃ syānmṛdvīkāsambhavaṃ tathā 76
tuṣāmbu sandhitaṃ jñeyamāmairvidalitairyavaḥ
yavaistu nistuṣaiḥ pakvaiḥ sauvīraṃ sādhitaṃ bhavet 77
āranālantu godhūmairāmaiḥ syānnistustuṣīkṛtaiḥ
pakvairvā saṃhitaṃ tattu sauvīrasadṛśaṃ guṇaiḥ 78
kulmāṣadhānyamaṇḍādisahitaṃ kāñjikaṃ viduḥ
śiṇḍākī saṃhitā jñeyā mūlakaiḥ sarṣapādibhiḥ 79
iti śrīmiśralaṭakana tanaya śrīmiśrabhāva viracite bhāvaprakāśe paribhāṣādiprakaraṇe
dvitīyaṃ bheṣajavidhānaprakaraṇaṃ samāptam 2
<1.2.3>
atha tṛtīyaṃ dhātvādiśodhanamāraṇa vidhiprakaraṇam 3
dāhe raktaṃ sitaṃ chede nikaṣe kuṅkumaprabham
tāraśulbojjhitaṃ snigdhaṃ komalaṃ guru hema sat 1
tacchede kaṭhinaṃ rūkṣaṃ vivarṇaṃ samalaṃ dalam
dāhe chede sitaṃ śvetaṃ kaṣe sphuṭaṃ laghu tyajet 2
pattalīkṛtapatrāṇi hemno vahnau pratāpayet
niṣiñcettaptataptāni taile takre ca kāñjike 3
gomūtre ca kulatthānāṃ kaṣāye tu tridhā tridhā
evaṃ hemnaḥ pareṣāñca dhātūnāṃ śodhanaṃ bhavet 4
balaṃ savīryyaṃ harate narāṇāṃ rogavrajaṃ poṣayatīha kāye
asaukhyakāryeva sadā suvarṇamaśuddhametanmaraṇañca kuryāt 5
svarṇasya dviguṇaṃ sūtamamlena saha mardayet
tadgolakasamaṃ gandhaṃ nidadhyādadharottaram 6
golakañca tato ruddhvā śarāvadṛḍhasampuṭe
triṃśadvanopalairdadyātpuṭānyevaṃ caturdaśa
nirutthaṃ jāyate bhasma gandho deyaḥ punaḥ punaḥ 7
kāñcane galite nāgaṃ ṣoḍaśāṃśena nikṣipet
cūrṇayitvā tathāmlena ghṛṣṭvā kṛtvā tu golakam 8
golakena samaṃ gandhaṃ dattvā caivādharottaram 9
śarāvasampuṭe dhṛtvā puṭet triṃśadvanopalaiḥ
evaṃ saptapuṭairhema nirutthaṃ bhasma jāyate 10
kāñcanārarasairghṛṣṭvā samasūtakagandhayoḥ
kajjaladyaṃ hemapatrāṇi lepayetsamayā tathā 11
kāñcanāratvacaḥ kalkairmūṣāyugmaṃ prakalpayet
dhṛtvā tatsaṃpuṭe golaṃ mṛṇmūṣāsampuṭe ca tat 12
nidhāya sandhirodhaṃ ca kṛtvā saṃśoṣya golakam
vahniṃ kharataraṃ kuryādevaṃ dattvā puṭatrayam 13
nirutthaṃ jāyate bhasma sarvakarmasu yojayet
kāñcanāraprakāreṇa lāṅgalī hanti kāñcanam 14
jvālāmukhī tathā hanyād yathā hanti manaḥśilā 15
śilāsindūrayoścūrṇaṃ samayorarkadugdhakaiḥ
saptadhā bhāvanāṃ dadyācchoṣayecca punaḥ punaḥ 16
tatastu galite hemni kalko'ya dīyate samaḥ
punardhamedatitarāṃ yathā kalko vilīyate
evaṃ velātrayaṃ dadyātkalkaṃ hemamṛtirbhavet 17
suvarṇaṃ śītalaṃ vṛṣyaṃ balyaṃ guru rasāyanam
svādu tiktaṃ ca tuvaraṃ pāke ca svādu picchilam 18
pavitraṃ bṛṃhaṇaṃ netr! yaṃ medhāsmṛtimatipradam
hṛdyāmāyuṣkaraṃ kāntivāgviśuddhisthiratvakṛt
viṣadvayakṣayonmādatridoṣajvaraśoṣajit 19
asamyaṅmāritaṃ svarṇaṃ balaṃ vīryaṃ ca nāśayet
karoti rogān mṛtyuṃ ca taddhanyād yatnatastataḥ 20
lohāderanapunarbhāvastadguṇatvaṃ guṇāḍhyatā
salile taraṇaṃ cāpi tatsiddhiḥ puṭanādbhavet 21
gambhīre vistṛte kuṇḍe dvihaste caturasrake
vanopalasahasreṇa pūrite puṭanauṣadham 22
koṣṭhe ruddhaṃ prayatnena goviṣṭhopari dhārayet
vanopalasahasrārddhaṃ koṣṭhikopari nikṣipet 23
vahniṃ vinikṣipettatra mahāpuṭamiti smṛtam 24
sapādahastamānena kuṇḍe nimne tathā'yate
vanopalasahasreṇa pūrṇe madhye vidhārayet 25
puṭanadra vyasaṃyuktāṃ koṣṭikāṃ mudri tāṃ mukhe
adho'dhāni karaṇḍāni ardhānyupari nikṣipet
etadgajapuṭaṃ proktaṃ khyātaṃ sarvapuṭottamam 26
aratnimātrake kuṇḍe puṭaṃ vārāhamucyate
vitastimātrake khāte kathitaṃ kaukkuṭaṃ puṭam 27
ṣoḍaśāṅgulake khāte kasyacitkaukkuṭaṃ puṭam 28
yatpuṭaṃ dīyate khāte hyaṣṭasaṃkhyairvanopalaiḥ
kapotapuṭametattu kathitaṃ puṭapaṇḍitaiḥ 29
goṣṭhāntargokhurakṣuṇṇaṃ śuṣkaṃ cūrṇitagomayam
gobaraṃ tatsamākhyātaṃ variṣṭhaṃ rasasādhane 30
bṛhadbhāṇḍasthitairyatra gaubarairdīyate puṭam
tad gobarapuṭaṃ proktaṃ bhiṣagbhiḥ sūtabhasmani 31
bṛhadbhāṇḍe tuṣaiḥ pūrṇe madhye mūṣāṃ vidhārayet
kṣiptvā'gni mudra yedbhāṇḍaṃ tadbhāṇḍapuṭamucyate 32
bhāṇḍe vitastigambhīre madhye nihitakūpike
kūpikākaṇṭhaparyantaṃ vālukābhiśca pūrite 33
bheṣajaṃ kūpikāsaṃsthaṃ vahninā yatra pacyate
bālukāyantrametaddhi yantraṃ tatra budhaiḥ smṛtam 34
nibaddhamauṣadhaṃ sūtaṃ bhūrje tattriguṇe vare
rasapoṭalikāṃ kāṣṭhe dṛḍhaṃ baddhvā guṇena hi 35
sandhānapūrṇakumbhāntaḥsvāvalambanasandhitam
adhastājjvālayedagniṃ tattaduktakrameṇa hi
dolāyantramidaṃ proktaṃ svedanākhyaṃ tadeva hi 36
sāmbusthālīmukhe baddhe vastre svedyaṃ nidhāya ca
pidhāya pacyate tatra tad yantraṃ svedanaṃ smṛtam 37
adhaḥsthālyāṃ rasaṃ kṣiptvā vidadhyāttanmukhopari
sthālīmūrdhvamukhīṃ samyaṅnirudhya mṛdumṛtsnayā 38
ūrdhvasthālyāṃ jalaṃ kṣiptvā cullyāmāropya yatnataḥ
adhastājjvālayedagniṃ yāvatpraharapañcakam 39
svāṅgaśītaṃ tato yantrād gṛhṇīyādra samuttamam
vidyādharābhidhaṃ yantrametattajjñairudāhṛtam 40
bālukābhiḥ samastāṅgaṃ gartte mūṣāṃ rasānvitām
dīptopalaiḥ saṃvṛṇuyād yantraṃ bhūdharanāmakam 41
yantraṃ ḍamarusaṃjñaṃ syāttatsthālyā mudri te mukhe 42
guru snigdhaṃ mṛdu śvetaṃ dāhe chede ghanakṣamam
varṇāḍhyaṃ candra vat svacchaṃ tāraṃ navaguṇaṃ śubham 43
kaṭhinaṃ kṛtrimaṃ rūkṣaṃ raktaṃ pītadalaṃ laghu
dāhacchedaghanairnaṣṭaṃ rūpyaṃ duṣṭaṃ prakīrttitam 44
pattalīkṛtapatrāṇi tārasyāgnau pratāpayet
niṣiñcettaptataptāni taile takre ca kāñjike 45
gomūtre ca kulatthānāṃ kaṣāye ca tridhā tridhā
evaṃ rajatapatrāṇāṃ viśuddhiḥ saṃprajāyate 46
rūpyaṃ tvaśuddhaṃ prakaroti tāpaṃ vibandhakaṃ vīryabalakṣayaṃ ca
dehasya puṣṭiṃ harate tanoti rogāṃstataḥ śodhanamasya kuryāt 47
bhāgaikaṃ tālakaṃ mardyaṃ yāmamamlena kenacit
tena bhāgatrayaṃ tārapattrāṇi parilepayet 48
dhṛtvā mūṣāpuṭe ruddhvā puṭettriṃśadvanopalaiḥ
samuddhṛtya punastālaṃ dattvā ruddhvā puṭe pacet
evaṃ caturdaśapuṭaistāraṃ bhasma prajāyate 49
snuhīkṣīreṇa sampiṣṭaṃ mākṣikaṃ tena lepayet 50
tālakasya prakāreṇa tārapatrāṇi buddhimān
puṭeccaturdaśapuṭaistāraṃ bhasma prajāyate 51
raupyaṃ śītaṃ kaṣāyaṃ ca svādupākarasaṃ saram
vayasaḥ sthāpanaṃ snigdhaṃ lekhanaṃ vātapittajit
pramehādikarogāṃśca nāśayatyacirādr dhruvam 52
japākusumasaṅkāśaṃ snigdhaṃ guru ghanakṣamam
lohanāgojjhitaṃ tāmraṃ māraṇāya praśasyate 53
kṛṣṇaṃ rūkṣamatisvacchaṃ śvetaṃ cāpi ghanāsaham
lohanāgayutaṃ ceti śulvaṃ duṣṭaṃ prakīrttitam 54
pattalīkṛtapatrāṇi tāmrasyāgnau pratāpayet
niṣiñcettaptataptāni taile takre ca kāñjike 55
gomūtre ca kulatthānāṃ kaṣāye ca tridhā tridhā
evaṃ tāmrasya patrāṇāṃ viśuddhiḥ saṃprajāyate 56
eko doṣo viṣe tāmre tvaśuddhe'ṣṭau bhramo vamiḥ
virekasveda utkledo mūrcchā dāho'rucistathā 57
na viṣaṃ viṣamityāhustāmrantu viṣamucyate
eko doṣo viṣe tāmre tvaṣṭau doṣāḥ prakīrttitāḥ 58
sūkṣmāṇi tāmrapatrāṇi kṛtvā saṃsvedayed budhaḥ
vāsaratrayamamlena tataḥ khalve vinikṣipet 59

gandhakenāmlaghṛṣṭena tasya kuryācca golakam
tataḥ piṣṭvā ca mīnākṣīṃ cāṅgerīṃ vā punarnavām 61

dhṛtvā tad golakaṃ bhāṇḍe śarāveṇa ca rodhayet 62

svāṅgaśītaṃ samuddhṛtya mardayecchūraṇadra vaiḥ 64

vamanaṃ ca virekaṃ ca bhramaṃ klamamathārucim
vidāhaṃ svedamutkledaṃ na karoti kadācana 67
tāmraṃ kaṣāyaṃ madhuraṃ satiktamamlañca pāke kaṭu sārakaṃ ca
pittāpahaṃ śleṣmaharaṃ ca śītaṃ tadro paṇaṃ syāllaghu lekhanaṃ ca 68
pāṇḍūdarārśojvarakuṣṭhakāsaśvāsakṣayānpīnasamamlapittam
śothaṃ kṛmīñśūlamapākaroti prāhurbudhā bṛṃhaṇamalpametat 69
eko doṣo viṣe tāmre tvasamyaṅmārite punaḥ
dāhaḥ svedo'rucirmūrcchotkledo reko vamirbhramaḥ 70
vaṅgaṃ ca girijaṃ tacca khurakaṃ miśrakaṃ dvidhā
tayostu khurakaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ miśrakaṃ tvahitaṃ matam 71
vaṅgaṃ vidhatte khalu śuddhihīnamākṣepakampau ca kilāsagulmau
kuṣṭhāni śūlaṃ kila vātaśothaṃ pāṇḍuṃ pramehañca bhagandarañca 72

mehāśmarīvidra dhimuṣkarogān nāgo'pi kuryātkathitānvikārān 73
vaṅganāgau prataptau ca galitau tau niṣecayet
tridhā tridhā viśuddhiḥ syādra vidugdhe'pi ca tridhā 74
mṛtpātre drā vite vaṅge ciñcā'śvatthatvaco rajaḥ
piṣṭvā vaṅgacaturthāṃśamathodarvyā pracālayet 75

atha bhasmasamaṃ tālaṃ kṣiptvā'mlena vimardayet 76

evaṃ daśapuṭaiḥ pakvaṃ vaṅgaṃ bhavati māritam 77

nihanti pāṇḍuṃ saśvāsaṃ netr! yamīṣattu pittalam 78

nānārujānāṃ ca tathā prakopaṃ kuryācca hṛllāsamaśuddhalauham 89
pattalīkṛtapatrāṇi lauhasyāgnau pratāpayet
niṣiñcettaptataptāni taile takre ca kāñjike 90

evaṃ lauhasya patrāṇāṃ viśuddhiḥ saṃprajāyate 91
śuddhaṃ lauhabhavaṃ cūrṇaṃ pātālagaruḍīrasaiḥ

puṭaṣaṭkaṃ tato dadyādevaṃ tīkṣṇamṛtirbhavet 93
