niculaṃ triphalā bhārgī rohiṣaṃ gajapippalī 96
śṛṅgaberaviḍaṅgāni cavyaṃ kampillakaṃ tathā
garbheṇānena tatsiddhaṃ pāyayettu yathābalam 97
etaddhānvantaraṃ nāma vikhyātaṃ sarpiruttamam
kuṣṭhapramehagulmāṃśca śvayathuṃ vātaśoṇitam 98
plīhodarāṇi cārśāṃsi vidra dhiṃ piḍikāśca yāḥ
apasmāraṃ tathonmādaṃ sarpiretanniyacchati 99
pṛthaktoye'maṇe hyatra paced dra vyācchataṃ śatam
śatatrayādhike toye vyutsargaḥ kramato bhavet 100
arjunapaṭolanimbaiḥ savacādīpyakarasāsamañjiṣṭhaiḥ
bhallātakāgurughanaiḥ sagadānalacandanośīraiḥ 101
gokṣurakasomavalkairnavapaṭolairharidra yā triphalayā
aśmantakārjunābhyāṃ dīpyakayuktena caiva lodhreṇa 102
mañjiṣṭhā'tiviṣābhyāṃ kalkakaṣāyaiḥ pacettailam
kaphavātotthe mehe pittakṛte sādhayetsarpiḥ 103
sāravargakaṣāyaṃ caturthāṃśāvaśiṣṭamavatārya parisrāvya punarapanīya sādhayet
siddhyti cāmalakalodhrapriyaṅgudantīkṛṣṇāyasatāmracūrṇānyāvapet
tadetad dagdhaṃ lehībhūtamavatāryānuguptaṃ nidadhyāt
tato yathāyogamupayañjīta eṣa lehaḥ sarvamehānapahanti 104
gokaṇṭakaṃ sadalamūlaphalaṃ gṛhītvā saṅakuṭṭitaṃ palaśataṃ kvathitaṃ tu toye
pādasthitena salilena palāni datvā pañcāśataṃ tu vipacedatha śarkarāyāḥ 105
tasminghanatvamupagacchati cūrṇitāni dadyātpaladvayamitāni subhājanāni
śuṇṭhīkaṇāmaricanāgadalatvagelā jātīyakoṣakakubhatrapusīphalāni 106
vāṃśīpalāṣṭakamiha praṇidhāya nityaṃ lehyaṃ tu śuddhamamṛtaṃ palasammitantu
hantyāśu mūtraparidāhavibandhaśukrakṛcchrāśmarīrudhiramehamadhupramehān 107
asanaśca priyālañca śālaṃ khādirakaṃ tathā
śālavargaṃ tathā grāhyaṃ bhaveccaitadvicakṣaṇaiḥ 108
madhumehatvamāpannaṃ bhiṣagbhiḥ parivarjitam
yogenānena matimānpramehiṇamupācaret 109
māsi śukre śucau vā'pi śailāḥ sūryāṃśutāpitāḥ
jatuprakāśaṃ svarasaṃ śilābhyaḥ prasravanti hi 110
śilājatviti vikhyātaṃ mahāvyādhinivāraṇam
trapvādīnāṃ tu lohānāṃ ṣaṇṇāmanyatamañca yat 111
jñeyaṃ svagandhataścāpi ṣaḍyoniprathitaṃ kṣitau
lohādbhavati tadyasmācchilājatu jatuprabham 112
tasya lohasya tadvīryaṃ rasaṃ vāpi bibhartti tat
trapusīsāyasādīni pradhānānyuttarottaram 113
yathā tathā prayogepi śreṣṭhe śreṣṭhaguṇāḥ smṛtāḥ
tatsarvaṃ tiktakaṭukaṃ kaṣāyānurasaṃ saram 114
kaṭupākyuṣṇavīryañca śoṣaṇaṃ chedanaṃ tathā
tatra yallaghu kṛṣṇābhaṃ snigdhaṃ niḥśarkarañca yat 115
gomūtragandhi nīlaṃ vā tatpradhānañca vakṣyate
tadbhāvitaṃ sāragaṇairhṛtadoṣaṃ dināditaḥ 116
pibetsārodakenaiva ślakṣṇapiṣṭaṃ yathābalam
jāṅgalena rasenādyāttasmiñjīrṇe tu bhojanam 117
upayujya tulāmekāmamṛtasyāsya janmataḥ
vijitya madhumehākhyamātaṅkaṃ rogakārakam 118
vapurvarṇabalopetaḥ śataṃ jīvatyanāmayaḥ
śataṃ śataṃ tulāyāṃ tu sahasraṃ daśataulikam 119
bhallātakavidhānena parihāravidhiḥ smṛtaḥ
mehaṃ kuṣṭhamapasmāramunmādaṃ ślīpadaṃ garam 120
śoṣaṃ śophārśasī gulmaṃ pāṇḍutāṃ viṣamajvaram
vyapohatyacirātkālācchilājatu niṣevitam 121
na so'sti rogo yaṃ vāpi na nihanyācchilājatu
śarkarā cirasambhūtāṃ bhinatti ca tathāśmarīm 122
bhāvanāloḍane cāsya karttavye bheṣajairhitaiḥ
evaṃ ca mākṣikaṃ dhātuṃ tāpījamamṛtopamam 123
madhuraṃ kāñcanābhāsamamlaṃ vā rajataprabham
vyapohati jarākuṣṭhamehapāṇḍvāmayakṣayān 124
tadbhāvitānkulatthāṃśca kapotāṃśca vivarjayet 125
pramehapiḍikānāṃ prākkāryaṃ raktāvasecanam
pāṭanañca vipakvānāṃ tāsāṃ pāne praśasyate 126
kvātho vanaspaterbāstyaṃ mūtraṃ tīkṣṇañca śodhanam
elādikena kalkena tailaṃ ca vraṇaropaṇam 127
āragvadhādinā kvāthaṃ kuryādudvarttanāni ca
śālasārādinā sekānbhojyādīṃścaṇakādinā 128
pramehiṇo yadā mūtramanāvilamapicchilam
viśadaṃ tiktakaṭukaṃ tadārogyaṃ pracakṣate 129
ityaṣṭatriṃśaḥ pramehapiḍikā'dhikāraḥ samāptaḥ 38
<2.3.10>
athaikonacatvāriṃśattamaḥ sthaulyādhikāraḥ 39
avyāyāmadivā svapnaśleṣmalāhārasevinaḥ
madhuro'nnarasaḥ prāyaḥ snehānmedo vivarddhayet 1
medasāvṛtamārgatvātpuṣyantyanye na dhātavaḥ
medastu cīyate tasmādaśaktaḥ sarvakarmasu 2
kṣudra śvāsatṛṣāmohasvapnakrathanasādanaiḥ
yuktaḥ kṣutsvedadaurgandhyairalpaprāṇo'lpamaithunaḥ 3
medastu sarvabhūtānāmudare hi vyavasthitam
ata evodare vṛddhiḥ prāyo medasvino bhavet 4
medasā'vṛtamārgatvādvāyuḥ koṣṭhe viśeṣataḥ
caransandhukṣayatyagnimāhāraṃ śoṣayatyapi 5
tasmātsa śīghraṃ jarayatyāhārañcābhikāṅkṣati
vikārāṃścāśnute ghorānkāṃścitkālavyatikramāt 6
etāvupadra vakarau viśeṣādagnimārutau
etau hi dahataḥ sthūlaṃ vanaṃ dāvānilau yathā 7
medasyatīva saṃvṛddhe sahasaivānilādayaḥ
vikārāndāruṇānkṛtvā nāśayantyāśu jīvitam 8
medomāṃsātivṛddhatvāccalasphigudarastanaḥ
ayathopacayotsāho naro'tisthūla ucyate 9
sthūle syurdustarāḥ kuṣṭhā visarpāḥ sabhagandarāḥ
jvarātīsāramehārśaḥślīpadāpacikāmalāḥ
medasaḥ svedadaurgandhyājjāyante jantavo'ṇavaḥ 10
purāṇāḥ śālayo mudgāḥ kulatthoddālakodra vāḥ
lekhanā vastayaścaiva sevyā medasvinā sadā 11
dhūmrapānaṃ tathā krodho raktamokṣaṇameva ca
jīrṇe ca bhojanaṃ kāryaṃ yavagodhūmayoḥsadā 12
upavāso'sukhā śayyā sattvaudāryaṃ tamojayaḥ
santarpaṇakṛtairdoṣaiḥsthaulyād yuktyā vimucyate 13
śramacintāvyavāyādhva kṣaudra jāgaraṇapriyaḥ
hantyavaśyamatisthaulyaṃ yavaśyāmākabhojanaḥ 14
sacavyajīrakavyoṣahiṅgu sauvarcalānalāḥ
mastunā śaktavaḥ pītā medoghnā vahnidīpanāḥ 15
phalatrayaṃ trikaṭukaṃ satailaṃ lavaṇānvitam
ṣaṇmāsādupayogena kaphamedo'nilāpaham 16
viḍaṅgaṃ nāgaraṃ kṣāraḥ kālaloharajo madhu
yavāmalakacūrṇantu yogo'tisthaulyanāśanaḥ 17
mūlaṃ vā triphalācūrṇaṃ madhuyuktaṃ madhūdakam
bilvādipañcamūlasya prayogaḥ kṣaudra saṃyutaḥ 18
atisthaulyaharaḥ prokto maṇḍakaḥ sevito dhruvam 19
karkaśadalavahnisalilaṃ śatapuṣpāhiṅgusaṃyuktam
pibato nihantiniyataṃ sarvabhavāṃ medasāṃvṛddhim 20
kṣāraṃ vātāripatrasya hiṅguyuktaṃ pibennaraḥ
medovṛddhivināśāya bhaktaṃ maṇḍasamanvitam 21
gavedhukānāṃ piṣṭānāṃ yavānāñcātha saktavaḥ
sakṣaudra triphalākvāthaḥ pīto medoharo mataḥ 22
guḍūcītriphalākvāthastathā loharajo'nvitaḥ
aśmajaṃ mahiṣākṣaṃ vā tenaiva vidhinā pacet 23
atimuktabījamadhyaṃ madhulīḍhaṃhantyudaravṛddhim
madhunā citrakamūlaṃ tathaiva hitabhojano bhuṅkte 24
yadvoruvūkamūlaṃ madhudigdhaṃ sthāpyate niśāṃ sakalām
tasya salilasya pānājjaṭhare vṛddhiḥ śamaṃ yāti 25
prātarmadhuyutaṃ vāri sevitaṃ sthaulyanāśanam
uṣṇamannasya maṇḍaṃ vā pibankṛśatanurbhavet 26
badarīpatrakalkena peyā kāñjikasādhitā
sthaulyanutsyādagnimantharasakvāthaḥ śilājatu 27
śaileyakuṣṭhāgurudevadārukauntīsamustānyatha pañcapatraiḥ
śrīvāsaspṛkkākharapuṣpadevapuṣpaṃ tathā sarvamidaṃ prapiṣya
dhattūrapatrasya rasena gāḍhamudvarttanaṃ sthaulyaharaṃ pradiṣṭam 28
amṛtātruṭivellavatsakaṃ kalipathyā'malakāni gugguluḥ
kramavṛddhamidaṃ madhuplutaṃ piḍikāsthaulyabhagandarāñjayet 29
vyoṣāgnitriphalāmustaviḍaṅgairguggulaṃ samam
khādansarvāñjayed vyādhīnmedaḥśleṣmāmavātajān 30
tryūṣaṇāgnighanavellavacābhirbhakṣayansamaghṛtaṃ mahiṣākṣam
āśu hanti kaphamārutamedodoṣajānbalavato'pi vikārān 31
guggulustālamūlī ca triphalā khadiraṃ vṛṣam
trivṛtā'lambuṣā śuṇṭhī nirguṇḍī citrakastathā 32
eṣāṃ daśapalānbhāgāṃstoye pañcāḍhake pacet
pādaśeṣaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā kaṣāyamavatārayet 33
paladvādaśakaṃ deyaṃ rukmalohaṃ sucūrṇitam 34
purāṇasarpiṣaḥ prasthaṃ śarkarā'ṣṭapalānvitam
pacettāmramaye pātre suśīte cāvadārite 35
prasthārddhaṃ mākṣikaṃ deyaṃ śilājatu paladvayam
elātvacoḥ palārddhañca viḍaṅgāni palatrayam 36
maricaṃ cāñjanaṃ kṛṣṇā dvipalaṃ triphalā'nvitam
paladvayantu kāsīsaṃ sūkṣmacūrṇīkṛtaṃ budhaiḥ 37
cūrṇaṃ datvā sumathitaṃ snigdhe bhāṇḍe nidhāpayet
tataḥ saṃśuddhadehastu bhakṣayedakṣamātrakam 38
anupānaṃ pibetkṣīraṃ jāṅgalānāṃ rasaṃ tathā
vātaśleṣmaharaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ kuṣṭhamehodarāpaham 39
kāmalāṃ pāṇḍurogañca śvayathuṃ sabhagandaram
mūrcchāmohaviṣonmādagarāṇi viṣamāṇi ca 40
sthūlānāṃ karṣaṇaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ medure paramauṣadham
karṣayeccātimātreṇa kukṣiṃ pātālasannibham 41
balyaṃ rasāyanaṃ medhyaṃ vājīkaraṇamuttamam
śrīkaraṃ putrajananaṃ valīpalitanāśanam 42
nāśnīyātkadalīkandaṃ kāñjikaṃ karamardakam
karīraṃ kāravellañca kakārādi vivarjayet 43
śālasārādiniryūhaṃ caturthāṃśāvaśeṣitam
parisrutaṃ tataḥ śītaṃ madhunā madhurīkṛtam 44
phāṇitībhāvamāpannaṃ guḍaṃ śodhitameva ca
sūkṣmapiṣṭāni cūrṇāni pippalyādergaṇasya ca 45
ekadhyamāvapetkumbhe saṃskṛte ghṛtabhāvite
pippalīcūrṇamadhubhiḥ pralipte cāntare śucau 46
sūkṣmāṇi tīkṣṇalohasya tanupatrāṇi buddhimān
khadirāṅgārataptāni bahuśaḥ prakṣiped budhaḥ 47
supidhānaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā yavapalve nidhāpayet
māsāṃstrīṃścaturo vā'pi yāvadvā lohasaṃkṣayāt 48
tato jātarasaṃ jantuḥ prātaḥ prātaryathābalam
upayuñjyādyathāyogyamāhāraṃ cāsya kalpayet 49
eṣa sthūlaṃ samākarṣennaṣṭasyāgneḥ prabodhanaḥ
śothaghnaḥ kuṣṭhamehaghno gulmapāṇḍvāmayāpahaḥ 50
plīhodaraharaḥ śīghraṃ viṣamajvaranāśanaḥ
abhiṣyandāpaharaṇe lohāriṣṭo mahāguṇaḥ 51
vyoṣacitrakaśigrūṇi triphalāṃ kaṭurohiṇīm
bṛhatyau dve haridre ca pāṭhāmativiṣāṃ sthirām
hiṅgukevukamūlāni yavānīṃ dhānyacitrakam
sauvarccalamajājīñca hapuṣāṃ ceti cūrṇayet 53
cūrṇatailaghṛtakṣaudra bhāgāḥ syurmānataḥ samāḥ
śaktūnāṃ ṣoḍaśaguṇo bhāgaḥ santarpaṇaṃ pibet 54
prayogāttvasya śāmyanti rogāḥ santarpaṇotthitāḥ
pramehā mūḍhavātāśca kuṣṭhānyarśāṃsi kāmalāḥ 55
plīhā pāṇḍvāmayaḥ śotho mūtrakṛcchramarocakaḥ
hṛdro go rājayakṣmā ca kāsaśvāsau galagrahaḥ 56
kṛmayo grahaṇīdoṣaḥ śvaitryaṃ sthaulyamatīva ca
narāṇāṃ dīpyate vahniḥ smṛtirbuddhiśca varddhate 57
triphalātiviṣāmūrvātri vṛccitrakavāsakaiḥ
nimbāragvadhaṣaḍgranthā saptaparṇaniśādvayaiḥ 58
guḍūcīndrā surīkṛṣṇā kuṣṭhasarṣapanāgaraiḥ
tailamebhiḥ samaiḥ pakvaṃ surasādirasāplutam 59
pānābhyañjanagaṇḍūṣanasyavastiṣu yojitam
sthūlatālasyapāṇḍvādīñjayetkaphakṛtāngadān 60
candanaṃ kuṅkamośīrapriyaṅgutruṭirocanāḥ
turuṣkāgurukastūrīkarpūrā jātipatrikā 61
jātīkaṅkolapūgānāṃ lavaṅgasya phalāni ca
nalikā naladaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ hareṇu tagaraṃ plavam 62
navavyāghranakhaṃ spṛkkā bolaṃ damanakaṃ tathā
sthauṇeyakaṃ corakañca śaileyaṃ sailavālukam 63
saralaṃ saptaparṇañca lākṣā tāmalakī tathā
lāmajjakaṃ padmakañca dhātakyāḥ kusumāni ca 64
prapauṇḍarīkaṃ karcūraṃ samāṃśaiḥ śāṇamātrakaiḥ
mahāsugandhamityetattailaprasthena sādhayet 65
prasvedajaladaurgandhyakaṇḍūkuṣṭhaharaṃ param
anenābhyaktagātrastu vṛddhaḥ saptatikopi vā 66
yuvā bhavati śukrāḍhyaḥ strīṇāmatyantavallabhaḥ
subhago darśanīyaśca gacchecca pramadāśatam 67
vandhyāpi labhate garbhaṃ ṣaṇḍhopi puruṣāyate
aputraḥ putramāpnoti jīvecca śaradāṃ śatam 68
iti mahāsugandhi tailam
vāsādalaraso lepācchaṅkhacūrṇena saṃyutaḥ
bilvapatraraso vāpi gātradaurgandhyanāśanaḥ 69
alambuṣābhavaṃ cūrṇaṃ pītaṃ kāñjikasaṃyutam
daurgandhyaṃ nāśayatyāśu duṣṭaṃ medobhavaṃ nṛṇām 70
bilvaśivā samabhāgā lepād bhujamūlagandhamapaharati
pariṇatapiḍikāñcāpi pūtikarañjotthabījaṃ vā 71
ciñcāpatrasvarasaṃ mrakṣitakalkādiyojitaṃ jayati
dagdhaharidro dvarttanamacirācciradehadaurgandhyam 72
śirīṣalāmajjakahemalodhraistvagdoṣasaṃsvedaharaḥ pragharṣaḥ
patrāmbulohābhayacandanāni śarīradaurgandhyaharaḥ pradehaḥ 73
hilamociraso yuktaścūrṇairudadhiphenajaiḥ
pralepena haratyāśu dehadaurgandhyamutkaṭam 74
harītakīṃ tu sampiṣya gātramudvarttayennaraḥ
paścātsnānaṃ prakurvīta dehasvedapraśāntaye 75
harītakī lodhramariṣṭapatraṃ cūtatvaco dāḍimavalkalañca
eṣo'ṅgarāgaḥ kathito'ṅganānāṃ jambvāḥ kaṣāyastu narādhipānām 76
gomūtrapiṣṭaṃ vinihanti kuṣṭhaṃ varṇojjvalaṃ gopayasā ca yuktam
kakṣādidaurgandhyaharaṃ payobhiḥ śastaṃ vaśīkṛdra janīdvayena 77
babbūlasya dalaiḥ samyagvāriṇā paripeṣitaiḥ
gātramudvarttayetpaścāddharītakyā supiṣṭayā 78
bhūya udvarttanaṃ kṛtvā paścātsnānaṃ samācaret
prasvedānmucyate śīghraṃ tatastvevaṃ samācaret 79
bilvāmrajambūphalapūrakāṇāṃ patraiḥ kapitthasya dalānumiśraiḥ
āpūrvavatkarmavidhānayogairvacā viśodhyā varagandhahetoḥ 80
pathyānakhīcandanakuṣṭhasarjaiḥ punaḥ punaścāguruśarkarābhyām
dhūpo janānāṃ hṛdayāpahārī vikhyātanāmā malayānilo'yam 81
caṇḍāṃśukatilairlodhra śirīṣośīrakeśaraiḥ
udvarttanaṃ bhaved grīṣme svedakarmanivāraṇam 82
surayā samamabhayāphalacūrṇaṃ madhunā vilihya pratyūṣe
svedānhatvā labhate puruṣopyatyantasaurabhyam 83
mallīkusumābhayakarilepo gharme vicarcikādāhe
vicakilapatraharidre parkaṭipatrañca dūrvayā sahitam 84
sampiṣya gātralepādgharmavicarcī śamaṃ yāti 85
hastapādasrutau yojyo guggulaḥ pañcatiktakaḥ
aśaktau pañcatiktākhyaṃ ghṛtaṃ khādedatandri taḥ 86
ityekonacatvāriṃśattamaḥ sthaulyādhikāro vā medorogādhikāraḥ samāptaḥ 39
<2.3.11>
atha catvāriṃśattamaḥ kārśyādhikāraḥ 40
vāto rukṣānnapānāni laṅghanaṃ pramitāśanam
kriyā'tiyogaḥ śokaśca veganidrā vinigrahaḥ 1
nityaṃ rogo ratirnityaṃ vyāyāmo bhojanālpatā
bhītirdhanādicintā ca kārśyakāraṇamīritam 2
śuṣkasphigudaragrīvā dhamanījālasantatiḥ
tvagasthiśoṣo'tikṛśaḥ sthūlaparvānano mataḥ 3
plīhakāsakṣayaśvāsagulmārśāṃsyudarāṇi ca
bhṛśaṃ kṛśaṃ pradhāvanti rogāśca grahaṇīmukhāḥ
kaścidanyaḥ kṛśo'tīva balavāndṛśyate tadā 4
ādhānasamaye yasya śukrabhāgo'dhiko bhavet
medobhāgastu hīnaḥ syātsa kṛśo'pi mahābalaḥ 5
medasastvadhiko yasya śukrabhāgo'lpako bhavet
sa snigdho'pi supuṣṭo'pi balahīno vilokyate 6
rukṣānnādinimitte tu kṛśe yuñjīta bheṣajam
bṛṃhaṇaṃ balakṛd vṛṣyaṃ tathā vājīkarañca yat 7
pītā'śvagandhā payasā'ddhamāsaṃ ghṛtena tailena sukhāmbunā vā
kṛśasya puṣṭiṃ vapuṣo vidhatte bālasya sasyasya yathā'mbuvṛṣṭiḥ 8
aśvagandhasya kalkena kvāthe tasminpayasyapi
siddhaṃ tailaṃ kṛśāṅgānāmabhyaṅgādaṅgapuṣṭidam 9
puṣṭikṛdvālarogoktamaśvagandhāghṛtaṃ bhajet
vājīkaroditaṃ tadvadaśvagandhāghṛtādikam 10
svabhāvādatikārśyoyaḥ svabhāvādalpapāvakaḥ
svabhāvādabalo yaśca tasya nāsti cikitsitam 11
iti catvāriṃśattamaḥ kārśyādhikāraḥ samāptaḥ 40
<2.3.12>
athaikacatvāriṃśattama udarādhikāraḥ 41
rogāḥ sarve'pi mande'gnau sutarāmudarāṇi ca
ajīrṇānmalinaiścānnaiścīyante malasañcayāt 1
ruddhvā svedāmbuvāhīni doṣāḥ srotāṃsi sañcitāḥ
prāṇānapānānsaṃdūṣya janayantyudaraṃ nṛṇām 2
ādhmānaṃ gamane'śaktirdaurbalyaṃ durbalāgnitā
śothaḥ sadanamaṅgānāṃ saṅgo vātapurīṣayoḥ
dāhastandrā ca sarveṣu jaṭhareṣu bhavanti hi 3
pṛthagdoṣaiḥ samastaiśca plīhabaddhakṣatodakaiḥ
sambhavantyudarāṇyaṣṭau teṣāṃ liṅgaṃ pṛthak śṛṇu 4
tatra vātodare śothaḥ pāṇipānnābhikukṣiṣu
kukṣipārśvodarakaṭī pṛṣṭharukparvabhedanam 5
śuṣkakāso'ṅgamardaśca gurutāmalasaṃgrahaḥ
śyāvāruṇatvagāditvamakasmāddhrāsavṛddhimat 6
satodabhedamudaraṃ tanukṛṣṇaśirātatam
ādhmātadṛtivacchabdamāhataṃ prakaroti ca 7
vāyuścātra sarukchabdo vicaretsarvato gatiḥ 8
pittodare jvaro mūrcchā dāhastṛṭ kaṭukāsyatā
bhramo'tisāraḥ pītatvaṃ tvagādāvudaraṃ harit 9
pītatāmraśirānaddhaṃ sasvedaṃ soṣma dahyate
dhūmāyate mṛdusparśaḥ kṣiprapākaṃ pradūyate 10
śleṣmodare'ṅgasadanaṃ śvayathurgauravaṃ tathā
tandro tkleśo'ruciḥ svāpaḥ kāsaḥ śauklyaṃ tvagādiṣu 11
udaraṃ stimitaṃ snigdhaṃ śuklarājītataṃ mahat
cirābhivṛddhi kaṭhinaṃ śītasparśaṃ guru sthiram 12
striyo'nnapānaṃ nakhalomamūtraviḍārttavairyuktamasādhuvṛttāḥ
yasmai prayacchantyarayo garāṃśca duṣṭāmbudūṣīviṣasevanācca 13
tasyāśu raktaṃ kupitāśca doṣāḥ kuryuḥ sughoraṃ jaṭharaṃ triliṅgam
tacchītavāte bhṛśadurdine ca viśeṣataḥ kupyati dahyate ca 14
sa cāturo mūrcchati hi prasaktaṃ pāṇḍuḥ kṛśaḥ śuṣyati tṛṣṇayā ca
dūṣyodaraṃ kīrttitametadeva plīhodaraṃ kīrttayatā nibodha 15
varddhate plīhavṛddhyā yadvidyātplīhodaraṃ hi tat
hṛdvāme varddhate pārśve nimittaṃ tatra yasya yat 16
pravṛddhe plīhni liṅgāni yānyuktāni bhiṣagvaraiḥ
plīhodarepi dṛśyante tāni sarvāṇi dehinām
plīhodarasyaiva medo yakṛddālyudaraṃ tathā 17
savyānyapārśve yakṛti pravṛddhe jñeyaṃ yakṛddālyudaraṃ tadeva 18
yasyāntramannairupalepibhirvā bālaśmabhirvā pihitaṃ yathāvat
sañcīyate yasya malo narasya śanaiḥ śanaiḥ saṅkaravacca nāḍyām 19
nirudhyate tasya gude purīṣaṃ nireti kṛcchrādaticālpamalpam
hṛnnābhimadhye parivṛddhimeti tasyodaraṃ baddhagudaṃ vadanti 20
śalyaṃ tathā'nnopahitaṃ yadantraṃ bhuktaṃ bhinattyāgatamanyathā vā
tasmātsruto'ntrātsalilaprakāśaḥ srāvaḥ sravedvai gudatastu bhūyaḥ 21
nābheradhaścodarameti vṛddhiṃ nistudyate dālyati cātimātram
etatparisrāvyudaraṃ pradiṣṭaṃ kṣatodaraṃ kīrttayato nibodha 22
yaḥ snehapīto'pyanuvāsito vā vānto virikto'pyatha vā nirūḍhaḥ
pibejjalaṃ śītalamāśu tasya srotāṃsi dūṣyanti hi tadvahāni 23
snehopalipteṣvatha vā'pi teṣu dakodaraṃ pūrvavadabhyupaiti
snigdhaṃ mahattatparivṛttanābhi samantataḥ pūrṇamivāmbunā ca
yathā dṛtiḥ kṣubhyati kampate ca śabdāyate vā'pyudakodaraṃ tat 24
janmanaivodaraṃ sarvaṃ prāyaḥ kṛcchratamaṃ matam
balinastadajātāmbu yatnasādhyaṃ navotthitam 25
pakṣād baddhagudaṃ tūrdhvaṃ sarvaṃ jātodakaṃ tathā
prāyo bhavatyabhāvāya chidrā ntraṃ codaraṃ nṛṇām 26
payaḥ pūrṇā dṛtiriva kṣobhe śabdakaraṃ mṛdu
apravyaktaśirāśūnyaṃ nīrārttamudaraṃ mahat 27
ālasyamāsyavairasyaṃ mūtraṃ bahuśakṛd drutam
jātodakasya liṅgaṃsyānmandāgniḥ pāṇḍutā'pi ca 28
śūnākṣaṃ kuṭilopasthamupaklinnatanutvacam
balaśoṇitamāṃsāgniparikṣīṇañca varjayet 29
pārśvabhaṅgānnavidveṣa śophātīsārapīḍitam
viriktaṃ cāpyudariṇaṃ pūryamāṇaṃ vivarjayet 30
eraṇḍatailaṃ daśamūlamiśraṃ gomūtrayuktastriphalāraso vā
nihanti vātodaraśothaśūlaṃ kvāthaḥ samūtro daśamūlajaśca 31
kuṣṭhaṃ dantī yavakṣāro vyoṣaṃ trilavaṇaṃ vacā 32
ajājī dīpyakaṃ hiṅgu svarjikācavyacitrakam
śuṇṭhī coṣṇāmbhasā pītā vātodararujā'pahā 33
laśunasya tulāmekāṃ jaladro ṇe vipācayet
trikuṭu triphalā dantī hiṅgusaindhavacitrakam 34
devadāru vacā kuṣṭhaṃ madhu śigruḥ punarnavā
sauvarcalaṃ viḍaṅgāni dīpyako gajapippalī 35
eteṣāṃ palikānbhāgāṃstrivṛtaḥ ṣaṭ palāni ca
piṣṭvā kaṣāyeṇānena tailaṃ mṛdvagninā pacet 36
tatpibetprātarutthāya yathā'gnibalamātrayā
nihanti sakalān rogānudarāṇi viśeṣataḥ 37
mūtrakṛcchramudāvarttamantravṛddhiṃ gudakṛmīn
pārśvakukṣibhavaṃ śūlamāmaśūlamarocakam 38
yakṛdaṣṭhīlikānāhānplīhānaṃ cāṅgavedanām
māsamātreṇa naśyanti hyaśītirvātajā gadāḥ 39
pittodare tu balinaṃ pūrvamehaṃ virecayet
payasā ca trivṛtkalkai rubūkasya śṛtena ca 40
pippalyādigaṇenājyaṃ pācitaṃ pāyayedbhiṣak
naraṃ pathyabhujaṃ nityaṃ kaphodaranivṛttaye 41
nāgaratriphalākalkai rdadhyambuparipeṣitaiḥ
pācitaṃ tailamājyaṃ vā pibetsarvodareṣu ca 42
śāliṣaṣṭika godhūmayavanīvārabhojanam
nirūho recanaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ sarveṣu jaṭhareṣu ca 43
ānūpamaudakaṃ māṃsaṃ śākaṃ piṣṭakṛtaṃ tilāḥ
vyāyāmādhvadivāsvapnasnehapānāni varjayet 44
tathogralavaṇoṣṇāni vidāhīni gurūṇi ca
nādyādannāni jaṭhare toyapānañca varjayet 45
udarāṇāṃ malāḍhyatvād bahuśaḥ śodhanaṃ hitam
kṣīrameraṇḍajaṃ tailaṃ pibenmūtreṇa vā'sakṛt 46
vātodarī pibettakraṃ pippalīlavaṇānvitam
śarkarāmaricopetaṃ svādu pittodarī pibet 47
yavānīhapuṣā'jājīvyoṣayuktaṃ kaphodarī
sannipātodarī yuktaṃ trikaṭukṣārasaindhavaiḥ 48
yavānī hapuṣā dhānyaṃ triphalā copakuñcikā
kāravī pippalīmūlamajagandhā śaṭī vacā 49
śatāhvā jīrakaṃ vyoṣaṃ svarṇakṣīrī ca citrakam
dvau kṣārau pauṣkaraṃ mūlaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ lavaṇapañcakam 50
viḍaṅgaśca samāṃśāni dantyā bhāgatrayaṃ bhavet
trivṛdviśālā dviguṇā śātalā syāccaturguṇā 51
eṣa nārāyaṇo nāmnā cūrṇo rogagaṇāpahaḥ
enaṃ prāpya nivarttante rogā viṣṇumivāsurāḥ 52
takreṇodaribhiḥ peyo gulmibhirvadarāmbunā
ānaddhavāte surayā vātaroge prasannayā 53
dadhimaṇḍena viḍbhede dāḍimāmbubhirarśasi
parikarttiṣu vṛkṣāmlairuṣṇāmbubhirajīrṇake 54
bhagandare pāṇḍuroge kāse śvāse galagrahe
hṛdro ge grahaṇīroge kubje mande'nale jvare 55
daṃṣṭrāviṣe mūlaviṣe sagare kṛttrime viṣe
yathā'ha snigdhakoṣṭhena peyametadvirecanam 56
snukkṣīradantītriphalāviḍaṅgasiṃhītrivṛccitrakakarṣakarṣam
ghṛtaṃ vipakvaṃ kuḍavapramāṇaṃ toyena tasyākṣamathārddhakarṣam 57
pītvoṣṇamambho'nupibedvireke peyāṃ rasaṃ vā prapibedvidhijñaḥ
nārācametajjaṭharāmayānāṃ yuktyopayuktaṃ pravadanti santaḥ 58
vajrāṇḍyāḥ karṣamātrāyāḥ kalkaṃ dadhyādiveṣṭitam
nigiledvāriṇā nityamudaravyādhiśāntaye 59
punarnavā dāruniśā satiktā paṭolapathyāpicumandamustāḥ
sanāgarācchinnaruheti sarvaiḥ kṛtaḥ kaṣāyo vidhinā vidhijñaiḥ 60
gomūtrayugguggulunā ca yuktaḥ pītaḥ prabhāte niyataṃ narāṇām
sarvāṅgaśothodarakāsaśūlaśvāsānvitaṃ pāṇḍugadaṃ nihanti 61
ityekacattvāriṃśattama udarogādhikāraḥ samāptaḥ 41
<2.3.13>
atha dvicatvāriṃśattamaḥ śothādhikāraḥ 42
śuddhymāyābhaktakṛśābalānāṃ kṣārāmlatīkṣṇoṣṇagurūpasevā
dadhyāmamṛcchākavirodhipiṣṭagaroṣasṛṣṭānnaniṣevaṇācca 1
arśāṃsyaceṣṭā vapuṣo hyaśuddhirmarmābhighātī viṣamā prasūtiḥ
mithyopacāraḥ pratikarmaṇāñca nijasya hetuḥ śvayathoḥ pradiṣṭaḥ 2
raktapittakaphānvāyurduṣṭo duṣṭānbahiḥ śirāḥ
nītvā ruddhagatistairhi kuryāttvaṅmāṃsasaṃśrayam
utsedhaṃ saṃhataṃ śothaṃ tamāhurnicayādataḥ 3
sagauravaṃ syādanavasthitatvaṃ sotsedhamūṣmā'tha śirātanutvam
salomaharṣañca vivarṇatāṃ ca sāmānyaliṅgaṃ śvayathoḥ pradiṣṭam 4
carastanutvakparuṣo'ruṇo'sitaḥ prasuptiharṣārttiyuto'nimittataḥ
praśāmyati pronnamati prapīḍito divābalī syācchvayathuḥ samīraṇāt 5
mṛduḥ sagandhā'sitapītarāgavān bhramajvarasvedatṛṣāmadānvitaḥ
yastūṣyate sparśarugakṣirāgavānsa pittaśotho bhṛśadāhapākavān 6
guruḥ sthiraḥ pāṇḍurarocakānvitaḥ prasekanidrā vamivahnimāndyakṛt
sakṛcchrajanmapraśamo nipīḍito naconnamedrā tribalī kaphātmakaḥ 7
nidānākṛtisaṃsargājjñeyaḥ śotho dvidoṣajaḥ 8
sarvākṛtiḥ sannipātācchotho vyāmiśralakṣaṇaḥ 9
abhighātena śastrādicchedabhedakṣatādibhiḥ
himānilodadhyanilairbhallātakapikacchujaiḥ 10
rasaiḥ śūkaiśca saṃsparśācchvayathuḥ syādvisarpavān
bhṛśoṣmā lohitābhāsaḥ prāyaśaḥ pittalakṣaṇaḥ 11
viṣajaḥ saviṣaprāṇiparisarpaṇamūtraṇāt
daṃṣṭrādantanakhāghātādaviṣaprāṇināmapi 12
viṇmūtraśukropahatamalavadvastusaṅkarāt
viṣavṛkṣānilasparśādgarayogāvacūrṇanāt
mṛduścalo'valambī ca śīghro dāharujākaraḥ 13
doṣāḥ śvayathumūrdhvaṃ hi kurvantyāmāśaye sthitāḥ
pittāśayasthā madhye tu varcaḥ sthānagatāstvadhaḥ
kṛtsnaṃ dehamanuprāpya kuryuḥ sarvasarantathā 14
chardiḥ śvāso'rucistṛṣṇā jvaro'tīsāra eva ca
saptako'ya sadaurbalyaḥ śothasyaite upadra vāḥ 15
śvāsaḥ pipāsā chardiśca daurbalyaṃ jvara eva ca
yasya cānne rucirnāsti śothinaṃ taṃ vivarjayet 16
yo madhyadeśe śvayathuḥ kaṣṭaḥ sarvāṅgagaśca yaḥ
arddhāṅge'riṣṭabhūtaḥ syādyaścordhvaṃ parisarpati 17
ananyopadra vakṛtaḥ śothaḥ pādasamutthitaḥ
puruṣaṃ hanti nārīntu mukhajo bastijo dvayam 18
śuṇṭhīpunarnavairaṇḍapañcamūlīśṛtaṃ jalam
vātike śvayathau śastaṃ pānāhāraparigrahe 19
paṭolatriphalā'riṣṭadārvīkvāthaḥ sagugguluḥ
tadvatpittakṛtaṃ śothaṃ hanti śleṣmodbhavaṃ tathā 20
miśre miśrakramaṃ kuryātsarvaje sarvameva hi
bilvapatrarasaṃ pūtaṃ soṣaṇaṃ tribhave pibet 21
śothe tvāgantuje kuryātsekalepādi śītalam
bhallātakyā harecchothaṃ satilā kṛṣṇamṛttikā 22
mahiṣīkṣīrasaṃpiṣṭairnavanītasamanvitaiḥ
tilairliptaḥ śamaṃ yāti śotho bhallātakotthitaḥ 23
yaṣṭīdugdhatilairlepo navanītena saṃyutaḥ
śothamāruṣkaraṃ hanti cūrṇaiḥ śāladalasya ca 24
mahiṣyā navanītaṃ vā lepād dugdhatilānvitam 25
pathyāniśābhārgyamṛtā'gnidārvīpunarnavādārumahauṣadhānām
kvāthaḥ prasahyodarapāṇipādamukhāśritaṃ hantyacireṇa śotham 26
phalatrikodbhavaṃ kvāthaṃ gomūtreṇaiva sādhitam
vātaśleṣmodbhavaṃ śothaṃ hanyād vṛṣaṇasambhavam 27
bṛścīradevadrumanāgarairvā dantītrivṛttr yūṣaṇacitrakairvā
dugdhaṃ susiddhaṃ vidhinā nipītaṃ gītaṃ paraṃ śothaharaṃ bhiṣagbhiḥ 28
sekastathārkavarṣābhūnimbakvāthena śothahṛt
gomūtreṇāpi kurvīta sukhoṣṇenāvasecanam 29
punarnavā dāru śuṇṭhī śigruḥ siddhārthakastathā
amlapiṣṭaḥ sukhoṣṇo'ya pralepaḥ sarvaśothahṛt 30
guḍārdra kaṃ vā guḍanāgaraṃ vā guḍābhayāṃ vā guḍapippaladyaṃ vā
karṣābhivṛddhyā tripalapramāṇaṃ khādennaraḥ pakṣamathāpi māsam 31
śothapratiśyāyagalāsyarogānsaśvāsakāsārucipīnasādīn
jīrṇajvarārśograhaṇīvikārānhanyāttathānyānkaphavātarogān 32
viśvaṃ guḍena tulyaṃ bṛścīrarasānupānamabhyastam
vinihanti sarvaśothaṃ ghanavṛndaṃ caṇḍavāyuriva 33
kaṇānāgarajaṃ cūrṇaṃ saguḍaṃ śothanāśanam
āmājīrṇapraśamanaṃ śūlaghnaṃ bastiśodhanam 34
guḍātpalatrayaṃ grāhyaṃ śṛṅgaberapalatrayam
śṛṅgaberasamā kṛṣṇā lohaviṭtilayoḥ palam
cūrṇametatsamuddiṣṭaṃ sarvaśvayathunāśanam 35
māṇakakvāthakalkābhyāṃ ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet
ekajaṃ dvandvajaṃ śothaṃ tridoṣañca vyapohati 36
śuṣkamūlakavarṣā bhūdārurāsnāmahauṣadhaiḥ
pakvamabhyañjanaṃ tailaṃ saśūlaṃ śvayathuṃ haret 37
iti dvicatvāriṃśattamaḥ śothādhikāraḥ samāptaḥ 42
<2.3.14>
atha tricatvāriṃśattamo vṛddhibradhnādhikāraḥ 43
doṣāsramedomūtrāntraiḥ sa vṛddhiḥ saptadhā gadaḥ
mūtrāntrajāvapyanilāddhetubhedastu kevalaḥ 1
vṛddhiṃ karoti koṣasyaphalakoṣābhivāhinīḥ
ruddhvā ruddhagatirvāyurdhamanīrmuṣkagāminīḥ 2
vātapūrṇadṛtisparśo rukṣo vātādaheturuk 3
pakvodumbarasaṅkāśaḥpittād dāhoṣmapākavān 4
kaphācchīto guruḥ snigdhaḥ kaṇḍūmānkaṭhino'lparuk 5
kṛṣṇasphoṭāvṛtaḥ pittavṛddhiliṅgaśca raktajaḥ 6
kaphavanmedaso vṛddhirmṛdustālaphalopamaḥ 7
mūtradhāraṇaśīlasya mūtrajaḥ sa tu gacchataḥ
ambhobhiḥ pūrṇadṛtivatkṣobhaṃ yāti saruṅ mṛduḥ
mūtrakṛcchramadhaḥ kuryātsañcalaṃ phalakoṣayoḥ 8
vātakopibhirāhāraiḥ śītatoyāvagāhanaiḥ
dhāraṇeraṇabhārādhva viṣamāṅgapravarttanaiḥ
kṣobhaṇaiḥ kṣobhito'nyaiśca kṣudrā ntrāvayavaṃ yadā
pavano viguṇīkṛtya svaniveśādadho nayet
kuryādvaṅkṣaṇasandhistho granthyābhaṃ śvayathuṃ tadā 9
upekṣyamāṇasya ca muṣkavṛddhimādhmānarukstambhavatīṃ sa vāyuḥ
prapīḍito'nta svanavānprayāti pradhmāpayanneti punaśca muktaḥ 10
yasyāntrāvayavāśleṣo muṣkayorvātasañcayāt
antravṛddhirasādhyo'ya vātavṛddhisamākṛtiḥ 11
atyabhiṣyandigurvanna śuṣkapūtyāmiṣāśanāt
karoti granthivacchothaṃ doṣo vaṅkṣaṇasandhiṣu
jvaraśūlāṅgasādāḍhyaṃ taṃ bradhneti vinirdiśet 12
vṛddhāvatyaśanaṃ mārgamupavāsaṃ gurūṇi ca
vegāghātaṃ pṛṣṭhayānaṃ vyāyāmaṃ maithunaṃ tyajet 13
vātavṛddhau pibet snigdhaṃ yathāprāptaṃ virecanam
sakṣīrañca pibettailaṃ māsameraṇḍasambhavam 14
guggulveraṇḍajaṃ tailaṃ gomūtreṇa pibennaraḥ
vātavṛddhiṃ jayatyāśu cirakālānubandhinīm 15
pittagranthikrameṇaiva pittavṛddhimupācaret
jalaukābhirharedra ktaṃ vṛddhau pittasamudbhave 16
candanaṃ madhukaṃ padmamuśīraṃ nīlamutpalam
kṣīrapiṣṭaṃ pralepena dāhaśotharujā'paham 17
trikaṭutriphalākvāthaṃ sakṣāralavaṇaṃ pibet
virecanamidaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ kaphavṛddhivināśanam 18
lepanāḥ kaṭutīkṣṇoṣṇāḥ svedanaṃ rūkṣameva ca
pariṣekopanāhau ca sarvamuṣṇamiheṣyate 19
muhurmuhurjalaukābhiḥ śoṇitaṃ raktaje haret
pibedvirecanaṃ vā'pi śarkarākṣaudra saṃyutam 20
śītamālepanaṃ śastaṃ sarvapittaharaṃ tathā
pittavṛddhikramaṃ kuryādāme pakve ca raktaje 21
svinnaṃ medaḥ samutthantu lepayetsurasā'dinā
śirovirecanadra vyaiḥ sukhoṣṇairmūtrasaṃyutaiḥ 22
saṃsvedya mūtraprabhavaṃ vastrapaṭaṭena veṣṭayet
sīvanyāḥ pārśvato'dhastādvidhyed brīhimukhena vai 23
muṣkakoṣamagacchantyā mantravṛddhau vicakṣaṇaḥ
vātavṛddhikramaṃ kuryātsvedaṃ tatrāgninā hitam 24
tailameraṇḍajaṃ pītvā balāsiddhaṃ yathocitam
ādhmānaśūlopacitāmantravṛddhiṃ jayanneraḥ 25
rāsnāyaṣṭyamṛtairaṇḍabalā'ragvadhagokṣuraiḥ
paṭolena vṛṣeṇāpi vidhinā vihitaṃ śṛtam
ruvutailena saṃyuktamantravṛddhiṃ vyapohati 26
gandharvahasta tailena kṣīreṇa vihitaṃ śṛtam
viśālāmūlajaṃ cūrṇaṃ vṛddhiṃ hanti na saṃśayaḥ 27
vacāsarṣapakalkena pralepaḥ śothanāśanaḥ
śigrutvaksarṣapairlepaḥ śothaśleṣmānilāpahaḥ 28
śuddhasūtaṃ tathā gandhaṃ mṛtānyetāniyojayet
lohaṃ vaṅgaṃ tathā tāmraṃ kāṃsyañcātha viśodhitam 29
tālakaṃ tutthakañcāpi tathā śaṅkhavarāṭakam
trikaṭu triphalāṃ cavyaṃ viḍaṅgaṃ vṛddhadārakam 30
karcūraṃ māgadhīmūlaṃ pāṭhāṃ sahavuṣāṃ vacām
elābījaṃ devakāṣṭhaṃ tathā lavaṇapañcakam 31
etāni samabhāgāni cūrṇayedatha kārayet
kaṣāyeṇa harītakyā vaṭikāṃ ṭaṅkasammitām 32
ekāṃ tāṃ vaṭikāṃ yastu nigiledvāriṇā saha
aṇḍavṛddhirasādhyā'pi tathyaṃ naśyati satvaram 33
bhṛṣṭaścairaṇḍatailena samyakkalko'bhayābhavaḥ
kṛṣṇāsaindhavasaṃyukto bradhnarogaharaḥ paraḥ 34
ajājī havuṣā kuṣṭhaṃ gomedaṃ badarānvitam
kāñjikena tu sampiṣṭaṃ tallepo bradhnajitparaḥ 35
iti tricatvāriṃśattamo vṛddhibradhnādhikāraḥ samāptaḥ 43
<2.3.15>
atha catuścatvāriṃśattamo galagaṇḍagaṇḍamālā
granthyarbudādhikāraḥ 44
nibaddhaḥ śvayathuryasya muṣkavallambate gale
mahānvā yadivā hrasvo galagaṇḍaṃ tamādiśet 1
vātaḥ kaphaścāpi gale praduṣṭau manye tu saṃśritya tathaiva medaḥ
kurvanti gaṇḍaṃ kramataḥ svaliṅgaiḥ samācitaṃ taṃ galagaṇḍamāhuḥ 2
todānvitaḥ kṛṣṇaśirā'vanaddhaḥ śyāvāruṇo vā pavanātmakastu
pāruṣyayuktaściravṛddhypāko yadṛcchayā pākamiyātkadācit
vairasyamāsyasya ca tasya jantorbhavettathā tālugalapraśoṣaḥ 3
sthiraḥ savarṇo gururugrakaṇḍūḥ śīto mahāṃścāpi kaphātmakastu
cirācca vṛddhiṃ bhajate cirādvā prapacyate mandarujaḥ kadācit 4
mādhuryamāsyasya ca tasya jantorbhavettathā tālugalapralepaḥ 5
snigdho mṛduḥ pāṇḍuraniṣṭagandho medo'nvitaḥ kaṇḍuyuto'rujaśca
pralambate'lābuvadalpamūlo dehānurūpakṣayavṛddhiyuktaḥ
snigdhāsyatā tasya bhavecca jantorgale'ṇuśabdaṃ kurute ca nityam 6
kṛcchrācchvasantaṃ mṛdu sarvagātraṃ saṃvatsarātītamarocakārttam
kṣīṇañca vaidyo galagaṇḍayuktaṃ bhinnasvaraṃ naiva naraṃ cikitset 7
karkandhukolāmalakapramāṇaiḥ kakṣāṃsamanyāgalavaṅkṣaṇeṣu
medaḥ kaphābhyāṃ ciramandapākaiḥ syādgaṇḍamālā bahubhiśca gaṇḍaḥ 8
te granthayaḥ kecidavāptapākāḥ sravanti naśyanti bhavanti cānye
kālānubandhaṃ ciramādadhāti saivāpacīti pravadanti kecit 9
sādhyā smṛtā pīnasapārśvaśūlakāsajvaracchardiyutā tvasādhyā 10
vātādayo māṃsamasṛkpraduṣṭāḥ sandūṣya medaśca tathā sirāśca
vṛttonnataṃ vigrathitantu śothaṃ kurvantyato granthiriti pradiṣṭaḥ 11
āyamyate vṛścyati tudyate ca prabhraśyate mathyati bhidyate ca
kṛṣṇo mṛdurvastirivātataśca bhinnaḥ sraveccānilajo'sramaccham 12
dandahmate dhūpyati cūṣyate ca pāpacyate prajvalatīva cāpi
raktaḥ sapīto'pyathavā'pi pittādbhinnaḥsraved duṣṭamatīva cāsram 13
śīto'vivarṇo'lparujo'tikaṇḍūḥ pāṣāṇavatsaṃhananopapannaḥ
cirābhivṛddhiśca kaphaprakopādbhinnaḥ sravecchuklaghanañca pūyam 14
śarīravṛddhikṣayavṛddhihāniḥ snigdho mahān kaṇḍuyuto'rujaśca
medaḥ kṛto gacchati cātra bhinno piṇyākasarpiḥpratimantu medaḥ 15
vyāyāmajātairabalasya taistairākṣipya vāyuśca śirāpratānam
saṅkocya sampīḍya viśoṣya cāpi granthiṃ karotyunnatamāśu vṛttam 16
granthiḥ śirājaḥ sa tu kṛcchrasādhyo bhavedyadi syātsarujaścalaśca
aruksa evāpyacalo mahāṃśca marmotthitaścāpi vivarjanīyaḥ 17
gātrapradeśe kvacideva doṣāḥ sammūrcchitā māṃsamasṛkpradūṣya
vṛttaṃ sthiraṃ mandarujaṃ mahāntamanalpamūlaṃ ciravṛddhypākam
kurvanti māṃsocchrayamatyagādhaṃ tadarbudaṃ śāstravido vadanti 18
vātena pittena kaphena cāpi raktena māṃsena ca medasā ca
tajjāyate tasya ca lakṣaṇāni grantheḥ samānāni sadā bhavanti 19
doṣaḥ praduṣṭo rudhiraṃ sirāśca saṅkocya sampīḍya tatastvapākam
sāsrāvamunnahyati māṃsapiṇḍaṃ māsāṅkurairāvṛtamāśu vṛddhim 20
sravatyajasraṃ rudhiraṃ praduṣṭamasādhyametadrudhirātmakaṃ tu
raktakṣayopadra vapīḍitatvātpāṇḍurbhavedarbudapīḍitastu 21
muṣṭiprahārādibhirardite'ṅge māṃsaṃ praduṣṭaṃ samupaiti śotham
avedanaṃ snigdhamananyavarṇamapākamaśmopamamapracālyam 22
praduṣṭamāṃsasya narasya gāḍhametadbhavenmāṃsaparāyaṇasya 23
yajjāyate'nyātkhalu pūrvajāte jñeyaṃ tadadhyarbudamarbudajñaiḥ
yad dvandvajātaṃ yugapatkramādvā dvirarbudaṃ tacca bhavedasādhyam 24
māṃsārbudaṃ tvetadasādhyamāhuḥ sādhyeṣvapīmāni vivarjayecca
samprasrutaṃ marmasu yacca jātaṃ srotaḥsu vā yattu bhavedacālyam 25
na pākamāyānti kaphādhikatvānmedobahutvācca viśeṣatastu
doṣasthiratvād grathanācca teṣāṃ sarvārbudānyeva nisargatastu 26
sarṣapāñchigrubījāni śaṇabījātasīyavān
mūlakasya ca bījāni takreṇāmlena peṣayet 27
galagaṇḍagaṇḍamālāgranthayaścaiva dāruṇāḥ
pralepādeva naśyanti vilayaṃ yānti satvaram 28
rakṣoghnatailayuktena jalakumbhīkabhasmanā
lepanaṃ galagaṇḍasya cirotthasyāpi śasyate 29
śvetāparājitāmūlaṃ prātaḥ piṣṭvā pibennaraḥ
sarpiṣā niyatāhāro galagaṇḍapraśāntaye 30
tiktālābuphale pakve saptāhamuṣitaṃ jalam
sadyaḥ syādgalagaṇḍaghnaṃ pānāt pathyānnasevinām 31
tailaṃ pibedvā'mṛtavallinimbahiṃsrā'bhayāvṛkṣakapippalībhiḥ
siddhaṃ balābhyāṃ saha devadāruṇā hitāya nityaṃ galagaṇḍarogī 32
yavamudgapaṭolādikaṭu rūkṣānnabhojanam
vamanaṃ raktamokṣañca galagaṇḍe prayojayet 33
dāpayetpracchanānyatra gaṇḍagopālikodbhavaḥ
pralepastvanubhūto'ya bahudhā bahubhirjanaiḥ 34
lavaṇaṃ jalakumbhīñca kaṇācūrṇena saṃyutam
prabhāte nityamaśnīyādgalagaṇḍapraśāntaye 35
kāñcanāratvacaḥ kvāthaḥ śuṇṭhīcūrṇena saṃyutaḥ
mākṣikāḍhyaḥ sakṛtpītaḥ kvātho varuṇamūlajaḥ 36
gaṇḍamālāṃ haratyāśu cirakālānubandhinīm 37
palamarddhapalañcāpi piṣṭāṃ taṇḍulavāriṇā
kāñcanāratvacaṃ pītvā gaṇḍamālāṃ vyapohati 38
kāñcanārasya gṛhṇīyāttvacaṃ pañcapalonmitām
nāgarasya kaṇāyāśca maricasya palaṃ palam 39
pathyābibhītadhātrīṇāṃ palamarddhaṃ pṛthakpṛthak
varuṇasyākṣamekaṃ ca patrakailātvacāṃ punaḥ 40
ṭaṅkaṃ ṭaṅkaṃ samādāya sarvāṇyekatra cūrṇayet
yāvaccūrṇamidaṃ sarvaṃ tāvānevātra gugguluḥ 41
saṃkuṭya sarvamekatra piṇḍaṃ kṛtvā vidhārayet
guṭikāḥ śāṇikāḥ kṛtvā prabhāte bhakṣayennaraḥ 42
galagaṇḍaṃ jayatyugramapacīmarbudāni ca
granthīnvraṇāni gulmāṃśca kuṣṭhāni ca bhagandaram 43
pradeyaścānupānārthaṃ kvātho muṇḍitikābhavaḥ
kvāthaḥ khadirasārasya kvāthaḥ koṣṇo'bhayābhavaḥ 44
cakramardakamūlasya palakalke vipācayet
keśarāgarase tailaṃ kaṭukaṃ mṛdunā'gninā 45
pādāṃśikaṃ vinikṣipya sindūramavatārayet
etattailaṃ nihantyāśu gaṇḍamālāṃ sudāruṇām 46
guñjāmūlaphalaistailaṃ vipakvaṃ dbiguṇāmbhasā
haredabhyaṅganasyābhyāṃ gaṇḍamālāṃ sudāruṇām 47
candanaṃ sābhayā lākṣā vacā kaṭukarohiṇī
etaistailaṃ śṛtaṃ pītvā samūlāmapacīṃ haret 48
vyoṣaṃ biḍaṅgaṃ madhukaṃ saindhavaṃ devadāru ca
tailamebhiḥ śṛtaṃ nasyātsakṛcchrāmapacīṃ haret 49
svarjikāmūlakakṣāraḥ śaṅkhacūrṇasamanvitaḥ
etena vihito lepo hanti granthiṃ tathā'budam 50
granthirna yo naśyati bheṣajena niṣkāśya taṃ śastracikitsakena
jātyādipakvena ghṛtena vaidyo vraṇena cānyena ca sañcikitset 51
granthimuddhṛtya tatrāpi vraṇoktaṃ kramamācaret
śirāgranthiṃ vihāyānye śeṣe śastraṃ prayujyate 52
granthyarbudānāṃ na yato viśeṣaḥ pradeśahetvākṛtidoṣadūṣyaiḥ
ataścikitsedbhiṣagarbudāni vidhānavid granthicikitsitena 53
haridrā lodhrapatrāṅgagṛha dhūmamanaḥśilāḥ
madhupragāḍho lepo'ya medo'budaharaḥ paraḥ 54
mūlakasya kṛtaḥ kṣāro haridrā yāstathaiva ca
śaṅkhacūrṇena saṃyukto lepaḥ siddho'budāpahaḥ 55
vaṭadugdhakuṣṭharomakaliptaṃ baddhaṃ vaṭasya patreṇa
adhyasthi saptarātrānmahadapyupaśāntimarbudaṃ gacchet 56
śigrumūlakayorbījaṃ rakṣoghnaṃ surasā yavam
takreṇāśvaripuṃ piṣṭvā limpedarbudaśāntaye 57
iti catuścatvāriṃśattamo galagaṇḍagaṇḍamālāgranthyarbudādhikāraḥ samāptaḥ 44
<2.3.16>
atha pañcacatvāriṃśattamaḥ ślīpadādhikāraḥ 45
purāṇodakabhūyiṣṭhāḥ sarvartuṣu ca śītalāḥ
ye deśāsteṣu jāyante ślīpadāni viśeṣataḥ 1
yaḥ sajvaro baṅkṣaṇajo bhṛśārttiḥ śotho nṛṇāṃ pādagataḥ krameṇa
tacchlīpadaṃ syātkarakarṇanetraśiśnauṣṭanāsāsvapi kecidāhuḥ 2
vātajaṃ kṛṣṇarūkṣaṃ hi sphuṭitaṃ tīvravedanam
animittarujañcāsya bahuśo jvara eva ca 3
pittajaṃ pītasaṃkāśaṃ dāhajvarayutaṃ bhṛśam
ślaiṣmikantubhavetsnigdhaṃ śvetaṃ pāṇḍu guru sthiram 4
trīṇyapyetāni jānīyācchlīpadāni kaphocchrayāt
gurutvañca mahatvañca yasmānnāsti vinā kaphāt 5
valmīkamiva sañjātaṃ kaṇṭakairupacīyate
abdātmakaṃ mahattattu varjanīyaṃ viśeṣataḥ 6
yacchlaiṣmikāhāravihārajātairjātaṃ tathā bhūrikaphasya puṃsaḥ
sāsrāvamatyunnatasarvaliṅgaṃ sakaṇḍukaṃ cāpi vivarjanīyam 7
laṅghanālepanasvedarecanai raktamokṣaṇaiḥ
prāyaḥ śleṣmaharairuṣṇaiḥ ślīpadaṃ samupācaret 8
siddhārthaśobhāñjanadevadāruviśvauṣadhairmūtrayutaiḥ pralimpet
punarnavānāgarasarṣapāṇāṃ kalkena vā kāñjikamiśritena 9
dhattūrairaṇḍanirguṇḍī varṣābhūśigrusarṣapaiḥ
pralepaḥ ślīpadaṃ hanti cirotthamapi dāruṇam 10
asādhyamapi yātyastaṃ ślīpadaṃ cirakālajam
mūlena sahadevāyāstālamiśreṇa lepanāt 11
saptatāmbūlapatrāṇāṃ kalkaṃ taptena vāriṇā
saṃsṛṣṭaṃ lavaṇopetaṃ sevitaṃ ślīpadaṃ haret 12
śākhoṭavalkalakvāthaṃ gomūtreṇa yutaṃ pibet
ślīpadānāṃ vināśāya medodoṣanivṛttaye 13
rajanīṃ guḍasaṃyuktāṃ gomūtreṇa pibennaraḥ
varṣābhūtriphalācūrṇaṃ pippalyā saha yojitam
sakṣaudraṃ ślīpade lihyāccirotthaṃ ślīpadaṃ jayet
gandharvatailasiddhāṃ harītakīṃ go'mbunā pibennityam
ślīpadabandhanamukto bhavatyasau saptarātreṇa 15
iti pañcacatvāriṃśattamaḥ ślīpadādhikāraḥ samāptaḥ 45
<2.3.17>
atha ṣaṭcatvāriṃśattamo vidra dhyadhikāraḥ 46
tvagraktamāṃsamedāṃsi pradūṣyāsthisamāśritāḥ
doṣāḥ śothaṃ śanairghoraṃ janayantyucchritā bhṛśam 1
mahāmūlaṃ rujāvantaṃ vṛttaṃ vā'pyatha vā'yatam
sa vidra dhiriti khyāto vijñeyaḥ ṣaḍvidhaśca saḥ 2
vidra dhiṣaḍvidhatvaṃ vivṛṇoti
pṛthag doṣaiḥ samastaiśca kṣatenāpyasṛjā tathā
ṣaṇṇāmapi hi teṣāṃ tu lakṣaṇaṃ sampracakṣyate 3
kṛṣṇo'ruṇo vā viṣamo bhṛśamatyarthavedanaḥ
cirotthānaprapākaśca vidra dhirvātasambhavaḥ 4
pakvodumbarasaṃkāśaḥśyāvo vā jvaradāhavān
kṣiprotthānaprapākaśca vidra dhiḥ pittasambhavaḥ 5
śarāvasadṛśaḥ pāṇḍuḥ śītaḥ snigdho'lpavedanaḥ
cirotthānaprapākaśca vidra dhiḥ kaphasambhavaḥ
tanupītasitāścaiṣāmāsrāvāḥ kramato matāḥ 6
nānāvarṇarujāsrāvī ghāṭālo viṣamo mahān
viṣamaṃ pacyate vā'pi vidra dhiḥ sānnipātikaḥ 7
taistairbhāvairabhihate kṣatevā'pathyakāriṇām
kṣatoṣmā vāyuvisṛtaḥ saraktaṃ pittamīrayet 8
jvarastṛṣā ca dāhaśca jāyate tasya dehinaḥ
āganturvidra dhirhyeṣa pittavidra dhilakṣaṇaḥ 9
kṛṣṇasphoṭāvṛtaḥ śyāvastīvradāharujājvaraḥ
pittavidra dhiliṅgastu raktavidra dhirucyate 10
ābhyantarānatastūrdhvaṃ vidra dhīn paricakṣate
gurvasātmyaviruddhānnaśuṣkaśākāmlabhojanaiḥ 11
ativyavāyavyāyāmavegāghātavidāhibhiḥ
pṛthak sambhūya vā doṣāḥ kupitā gulmarūpiṇam 12
valmīkavatsamunnaddhamantaḥ kurvanti vidra dhim
gude vastimukhe nābhyāṃ kukṣau vaṅkṣaṇayostathā 13
vṛkkayoḥ plīhni yakṛti hṛdi vā klomni cāpyatha
eṣāṃ liṅgāni jānīyād bāhyavidra dhilakṣaṇaiḥ 14
gude vātanirodhastu vastau kṛcchrālpamūtratā
nābhyāṃ hikkājṛmbhaṇaṃ ca kukṣau mārutakopanam 15
kaṭipṛṣṭhagrahastīvro vaṅkṣaṇotthe tu vidra dhau
vṛkkayoḥ pārśvasaṅkocaḥ plīhnyucchvāsāvarodhanam 16
sarvāṅgapragrahastīvro hṛdi kāsaśca jāyate
śvāso yakṛti hikkā ca klomni pepīyate payaḥ 17
nābheruparijāḥ pakvā yāntyūrdhvamitare tvadhaḥ 18
adhaḥsruteṣu jīvettu sruteṣūrdhvaṃ na jīvati
hṛnnābhivastivarjyeṣu teṣu bhinneṣu vāhyataḥ
jīvet kadācitpuruṣo netareṣu kadācana 19
sādhyā vidra dhayaḥ pañca vivarjyaḥ sānnipātikaḥ
āmapakvavidagdhatvaṃ teṣāṃ jñeyañca śothavat 20
jalaukāpātanaṃ śastaṃ sarvasminneva vidra dhau
reko mṛdurlaṅghanañca svedaḥ pittottaraṃ vinā 21
apakve vidra dhau yuñjyād vraṇaśothavadauṣadham
vātaghnamūlakalkaistu vasātailaghṛtānvitaiḥ 22
sukhoṣṇairbahulairlepaḥ prayojyo vātavidra dhau
yavagodhūmamudgaiśca piṣṭairājyena lepayet 23
vilīyate kṣaṇenaiva hyavipakvastu vidra dhiḥ 24
paittikaṃ vidra dhiṃ vaidyaḥ pradihyātsarpiṣā yutaiḥ
payasyośīramadhukacandanairdugdhapeṣitaiḥ 25
pañcavalkalakalkena ghṛtamiśreṇa lepayet
pibedvā triphalākvāthaṃ trivṛtkalkākṣasaṃyutam 26
iṣṭikā sikatā lohakiṭṭaṃ gośakṛtā saha
mūtraiḥ sukhoṣṇairlepena svedayecchleṣmavidra dhim 27
daśamūlīkaṣāyeṇa sasnehena rasena vā
śothavraṇaṃ vā koṣṇena saśūlaṃ pariṣecayet 28
pittavidra dhivatsarvāḥ kriyā niravaśeṣataḥ
vidra dhyoḥ kuśalaḥ kuryādra ktāgantunimittayoḥ 29
raktacandana mañjiṣṭhāniśā madhukagairikaiḥ
kṣīreṇa vidra dhau lepo raktāgantunimittake 30
pītā hyete nihantyāśu vidra dhiṃ koṣṭhasambhavam
kṛṣṇā'jājī viśālā ca dhāmārgavaphalaṃ tathā 31
śvetavarṣābhuvo mūlaṃ mūlaṃ vā varuṇasya ca
jalena kvathitaṃ pītamantarvidra dhihṛtparam 32
gāyatrītriphalānimbakaṭukāmadhukaṃ samam
trivṛtpaṭolamūlābhyāṃ catvāraoṃ'śā pṛthak pṛthak 33
masūrānnistuṣāndadyādeṣa kvātho vraṇāñcayet
gulmavidra dhibīsarpadāha mohajvarāpahaḥ 34
tṛṇmūrcchācchardihṛdro gapittāsṛkkuṣṭhakāmalāḥ
śigrumūlaṃ jale dhautaṃ piṣṭaṃ vastreṇa gālayet
tadra saṃ madhunā pītvā hantyantarvidra dhiṃ naraḥ 35
śobhāñjanakaniryūhohiṅgusaindhavasaṃyutaḥ
hantyantarvidra dhiṃ śīghraṃ prātaḥ prātarviśeṣataḥ 36
iti ṣaṭcatvāriṃśattamo vidra dhyadhikāraḥ samāptaḥ 46
<2.3.18>
atha saptacatvāriṃśattamo vraṇaśothādhikāraḥ 47
pṛthaksamastadoṣotthā raktajāgantujau tathā
vraṇaśothāḥ ṣaḍete syuḥ saṃyuktāḥ śothalakṣaṇaiḥ 1
viṣamaṃ pacyate vātātpittotthaścācirācciram
kaphajaḥ pittavacchothau raktāgantusamudbhavau 2
mandoṣṇatā'lpaśothatvaṃ kāṭhinyaṃ tvaksavarṇatā
mandavedanatā cāpi śothasyāmasya lakṣaṇam 3
dahyate dahaneneva kṣāreṇeva prapacyate
pipīlikāgaṇeneva daśyate chidyate tathā 4
bhidyate caiva śastreṇa daṇḍeneva ca tāḍyate
pīḍyate pāṇinevāntaḥ sūcībhiriva tudyate 5
soṣacoṣo vivarṇaḥ syādaṅgulyevāvapīḍyate
āsane śayane sthāne śāntiṃ vṛścikaviddhavat 6
na gacchedātataḥ śotho bhavedādhmātavastivat
jvarastṛṣṇā'ruciścaitatpacyamānasya lakṣaṇam 7
vedanopaśamaḥ śotho lohito'lpo na connataḥ
prādurbhāvo valīnāñca todaḥ kaṇḍūrmuhurmuhuḥ 8
upadra vāṇāṃ praśamo nimnatā sphuṭanaṃ tvacām
vastāvivāmbusañcāraḥ syācchothe'ṅagulipīḍite 9
pūyaśca pīḍayedekamantamante ca pīḍite
bubhukṣā vraṇaśothasya bhavetpakvasya lakṣaṇam 10
ṝte'nilādruṅ na vinā na pittaṃ pākaḥ kaphañcāpivinā na pūyaḥ
tasmāddhi sarvaṃ paripākakāle pacanti śothāstribhireva doṣaiḥ 11
kālāntareṇābhyuditantu pittaṃ kṛtvā vaśe vātakaphau prasahya
pacatyataḥ śoṇitameṣa pāko mataḥ pareṣāṃ viduṣāṃ dvitīyaḥ 12
kaphajeṣu ca śotheṣu gambhīraṃ pākametyasṛk
pakvaliṅgaṃ tataḥ spaṣṭaṃ yataḥ syācchothaśītatā
tvaksāvarṇyaṃ rujo'lpatvaṃ ghanasparśatvamaśmavat 13
kakṣaṃ samāsādya yathaiva vahnirvāteritaḥ saṃdahati prasahya
tathaiva pūyo hyaviniḥsṛtastu māṃsaṃ śirāḥ snāyumapīha khādet 14
āmaṃ vidahyamānañca samyakpakvantu yo bhiṣak
jānīyātsa bhavedvaidyaḥ śeṣāstaskaravṛttayaḥ 15
yaśchinattyāmamajñānādyaśca pakvamupekṣate
śvapacāviva vijñeyau tāvaniścitakāriṇau 16
ādau śothaharolepastatastu pariṣecanam
vimlāpanamasṛṅmokṣastataḥ syādupanāhanam 17
pācanaṃ bhedanaṃ paścātpīḍanaṃ śodhanaṃ tathā
ropaṇaṃ varṇakaraṇaṃ vraṇasyaite kramāḥ smṛtāḥ 18
yathā prajvalite veśmanyambhasā pariṣecanam
kṣipraṃ praśamayatyagnimevamālepanaṃ rujaḥ 19
bījapūrajaṭāhiṃsrā devadāru mahauṣadham
rāsnā'gnimanthau lepo'ya vātaśothavināśanaḥ 20
kalkaḥ kāñjikasampiṣṭaḥ snigdho madhukacandanaiḥ
dūrvā ca nalamūlañca padmakāṣṭhañca keśaram
uśīraṃ bālakaṃ padmaṃ lepo'ya pittaśothahā 21
nyagrodhodumbarāśvatthaplakṣavetasakalkalaiḥ
sasarpiṣkaiḥ pradehaḥ syācchothe pittasamudbhave 22
āgantuje raktaje ca lepa eṣo'bhipūjitaḥ 23
ajagandhā'jaśṛṃgī ca mañjiṣṭhā saralastathā
ekaiṣikā'śvagandhā ca lepo'yaśleṣmaśothahā 24
kṛṣṇāpurāṇapiṇyākaṃ śigrutvak sikatā śivā
mūtrapiṣṭaḥ sukhoṣṇo'ya pralepaḥ śleṣmaśothahā 25
na rātrau lepanaṃ dadyāddattañca patitaṃ tathā
na ca paryuṣitaṃ śuṣyamāṇaṃ tannaiva dhārayet 26
tamasā pihito'tyuṣmā romakūpamukhotthitaḥ
vinā lepena niryāti rātrau nālepayedataḥ 27
rātrāvapi pralepastuvidhātavyovicakṣaṇaiḥ
apākiśothe gambhīre raktapittasamudbhave 28
yathā'mbubhiḥ sicyamānaḥśāntimagnirhi gacchati
doṣāgnirevaṃ sahasā pariṣekeṇa śāmyati 29
vātaghnauṣadhaniṣkvāthai stailairmāṃsarasairghṛtaiḥ
uṣṇaiḥ saṃsecayecchothaṃ vātikaṃ kāñjikena ca 30
pittaraktābhighātotthaṃ śothaṃ siñcetsuśītalaiḥ
kṣīrājyamadhukhaṇḍekṣurasaiḥpittaharaiḥśṛtaiḥ 31
kaphaghnauṣadhaniṣkvāthaiḥ śītaistu pariṣecayet
tailakṣārāmbumūtraiśca śothaṃ śleṣmasamudbhavam 32
jātasya kaṭhinasyāsya kāryaṃ vimlāpanaṃ śanaiḥ 33
abhyajya svedayitvā tu veṇunāḍhyā śanaiḥ śanaiḥ
vimardayedbhiṣaṅ mandaṃ talenāṅguṣṭhakena vā 34
vedanopaśamārthāya tathā pākaśamāya ca
acirotpatite śothe śoṇitasrāvaṇaṃ caret 35
ekatastu kriyāḥ sarvā raktamokṣaṇamekataḥ
raktaṃ hi vedanāmūlaṃ taccennāsti na cāsti ruk 36
vivarṇaḥkaṭhinaḥ śyāvo vraṇoyaścālpavedanaḥ
viṣāṇaiśca viśeṣeṇa jalaukābhiḥ padairapi 37
rujāvatāṃ dāruṇānāṃ kaṭhinānāṃ tathaiva ca
śothānāṃ svedanaṃ kāryaṃ ye cāpyevaṃvidhā vraṇāḥ 38
śothayorupanāhañca dadyādāmavidagdhayoḥ
praśāmyatyavidagdhastu vidagdhaḥ pakvatāṃ vrajet 39
daśamūlī balā rāsnā vājigandhā prasāriṇī
mūlaṃ vātaripoḥ sindhurvāripūrṇe ghaṭe kṣipet 40
śobhāñjanaḥ kaṇā cāpi saindhavaṃ viśvabheṣajam
śaṇakārpāsayorbījamatasī ca kulatthikā 41
tilā yavāśca siddhārthaḥ kuṭhero mūlakaṃ misiḥ
yathāprāptairamībhiśca dra vyairamlena saṃyutaiḥ 42
kalkīkṛtaiḥ sukhoṣṇaiśca svedayedvidhivacchanaiḥ
anena praśamaṃ yāti vātaśotho na saṃśayaḥ 43
punarnavā dāru śuṇṭhī śigruḥ siddhārtha eva ca
amlapiṣṭaḥ sukhoṣṇo'ya pralepaḥ sarvaśothahā 44
na praśāmyati yaḥ śothaḥ pralepādividhānataḥ
dra vyāṇi pācanīyāni dadyāttatropanāhane 45
śaṇamūlakaśigruṇāṃ phalāni tilasarṣapāḥ
atasī śaktavaḥ kiṇvamuṣṇadra vyañca pācanam 46
antaḥ pūyeṣvavaktreṣu tathā cotsaṅgavatsvapi
gatimatsu ca rogeṣu bhedanaṃ samprayujyate 47
roge vyadhena sādhyetu yathādeśaṃ pramāṇataḥ
śastraṃ vidhāya doṣāṃstu srāvayetkathitaṃ yathā 48
bālavṛddhāsahakṣīṇabhīrūṇāṃ yoṣitāmapi
vraṇeṣu marmajāteṣu bhedanaṃ dra vyalepanam 49
cirabilvo'gniko dantī citrako hayamārakaḥ
kapotakākagṛdhrāṇāṃ malalepena bhedanam 50
kṣāradra vyaṃ tathā kṣāro dāraṇaḥ parikīrtitaḥ
hastidanto jale piṣṭo bindumātrapralepitaḥ
atyarthaṃ kaṭhine śothe kathito bhedanaḥ paraḥ 51
dra vyāṇāṃ picchilānāntu tvaṅmūlātiprapīḍanam
yavagodhūmamāṣāṇāṃ cūrṇāni ca samāsataḥ 52
śuṣyamāṇamupekṣeta pralepaṃ pīḍanaṃ prati
na cāpimukhamālimpettathā doṣaḥ prasicyate 53
vraṇasya tu viśuddhasya kvāthaḥ śuddhikaraḥ paraḥ
paṭolanimbapatrotthaḥ sarvatraiva prayujyate 54
vātike daśamūlānāṃ kṣīriṇāṃ paittike vraṇe
āragvadhādeḥ kaphaje kaṣāyaḥ śodhane hitaḥ 55
aśvatthodumbaraplakṣavaṭavetasajaṃ śṛtam
vraṇaśothopadaṃśānāṃ nāśanaṃ kṣālanātsmṛtam 56
tilasaindhavayaṣṭyāhvanimbapatraniśāyugaiḥ
trivṛdghṛtayutaiḥ piṣṭaiḥ pralepo vraṇaśodhanaḥ 57
ekaṃ vā sārivāmūlaṃ sarvavraṇaviśodhanam 58
nimbapatraṃ tilā dantī trivṛtsaindhavamākṣikam
duṣṭavraṇapraśamano lepaḥ śodhanakeśarī 59
lepānnimbadalaiḥ kalko vraṇaśodhanaropaṇaḥ
bhakṣaṇācchardimandāgnipittaśleṣmakṛmīnharet
vraṇānviśodhayedvattaryā sūkṣmān hi sandhimarmajān 60
abhayātrivṛtādantī lāṅgalīmadhusaindhavaiḥ 61
nimbapatraghṛta kṣaudra dārvīmadhukasaṃyutaiḥ
varttistilānāṃ kalko vā śodhayedro payed vraṇam 62
apetapūtimāṃsānāṃ māṃsasthānāmarohatām
kalkastu ropaṇodeyastilajo madhusaṃyutaḥ 63
aśvagandhā ruhā lodhraṃ kaṭphalaṃ madhuyaṣṭikā
samaṅgā dhātakīpuṣpaṃ paramaṃ vraṇaropaṇam 64
madhuyuktā surā puṃsāṃ kathitā vraṇaropaṇī
suṣavīpatradhattūra balāmoṭākuṭherakāḥ
pṛthagetaiḥ pralepena gambhīravraṇaropaṇam 65
kakubhodumbarāśvatthajambū kaphalalodhrajaiḥ
tvakcūrṇairdhūlitāḥ kṣipraṃ saṃrohanti vraṇā dhruvam 66
priyaṅgudhātakīpuṣpaṃ yaṣṭīmadhujatūni ca
sūkṣmacūrṇīkṛtāni syū ropaṇānyavadhūlanāt 67
yavacūrṇaṃ samadhukaṃ satailaṃ saha sarpiṣā
dadyādālepanaṃ koṣṇaṃ dāhaśūlopaśāntaye 68
karañjāriṣṭanirguṇḍīlepo hanyād vraṇakṛmīn
laśunasyāthavā lepo hiṅgunimbakṛto'thavā 69
nimbapatravacāhiṅgusarpirlavaṇasarṣapaiḥ
dhūpanaṃ syād vraṇe rukṣakṛmikaṇḍūrujā'paham 70
ye kledapākasrutigandhavanto vraṇāścirotthāḥ satatāśca śothāḥ
prayānti ye guggulumiśritena pītena śāntiṃ triphalāśṛtena 71
paṭolanimbāsanasāra dhātrīpathyā'kṣaniryūhamaharmukheṣu
pibedyutaṃ guggulunā visarpavisphoṭa duṣṭavraṇaśāntimicchan 72
manaḥśilā samañjiṣṭhā salākṣā rajanīdvayam
pralepaḥ saghṛtakṣaudra stvacaḥ sāvarṇyakṛtsmṛtaḥ 73
jīrṇaśālyodanaṃ snigdhamalpamuṣṇaṃ dra vāntaram
bhuñjāno jāṅgalairmāṃsaiḥ śīghraṃ vraṇamapohati 74
taṇḍulīyakajīvantīvāstūkasuniṣaṇṇakaiḥ
bālamūlakavārttākupaṭolaiḥ kāravellakaiḥ 75
sadāḍimaiḥ sāmalakairghṛtabhṛṣṭaiḥ sasaindhavaiḥ
anyairevaṅguṇairvā'pi mudgādīnāṃ rasena vā 76
amlaṃ dadhi ca śākañca māṃsamānūpamaudakam
kṣīraṃ gurūṇi cānnāni vraṇe ca parivarjayet 77
vraṇe śvayathurāyāsātsa ca rāgaśca jāgarāt
tau ca rukca divāsvāpātte ca mṛtyuśca maithunāt 78
kruddhe sadyovraṇe kuryādūrdhvaṃ cādhaśca śodhanam
kriyā śītā prayoktavyā raktapittoṣmanāśinī 79
laṅghanañca balaṃ jñātvā bhojanaṃ cāsramokṣaṇam
ghṛṣṭe vidalite caiva sutarāmiṣyate vidhiḥ 80
chinne bhinne tathā viddhe kṣate vā'sṛgatisravet
raktakṣayāttatra rujaḥ karoti pavano bhṛśam 81
pariṣekaṃ snehapānaṃ lepaṃ tatropanāhanam
kurvanti snehavastiñca rujāghnaṃ cauṣadhaṃ pṛthak 82
khaḍgādicchinnagātrasya tatkāle pūrito vraṇaḥ
gāṅgerukīmūlarasaiḥ sadyaḥ syādgatavedanaḥ 83
kaṣāyā madhurāḥ śītāḥ kriyāḥ sarvāḥ prayojayet
sadyovraṇānāṃ saptāhātpaścātpūrvoktamācaret 84
āmāśayasthe rudhire vidadhyādvamanaṃ naraḥ
tasminpakvāśayasthe tu prakurvīta virecanam 85
kvātho vaṃśatvagairaṇḍaśvadaṃṣṭrā'śmabhidā kṛtaḥ
hiṅgusaindhavasaṃyuktaḥ koṣṭhasthaṃ srāvayedasṛk 86
yavakolakulatthānāṃ niḥsnehena rasena ca
bhuñjītānnaṃ yavāgūṃ vā pibetsaindhavasaṃyutām 87
jātīnimbapaṭolapatrakaṭukādārvīniśāsārivā
mañjiṣṭhā'bhayasiktha tutthamadhukairnaktāhvabījaṃ samaiḥ
sarpiḥ siddhamanena sūkṣmavadanā marmāśritāḥ srāviṇo
gambhīrāḥ sarujo vraṇāḥ sagatikāḥ śudhyanti rohanti ca 88
vṛddhavaidyopadeśena pāramparyopadeśataḥ
jātīvṛte tu saṃsiddhaṃ kṣeptavyaṃ sikthakaṃ budhaiḥ 89
jātīnimbapaṭolānāṃ naktamālasya pallavāḥ
sikthakaṃ madhukaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ dve niśe kaṭurohiṇī 90
mañjiṣṭhā padmakaṃ pathyā lodhratvaṅnīlamutpalam
sārivā tutthakañcāpi naktamālaphalaṃ tathā
etāni samabhāgāni kalkīkṛtya prayatnataḥ
tilatailaṃ pacetsamyagvaidyaḥ pākavicakṣaṇaḥ 92
viṣavraṇe samutpanne sphoṭake kuṣṭharogiṇī
dadruvīsarparogeṣukīṭadaṣṭeṣu sarvathā 93
sadyaḥ śastraprahāreṇa dagdhaviddheṣu caiva hi
nakhadantakṣate dehe duṣṭamāṃsāpakarṣaṇe 94
mrakṣaṇena hitaṃ tailamidaṃ śodhanaropaṇam
tailaṃ jātyādināmnaitatprasiddhaṃ bhiṣagādṛtam 95
citrakarasonarāmaṭhaśarapuṅkhālāṅgalīkasindūraiḥ
saviṣaistathā sakuṣṭhaiḥ kaṭutailaṃ sādhu sampakvam 96
viparītamallasaṃjñaṃ tailaṃ duṣṭavraṇaṃ tathā nāḍīm
bahubheṣajairasādhyāmapathyabhoktuśca nistudati 97
amṛtāpaṭolamūlatriphalātrikaṭukakṛmighnānām
samabhāgānāṃ cūrṇaṃ sarvasamo guggulorbhāgaḥ 98
prativāsaramekaikāṃ guṭikāṃ khādedihāpi parimāṇam
jetuṃ vraṇavātāsṛggulmodaraśothavātarogāṃśca 99
pluṣṭasyāgniṣu tapanaṃ kāryamuṣṇaṃ tathauṣadham
samyak svinne śarīre tu svinnaṃ bhavati śobhanam 100
prakṛtyā salilaṃ śītaṃ skandayatyatiśoṇitam
tasmātsukhayati hyuṣṇaṃ na tu śītaṃ kadācana 101
śītāmuṣṇāñca durdagdhe kriyāṃ kuryāttataḥ punaḥ
ghṛtalepapradehāṃśca śītānevāsya kārayet 102
samyadagdhe tugākṣīrīplakṣacandanagairikaiḥ
sāmṛtaiḥ sarpiṣā yuktairālepaṃ kārayedbhiṣak
grāmyānūpaudakairmāṃsaiḥ piṣṭairenaṃ pralepayet 103
atidagdhe viśīrṇāni māṃsānyuddhṛtya śītalām
kriyāṃ kuryāttataḥ paścācchālitaṇḍulakaṇḍanaiḥ 104
tindukyāśca kaṣāyairvā ghṛtamiśrairpralepayet
sarveṣāmagnidagdhānāmetadro paṇamuttamam 105
sikthakakardamajīrakamadhupathyāsarvamiśritaṃ lepāt
gavyaṃ ghṛtamapaharati vipākajanitaṃ vraṇaṃ sadyaḥ 106
siddhaṃ kaṣāyakalkābhyāṃ paṭolyāḥ kaṭutailakam
dagdhavraṇarujāsrāvadāhavisphoṭanāśanam 107
vātāsramasrataṃ duṣṭaṃ saśothaṃ grathitaṃ vraṇam
kuryātsadāhaṃkaṇḍvādyaṃ vraṇagranthistu sa smṛtaḥ 108
kampillakaṃ viḍaṅgāni tvacaṃ dārvyāstathaiva ca
piṣṭvā tailaṃ pacettattu vraṇagranthiharaṃ param 109
iti saptacatvāriṃśattamo vraṇaśothādhikāro vā vraṇaśothā
gantuvraṇāgnidagdhavraṇādhikāraḥ samāptaḥ 47
<2.3.19>
athāṣṭacatvāriṃśattamo bhagnādhikāraḥ 48
bhagnaṃ samāsād dvividhaṃ hutāśa kāṇḍe ca sandhāvapi tatra sandhau
utpiṣṭaviśliṣṭavivarttitāni tiryaggataṃ kṣiptamadhaśca bhagnam 1
prasāraṇākuñcanavarttanogrā ruk sparśavidveṣaṇametaduktam
sāmānyataḥ sandhigatasya liṅgamutpiṣṭasandheḥ śvayathoḥ samantāt
viśeṣato rātribhavā rujā ca viśliṣṭaketau ca rujā ca nityam 2
vivarttite pārśvarujaśca tīvrāstiryaggate tīvrarujo bhavanti
kṣipte'tiśūlaṃ vigamaṃ rujaśca kṣipte tvadhorugvighaṭaśca sandheḥ 3
bhagnantu kāṇḍe bahudhā prayāti viśeṣato nāmabhireva tulyam 4
kāṇḍe tvataḥ karkaṭakāśvakarṇau vicūrṇitaṃ piccitamasthikhallitam
kāṇḍeṣu bhagnaṃ hyatipātitañca majjāgataṃ visphuṭitañca vakram 5
chinnaṃ dvidhā dvādaśadhā'pi kāṇḍe sāmānyamagre kila tasya liṅgam 6
srastāṅgatā śotharujā'tivṛddhistathā vyathāvṛddhiratīva nityam
sampīḍyamāne bhavatīha śabdaḥ sparśāsahaṃ spandanatodaśūlāḥ
sarvāsvavasthāsu na śarmalābho bhagnasya kāṇḍe khalu cihnametat 7
alpāśino'nātmavato jantorvātātmakasya ca
upadra vairvā juṣṭasya bhagnaṃ kṛcchreṇa sidhyati 8
bhinnaṃ kapālaṃ kaṭyāntu sandhimuktaṃ tathā cyutam
jaghanaṃ pratipiṣṭañca varjayettu cikitsakaḥ 9
asaṃśliṣṭakapālañca lalāṭe cūrṇitañca yat
bhagnaṃ stane gude pṛṣṭhe śaṅkhe mūrddhani varjayet 10
samyaksaṃhitamapyasthi durnyāsād duṣṭabandhanāt
saṅkṣobhādvā'pi yadgacchedvikriyāṃ tacca varjayet 11
taruṇāsthīni namyante bhidyante nalakāni tu
kapālāni vibhajyante sphuṭanti rucakāni ca 12
pāṇyoḥ pārśvayuge pṛṣṭhe vakṣojaṭharapāyuṣu
pādayorapi cāsthīni valayāni babhāṣire 13
ādau bhagnaṃ viditvā tu secayecchītalāmbunā
paṅkenālepanaṃ kāryaṃ bandhanañca kuśā'nvitam 14
avanāmitamunnahyedunnataṃ cāvapīḍayet
āñjedatikṣiptamadho gataṃ coparivarttayet 15
madhūkodumbarāśvatthakadambaniculatvacaḥ
vaṃśasarjārjunānāñca kuśārthamupasaṃharet 16
paṭasyopari badhnīyānna gāḍhaṃ śithilaṃ na ca
saptasaptadinācchīte gharme muñcettr yahāttr yahāt
māsānte pañcapañcāhādbhagnadoṣavaśena vā 17
ālepanārthaṃ mañjiṣṭhā madhūkaṃ cāmbupeṣitam
śatadhautaghṛtonmiśraṃ śālipiṣṭañca lepanam 18
sadyo'bhighātajanitā āgantuśvayathavaḥ praśāmyanti
piṣṭakalavaṇālepādamlīkāphalarasābhyāṃ vā 19
āmrātakajaṭā'mlīkāphalaṃ patrāṇi śigrujam 20
mūlaṃ paunarnavaṃ varddhamānasyāpi ca kembukāt
sarvaṃ saṃkṣudya takreṇa kāñjikena tathaiva ca 21
pācayitvā carecchreṣṭhaṃ tena pīḍā praṇaśyati
śothaścāsthi ca śīghreṇa sandhānaṃ yāti vai dhruvam 22
nyagrodhādikaṣāyantu suśītaṃ pariṣecane
pañcamūlīkaṣāyaṃ sakṣīraṃ dadyātsavedane 23
sukhoṣṇamavacāryaṃ vā cakratailaṃ vijānatā
avidāhibhirannaiśca piṣṭakaiḥ samupācaret 24
glānirhi nihatā tasya sandhiviśleṣakārikā
māṃsaṃ māṃsarasaḥ kṣīraṃ sarpiryūṣaḥsatīnajaḥ
bṛhaṇañcānnapānañca deyaṃ bhagnāya jānatā 25
gṛṣṭikṣīraṃ sasarpiṣkaṃ madhurauṣadhasādhitam
śītalaṃ lākṣayā yuktaṃ prātarbhagnaḥ pibennaraḥ 26
saghṛtenāsthisaṃhāraṃ lākṣāgodhūmamarjunam
sandhimukte'sthisambhagne pibetkṣīreṇa vā punaḥ 27
rasona madhulākṣā'jyasitākalkaṃ samaśnatām
chinnabhinnacyutāsthīnāṃ sandhānamacirādbhavet 28
cūrṇaṃ pureṇa saṃyojya ghṛtenārjunalākṣayoḥ
bhagnaḥsandhānamāyāti līḍhaṃ kṣīraghṛtāśinā 29
mūlaṃ śṛgālavinnāyāḥ pītvā māṃsarasena tu
cūrṇīkṛtya trisaptāhādasthibhagnamapohati 30
ābhācūrṇaṃ madhuyutamasthibhagnastryahaṃ pibet
pīte cāsthi bhavetsamyagvajrasāranibhaṃ dṛḍham 31
amlīkāphalakalkaiḥ sauvīraistailamiśritaiḥ svedāt
bhagnābhihatarujāghnairatha vauṣadhasādhitaṃ śvayathau 32
ābhāphalatrikavyoṣaiḥ sarvairetaiḥ samāṃśakaiḥ
tulyaṃ guggulunā yojyaṃ bhagnasandhiprasādhanam 33
lākṣā'sthisaṃhṛtkakubho'śvagandhā cūrṇīkṛtā nāgabalā puraśca
sambhagnamuktāsthirujaṃ nihanyādaṅgāni kuryātkuliśopamāni 34
rātrau rātrau tilānkṛṣṇānvāsayedasthire jale
divā divā śoṣayitvā gavāṃ kṣīreṇa bhāvayet 35
tṛtīyaṃ saptarātrantu bhāvayenmadhukāmbunā
tataḥ kṣīraṃ punaḥ pītāñchuṣkānsūkṣmānvicūrṇayet 36
kākolyādi sayaṣṭyāhvāṃ mañjiṣṭhāṃ sārivāṃ tathā 37
kuṣṭhaṃ sarjarasaṃ māṃsīṃ suradāru sacandanam
śatapuṣpāñca sañcūrṇya tilacūrṇena yojayet 38
pīḍanārthaṃ tu karttavyaṃ sarvagandhaiḥ śṛtaṃ payaḥ
caturguṇena payasā tattailaṃ pācayetpunaḥ 39
yaṣṭīmaṃśumatīmpatraṃ jīvantīṃ turagaṃ tathā
rodhraṃ prapauṇḍarīkañca tathā kālānusārivām 40
śaileyakaṃ kṣīraśuklāmanantāṃ samadhūlikām
piṣṭvā śṛṅgāṭakañcaiva prāguktānyauṣadhāni ca 41
ebhistadvipacettailaṃ śāstravinmṛdunā'gni
etattailaṃ sadā pathyaṃ bhagnānāṃ sarvakarmasu 42
ākṣepake pakṣaghāte tāluśoṣe tathā'dite
manyāstambhe śiroroge karṇaśūle hanugrahe 43
bādhirye timire caiva ye ca strīṣu kṣayaṅgatāḥ
pathyaṃ pāne tathā'bhyaṅge nasye vastiṣu bhojane 44
grīvāskandhorasāṃ vṛddhiretenaiva prajāyate
mukhañca padmapratimaṃ sasugandhisamīraṇam 45
rājārhametatkarttavyaṃ rājñāmeva cikitsakaiḥ
tilacūrṇasamaṃ tatra militaṃ cūrṇamiṣyate 46
pūrve vayasi jātaṃ hi bhagnaṃ sukaramādiśet
alpadoṣasya jantośca kāle tu samaśītale 47
prathame vayasi tvevaṃ māsātsandhiḥ sthiro bhavet
madhyame dviguṇātkālādantime triguṇāttathā 48
naiti pākaṃ yathā bhagnaṃ tathā yatnena rakṣayet
pakvamāṃsaśirāsnāyu taddhi kṛcchreṇa sidhyati 49
patanādabhighātādvā śūnabhaṅgaṃ yadakṣatam
śītānsekānpradehāṃśca bhiṣaktasyāvacārayet 50
savraṇasya tu bhagnasya vraṇaṃ sarpirmadhūttaraiḥ
pratisārya kaṣāyaiśca śeṣaṃ bhagnavadācaret
vātavyādhivinirdiṣṭānsnehāṃstatrāpi yojayet 51
lavaṇaṃ kaṭukakṣāramamlamāyāsamaithunam
vyāyāmañca na seveta bhagno rukṣānnameva ca 52
bhagnasandhimanāviddhamahīnāṅgamanulvaṇam
śubhaceṣṭāpracārañca samyaksandhitamādiśet 53
iti śrī miśralaṭakanatanayaśrīmanmiśrabhāvaviracite bhāvaprakāśe
madhyakhaṇḍe'ṣṭacatvāriṃśattamo bhagnādhikāraḥ samāptaḥ 48
tṛtīyobhāge'pi samāptaḥ
<2.4>
<2.4.1>
caturtho bhāgaḥ
athaikonapañcāśattamo nāḍīvraṇādhikāraḥ 49
yaḥ śothamāmamiti pakvamupekṣate'jño yo vā vraṇaṃ pracurapūyamasādhuvṛttaḥ
abhyantaraṃ praviśati pravidārya tasya sthānāni pūrvavihitāni tataḥ sa pūyaḥ
tasyātimātragamanād gatiriṣyate tu nāḍīva yadvahati tena matā ca nāḍī 1
doṣaistribhirbhavati sā pṛthagekaśaśca sammūrcchitairapi ca śalyanimittato'nyā 2
tatrānilātparuṣasūkṣmamukhī saśūlā phenānuviddhamadhikaṃ sravati kṣapāsu 3
pittāttu tṛḍjvarakarī paridāhayuktā pītaṃ sravatyadhikamuṣṇamahaḥsu cāpi 4
jñeyā kaphād bahughanā sitapicchilāsrāṃ stabdhā sakaṇḍurarujā rajanīpravṛddhā 5
dāhajvaraśvasanamūrcchanavaktraśoṣā yasyāṃ bhavantyabhihitāni ca lakṣaṇāni
tāmādiśetpavanapittakaphaprakopād ghorāṃ gatiṃ tvasuharāmiva kālarātrim 6
naṣṭaṃ kathañcidaṇumārgamudīriteṣu sthāneṣu śalyamacireṇa gatiṃ karoti
sā phenilaṃ mathitamuṣṇamasṛgvimiśraṃ srāvaṃ karoti sahasā sarujañca nityam 7
nāḍī tridoṣaprabhavā na sidhyedantyāścatasraḥ khalu yatnasādhyāḥ 8
atha nāḍīvraṇacikitsā
tatrānilotthāmupanāhya pūrvamaśeṣataḥ pūyagatiṃ vidārya
tilairapāmārgaphalaiḥ supiṣṭaiḥ sasaindhavaiḥ samparipūrya bandhet
prakṣālane vā'pi sadā vraṇasya yojyaṃ mahadyatkhalu pañcamūlam 9
hiṃsrāṃ haridrāṃ kaṭukāṃ balāñca gojihvikāñcāpi sabilvamūlām
saṃhṛtya tailaṃ vipaced vraṇasya saṃśodhanaṃ pūraṇaropaṇañca 10
pittātmikāṃ prāgupanāhya dhīmānutkārikābhiḥ sapayoghṛtābhiḥ
nipātya śastraṃ tilanāgadantīyaṣṭyāhvakalkaiḥ paripūrayecca
prakṣālane cāpi sasomanimbā niśā prayojyā kuśalena nityam 11
śyāmātribhaṇḍī triphalāsusiddhaṃ haridra yā tilvakavṛkṣakeṇa
ghṛtaṃ sadugdhaṃ vraṇatarpaṇena hanyādgatiṃ koṣṭhagatā'pi yā syāt 12
nāḍīṃ kaphotthāmupanāhya pūrvaṃ kulatthasiddhārthakasaktukiṇvaiḥ
mṛdūkṛtāmeṣyagatiṃ viditvā nipātayecchastramaśeṣakāri 13
dadyād vraṇe nimbatilāgnidantīsurāṣṭrajāḥ saindhavasamprayuktāḥ
prakṣālane cāpi karañjanimbajātyarkapīlusvarasāḥ prayojyāḥ 14
svarjikāsindhudantyagniyūthikā jalanīlikā
kharamañjaribījeṣu tailaṃ gomūtrasādhitam
duṣṭavraṇapraśamanaṃ kaphanāḍīvraṇāpaham 15
saindhavārkamaricajvalanākhyairmārkaveṇa rajanīdvayasiddham
tailametadacireṇa nihanyād dūragāmapi kaphānilanāḍīm 16
nāḍīṃ tu śalyaprabhavāṃ vidārya niṣkāsya śalyaṃ pratiśodhya mārgam
bandhed vraṇaṃ kṣaudra ghṛtapragāḍhaistilaistato ropaṇamasya kuryāt 17
kumbhīkakharjūrakapitthabilvavanaspatīnāñca śalāṭuvarge
kṛtvā kaṣāyaṃ vipacettu tailamāvāpya mustaṃ saralāṃ priyaṅgum 18
saugandhikaṃ mocarasāhipuṣpaṃ lodhrāṇi datvā khalu dhātakīñca
etena śalyaprabhavā hi nāḍī rohed vraṇo vā sukhamāśu caiva 19
iti kumbhīkādyatailam
snuhyarkadugdhadārvīṇāṃ varttiṃ kṛtvā prapūrayet
eṣa sarvaśarīrasthāṃ nāḍīṃ hanyātprayogarāṭ 20
āragvadhaniśākālā cūrṇājyakṣaudra saṃyutā
sūtravarttivraṇe yojyā śodhanī gatināśinī 21
varttīkṛtaṃ mākṣikasamprayuktaṃ nāḍīghnamuktaṃ lavaṇottamaṃ vā
duṣṭavraṇe yadvihitaṃ tu tailaṃ tatsevyamānaṃ gatimāśu hanti 22
jātyarkasampāka karañjadantī sindhūtthasauvarcalayāvaśūkaiḥ
varttiḥ kṛtā hantyacireṇa nāḍīṃ snukkṣīrapiṣṭā tu sacitrakeṇa 23
bibhītakāmrāsthi vaṭapravāla hareṇukāśaṅkhinibījamiśrā
vārāhaviṭsūkṣmamasī pradeyā nāḍīṣu tailena ca miśrayitvā 24
meṣaromamasītumbyā kaṭutailaṃ vipācitam
nāḍīvraṇaṃ cirodbhūtaṃ jayettu tūlasaṅgamāt 25
karcūrakasya svarase kaṭutailaṃ vipācayet
sindūrakalkitaṃ nāḍīduṣṭavraṇavisarpanut 26
karcūrakarase tailaṃ purasindūrakalkitam
pāmāduṣṭavraṇaṃ nāḍīṃ hanyātsarvavraṇāntakṛt 27
bhallātakārkamaricairlavaṇottamena siddhaṃ viḍaṅgarajanīdvayacitrakaiśca
syānmārkavasya ca rasena nihanti tailaṃ nāḍīṃ kaphānilakṛtāmapacīṃ vraṇāṃśca 28
svarjikā saindhavaṃ dantī nīlīmūlaṃ phalaṃ tathā
mūtre caturguṇe siddhaṃ tailaṃ nāḍīvraṇāpaham
sarvo vraṇakramaḥ kāryaḥ śodhanāropaṇādikaḥ 29
guggulutriphalāvyoṣaiḥ samāṃśairājyayojitaiḥ
akṣapramāṇāṃ guṭikāṃ khādedekāmatandri taḥ 30
nāḍīṃ duṣṭavraṇaṃ śūlamudāvarttaṃ bhagandaram
gulmañca gudajānhanyātpakṣirāṭ pannagāniva 31
yā dvivraṇīye vihitāstu vattaryastāḥ
sarvanāḍīṣu bhiṣagvidadhyāt 32
kṛśadurbalabhīrūṇāṃ nāḍīṃ marmāśritāmapi
kṣārasūtreṇa tāṃ chindyānna śastreṇa kadācana 33
eṣaṇyā gatimanviṣya kṣārasūtrānusāriṇīm
sūcīṃ nidadhyādgatyante pronnāmyāśu vinirharet 34
sūtrasyāntaṃ samānīya gāḍhaṃ bandhanamācaret
tataḥ kṣārabalaṃ vīkṣya sūtramanyatpraveśayet 35
kṣārāktaṃ matimānvaidyo yāvanna cchidyate gatiḥ
bhagandareṣveṣa vidhiḥ kāryo vaidyena jānatā 36
arbudādiṣu cotkṣipya mūle sūtraṃ nidhāpayet
sūcībhiryavavaktrābhirācitaṃ vā samantataḥ
mūle sūtreṇa badhnīyācchinne copacared vraṇam 37
ityekonapañcāśattamo nāḍīvraṇādhikāraḥ samāptaḥ 49
<2.4.2>
atha pañcāśattamo bhagandarādhikāraḥ 50
kaṭīkapālanistodadāha kaṇḍūrujā'dayaḥ
bhavanti pūrvarūpāṇi bhaviṣyati bhagandare 1
gudasya dvyaṅgule kṣetre pārśvataḥ piḍakā'ttikṛt
bhinno bhagandaro jñeyaḥ sa ca pañcavidho bhavet 2
bhagandaraśabdasya niruktimāha bhojaḥ
bhagaṃ parisamantācca gudaṃ vastiṃ tathaiva ca
bhagavad dārayedyasmāttasmādeṣa bhagandaraḥ 3
kaṣāyarukṣairatikopito'nilastvapānadeśe piḍakāṃ karoti yām
upekṣaṇātpākamupaiti dāruṇaṃ rujā ca bhinnā'ruṇaphenavāhinī
tatrāgamo mūtrapurīṣaretasāṃ vraṇairanekaiḥ śataponakaṃ vadet 4
prakopanaiḥ pittamatiprakopitaṃ karoti raktāṃ piḍakāṃ gude gatām
tadāśupākā'himapūtivāhinīṃ bhagandaraṃ coṣṭraśirodharaṃ vadet 5
kaṇḍūyano ghanasrāvī kaṭhino mandavedanaḥ
śvetāvabhāsaḥ kaphajaḥ parisrāvī bhagandaraḥ 6
bahuvarṇarujāsrāvā piḍakā gostanopamā
śambūkāvarttagatikaḥ śambūkāvarttako mataḥ 7
kṣatādgatiḥ pāyugatā vivarddhate hyapekṣaṇātsyuḥ kṛmayo vidārya te
prakurvate mārgamanekadhā mukhairvraṇaistamunmārgibhagandaraṃ vadet 8
ghorāḥ sādhayituṃ duḥkhāḥ sarva eva bhagandarāḥ
teṣvasādhyastridoṣotthaḥ kṣatajaścaviśeṣataḥ 9
vātamūtrapurīṣāṇi śukrañca kṛmayastathā
bhagandarātsravantastu nāśayanti tamāturam 10
athāsya piḍakāmeva tathā yatnādupācaret
śuddhysrasrutisekādyairyathā pākaṃ na gacchati 11
vaṭapatreṣṭakā śuṇṭhīsaguḍūcīpunarnavāḥ
supiṣṭaḥ piḍakā'vasthe lepaḥ śasto bhagandare 12
piḍakānāma pakvānāmapatarpaṇapūrvakam
karma kuryādvirekāntaṃ bhinnānāṃ vakṣyate kriyā 13
eṣaṇīpāṭanakṣāravahnidāhādikaṃ kramam
vidhāya vraṇavatkāryaṃ yathādoṣaṃ yathākramam 14
payaḥ piṣṭaistilāriṣṭamadhukaiśca suśītalaiḥ
bhagandare praśasto'ya sarakte vedanāvati 15
sumanā vaṭapatrāṇi guḍūcī viśvabheṣajam
sasaindhavastakrapiṣṭo lepo hanti bhagandaram 16
trivṛttilā nāgadantī mañjiṣṭhā saha sarpiṣā
utsādanaṃ bhavedetatsaindhavakṣaudra saṃyutam 17
khadirāmburato bhūtvā kaṣāyaṃ traiphalaṃ pibet
mahiṣākṣaviḍaṅgānāṃ bhagandaravināśanam 18
śambūkamāṃsaṃ bhuñjīta prakārairvyañjanādibhiḥ
ajīrṇavarjī māsena mucyate tu bhagandarāt 19
nyagrodhādirgaṇo yastu hitaḥ śodhanaropaṇaḥ
tailaṃ ghṛtaṃ vā tat pakvaṃ bhagandaravināśanam 20
tilā jyotiṣmatī kuṣṭhaṃ lāṅgalī girikarṇikā
śatāhvātrivṛtādantyaḥ śodhanāśca bhagandare 21
tilābhayālodhramariṣṭapatraṃ niśe balā lodhramagāradhūmam
bhagandare cāpyupadaṃśaje ca druṣṭavraṇe ropaṇaśodhanāya 22
snuhyarkadugdhadārvībhirvarttiṃ kṛtvā vicakṣaṇaḥ
bhagandaragatiṃ jñātvā pūrayettāṃ prayatnataḥ 23
eṣā sarvaśarīrasthāṃ nāḍīṃ hanyānnasaṃśayaḥ
triphalārasasaṃyuktaṃ viḍālāsthipralepanam 24
bhagandaraṃ nihantyāśu duṣṭavraṇaharaṃ param
trivṛttejovatīdantīkalko nāḍīvraṇāpahaḥ 25
jyotiṣmatī lāṅgalakī śyāmā dantī trivṛttilāḥ
kuṣṭhaṃ śatāhvā golomī tilvako girikarṇikā 26
kāsīsakāñcanakṣīryau vargaḥ śodhana iṣyate 27
madhutailayutā viḍaṅgasāratriphalāmāgadhikākaṇāśca līḍhāḥ
kṛmikuṣṭhabhagandarapramehakṣayanāḍīvraṇaropaṇā bhavanti 28
citrakārkau trivṛtpāṭhe malayūhayamārakau
sudhāṃ vacāṃ lāṅgalakīṃ haritālaṃ suvarcikām 29
jyotiṣmatīñca saṃhṛtya tailaṃ dhīmānvipācayet
etadviṣyandanaṃ nāma tailaṃ dadyādbhagandare
śodhanaṃ ropaṇañcaiva savarṇakaraṇaṃ tathā 30
niśā'kakṣīra sindhūtthapurāśvaharavatsakaiḥ
siddhamabhyañjanaṃ tailaṃ bhagandaraharaṃ param 31
karavīraniśā dantīlāṅgalīlavaṇāgnibhiḥ
mātuluṅgakavatsāhvaiḥ pacettailaṃ bhagandare 32
triphalāpurakṛṣṇānāṃ tripañcakāṃśayojitā
guṭikā śothagulmārśobhagandaravatāṃ hitā 33
nāḍyantare vraṇānkuryādbhiṣak tu śataponake
tatasteṣvavarūḍheṣu śeṣā nāḍīrupācaret 34
vyādhau tatra bahucchidre bhiṣajā tu vijānatā
arddhalāṅgalakacchedaḥ kāryo lāṅgalako'pi vā 35
sarvatobhadra ko vā'pi kāryo gotīrthako'pi vā 36
dvābhyāṃ samābhyāṃ pārśvābhyāṃ chedo lāṅgalako mataḥ
hrasvamekataraṃ yattu so'ddhalāṅgalakaḥ smṛtaḥ 37
sevanīṃ varjayitvā tu caturddhā dārite gude
sarvatobhadra kaṃ chedamāhuśchedavido janāḥ
pārśvādāgataśastreṇa cchedo gotīrthako mataḥ 38
sarvānāsrāvamārgāṃstu dahedvaidyastathā'gninā
athoṣṭragrīvameṣiṇyā chitvā kṣāraṃ nipātayet 39
utkṛtyāsrāvamārgantu parisrāviṇi buddhimān
kṣāreṇa vā srāvagatiṃ daheddhutavahena vā 40
gatimanviṣya śastreṇa cchindyātkharjūrapatrakam 41
candrā rddha candra cakrañca sūcīmukhamavāṅmukham
chitvā'gninā dahetsamyagevaṃ kṣāreṇa vā punaḥ 42
eṣāṃ tu śastra patanādvedanā yatra jāyate
tatrāṇutailenoṣṇena pariṣekaḥ praśasyate 43
āgantuje bhiṣaṅ nāḍīṃ śastreṇotkṛtya yatnataḥ 44
jambvoṣṭhenāgnivarṇena taptayā vā śalākayā
dahedyathoktaṃ matimāṃstaṃ vraṇaṃ susamāhitaḥ 45
vyāyāmaṃ maithunaṃ yuddhaṃ pṛṣṭhayānaṃ gurūṇi ca
saṃvatsaraṃ parihareduparūḍhavraṇo naraḥ 46
iti pañcāśattamo bhagandarādhikāraḥ samāptaḥ 50
<2.4.3>
athaikapañcāśattama upadaṃśādhikāraḥ 51
hastābhighātānnakha dantaghātādadhāvanādatyupasevanādvā
yonipradoṣācca bhavanti śiśne pañcopadaṃśā vividhāpacāraiḥ 1
satodabhedasphuraṇaiḥ sakṛṣṇaiḥ sphoṭairvyavasyetpavanopadaṃśam
pītairbahukledayutaiḥ sadāhaiḥ pittena raktaiḥ piśitāvabhāsaiḥ 2
sphoṭaiḥ sakṛṣṇai rudhiraṃ sravantaṃ raktātmakaṃ pittasamānaliṅgam
sakaṇḍuraiḥ śothayutairmahadbhiḥ śuklairghanaiḥ srāvayutaiḥ kaphena
nānāvidhasrāvarujopapannamasādhyamāhustrimalopadaṃśam 3
praśīrṇamāṃsaṃ kṛmibhiḥprajagdhaṃ muṣkāvaśeṣaṃ parivarjanīyam 4
sañjātamātre na karoti mūḍhaḥ kriyāṃ naro yo viṣaye prasaktaḥ
kālena śothakṛmidāhapākaiḥ praśīrṇaśiśno mriyate sa tena 5
upadaṃśeṣu sādhyeṣu snigdhasvinnasya dehinaḥ
meḍhramadhye śirāṃ vidhyātpātayedvā jalaukasaḥ 6
haredubhayataścāpi doṣānatyarthamūrcchitān
sadyo nirhṛtadoṣasya rukśothāvupaśāmyataḥ 7
yadi vā durbalojanturna vā prāptavirecanaḥ
nirūheṇa harettasya doṣānatyarthamūrcchitān
pāko rakṣyaḥ prayatnena śiśnakṣayakaro hi saḥ 8
prapauṇḍarīkayaṣṭyāhvasaralāgurudārubhiḥ
sarāsnākuṣṭhapṛthvīkairvātike lepasecane 9
niculairaṇḍabījāni yavagodhūmasaktavaḥ
etaiśca vātajaṃ snigdhaiḥ sukhoṣṇaiḥ sampralepayet 10
gairikāñcanamañjiṣṭhā madhukośīrapadmakaiḥ
sacandanotpalaiḥ snigdhaḥ paittikaṃ sampralepayet 11
padmotpalamṛṇālaiśca sasarjārjunavetasaiḥ
sarpiḥ snigdhaiḥ samadhukaiḥ paittikaṃ sampralepayet 12
secayecca ghṛtakṣīraśarkarekṣumadhūdakaiḥ
athavā'pi suśītena kaṣāyeṇa vaṭādinā 13
śālājakarṇāśvakarṇadhavatvagbhiḥ kaphotthitam
surāpiṣṭābhiruṣṇābhiḥ satailābhiḥ pralepayet 14
āragvadhādikvāthena pariṣekañca dāpayet 15
nimbārjunāśvatthakadambaśālajambūvaṭodumbaravetasaiśca
prakṣālanālepakṛtānikuryāccūrṇaṃ sapittāsrabhavopadaṃśe 16
tvaco dāruharidrā yāḥ śaṅkhanābhī rasāñjanam
lākṣāgomayaniryāsastailaṃ kṣaudraṃ ghṛtaṃ payaḥ 17
ebhistu piṣṭaistulyāṃśairupadaṃśaṃ pralepayet
vraṇāśca tena śāmyanti śvayathurdāha eva ca 18
upadaṃśadvaye'pyetāṃ pratyākhyāyācaretkriyām
tayoreva ca yā yogyā vīkṣya doṣabalābalam 19
śastreṇollekhayetkvāpi pākamāgatamāśu vai
tamapohya tilaiḥ sarpiḥ kṣaudra yuktaiḥ pralepayet 20
vaṭaprarohārjunajambupathyā lodhraṃ haridrā ca hitāḥ pralepe
tathopadaṃśeṣvavarohaṇārthaṃ cūrṇañca kāryaṃ vimalāñjanena 21
triphalāyāḥ kaṣāyeṇa bhṛṅgarājarasena vā
vraṇaprakṣālanaṃ kāryamupadaṃśapraśāntaye 22
jayājapā'śvamārārkaśampākānāṃ dalaiḥ kramāt
kṛtaṃ prakṣālanaṃ kvāthaṃ meḍhrapāke prayojayet 23
śampākanimbatriphalākirātakvāthaṃ pibedvā khadirāsanābhyām
sagugguluṃ vā triphalāyutaṃ vā sarvopadaṃśāpaharaḥprayogaḥ 24
nīlotpalāni kumudaṃ padmasaugandhikāni ca
upadaṃśeṣu cūrṇāni pradeho'ya praśasyate 25
bandhūkadalacūrṇena dāḍimatvagrajo'thavā
guṇḍanaṃ vṛṣaṇe śastaṃ lepaḥ pūgaphalena vā 26
saurāṣṭrī gairikaṃ tutthaṃ puṣpakāsīsasaindhavam
lodhraṃ rasāñjanañcāpi haritālaṃ manaḥ śilā 27
hareṇukaile tu tathā samaṃ saṃhṛtya cūrṇayet
taccūrṇaṃ kṣaudra saṃyuktamupadaṃśeṣu pūjitam 28
puṭadagdhaṃ kṛtaṃ bhasma haritālaṃ manaḥśilā
upadaṃśavisarpāṇāmetaddhānikaraṃ param 29
dahetkaṭāhe triphalāṃ tāṃ masīṃ madhusaindhavam
upadaṃśe pralepo'ya sadyo ropayati vraṇam 30
tirīṭāñjanavajrākṣakovidārebhakeśaraiḥ
lepanaṃ puruṣavyādhau jalapiṣṭaiḥ praśasyate 31
rasāñjanaṃ śirīṣeṇa pathyayā vā samanvitam
sakṣaudraṃ lepanaṃ yojyaṃ sarvāṅgagagadāpaham 32
bhārgīsambhavaśikharijamūlaṃ bhadra śriyaṃ susampiṣṭam
manaḥśilā vai madhunā śamayatyupadaṃśamacireṇa 33
śatadhautaṃ prayatnena liṅgotthamavacūrṇayet
rogaṃ kāsīsacūrṇena puruṣaḥ sukhamāpnuyāt 34
karavīrasya mūlena paripiṣṭena vāriṇā
asādhyā'pi vrajatyastaṃ liṅgotthā rukpralepanāt 35
varānimbārjunāśvatthakhadirāsanavāsakaiḥ
cūrṇitairguggulusamairvaṭakā akṣasaṃmitāḥ 36
karttavyā nāśayantyāśu sarvāṃlliṅgasamutthitān
upadaṃśānasṛgdoṣāṃstathā duṣṭavraṇānapi 37
karañjanimbārjunaśālajambūvaṭādibhiḥ kalkakaṣāyasiddham
sarpirnihanyādupadaṃśadoṣaṃ sadāhapākasrutirāgayuktam 38
bhūnimbanimbatriphalāpaṭolakarañjadhātrīkhadirāsanānām
satoyakalkairghṛtamāśu pakvaṃ sarvopadaṃśāpaharaṃ pradiṣṭam 39
ghṛtāni yāni vakṣyāmi kuṣṭhe nāḍīvraṇe vraṇe
upadaṃśe prayojyāni sekābhyañjanabhojanaiḥ 40
āgāradhūmo rajanī surākiṭṭaṃ ca taistribhiḥ
yathottaraiḥ pacettailaṃ kaṇḍūśotharujā'paham
śodhanaṃ ropaṇaṃ caiva hyupadaṃśaharaṃ param 41
gojīviḍaṅgayaṣṭībhiḥ sarvagandhaiśca saṃyutam
etatsarvopadaṃśeṣu tailaṃ ropaṇamiṣyate 42
jambuvetasapatrāṇi dhātrīpatraṃ tathaiva ca
naktamālasya patrāṇi tadvatpadmotpalāni ca 43
elā cātiviṣā'mrāsthi madhukañca priyaṅgavaḥ
lākṣā kālīyakaṃ lodhraṃ candanaṃ trivṛtāhvayā 44
etānyekīkṛtānyeva bastamūtreṇa peṣayet
akṣamātrairimairdra vyaistailaprasthaṃ vipācayet 45
sarvavraṇaharaṃ tailametatsiddhaṃ na saṃśayaḥ
upadaṃśaharaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ munibhiḥ parikīrtitam 46
yasya liṅgasya māṃsaṃ tu śīryate muṣkaśeṣataḥ 47
tiktakośātakīlābubījaṃ nāgarasādhitam
tailaṃ hantyaviśeṣeṇa vraṇaṃ duṣṭamanekadhā 48
sevennityaṃ yavānnañca pānīyaṃ kaupameva ca
arśasāṃ cchinnadagdhānāṃ kriyāṃ cātra prayojayet 49
ityekapañcāśattama upadaṃśādhikāraḥ samāptaḥ 51
<2.4.4>
atha dvipañcāśattamo liṅgārśo'dhikāraḥ 52
aṅkurairiva sañjātairuparyupari saṃsthitaiḥ
krameṇa jāyate varttistāmracūḍaśikhopamā 1
koṣasyābhyantare sandhau parvasandhigatāpi vā
liṅgavarttiriti khyātā liṅgārśa iti cāpare
savedanāpicchilā ca duścikitsyā tridoṣajā 2
kṣāreṇa pradahecchittvā liṅgavarttimaśeṣataḥ
vraṇavaccācaretsamyak samaṃ cūrṇamupadra vān 3
svarjikātutthaśaileyamañjanaṃ sarasāñjanam
manaḥśilā'le ca samaṃ cūrṇaṃ māṃsāṅkurāpaham 4
harati ghṛtakumārīpatramāveṣṭenena grathanavidhiviśeṣāṃścarmakīlāṃstṛtīye
ahani gurutarānapyaṅgalabdhapratiṣṭhān vidhiriva viparītaḥ pauruṣasya prakārān 5
śubhe tu cāraṭīmūlaṃ vṛṣamūtreṇa peṣayet
carmakīlānnihantyāśu pralepātsādhanodbhavān 6
iti dvipañcāśattamo liṅgārśo'dhikāraḥ samāptaḥ 52
<2.4.5>
atha tripañcāśattamaḥ śūkadoṣādhikāraḥ 53
akramācchephaso vṛddhiṃ yo'bhivāñchati mūḍhadhīḥ
vyādhayastasya jāyante daśa cāṣṭau ca śūkajāḥ 1
śūkadoṣā daśa cāṣṭau ca bhavanti
gaurasarṣapasaṃsthānā śūkadurbhagahetukā
piḍikā śleṣmavātābhyāṃ jñeyā sarṣapikā tu sā 2
kaṭhinā viṣamairbhugnairvāyunāṣṭhīlikā bhavet 3
śūkairyatpūritaṃ śaśvad grathitaṃ nāma tatkaphāt 4
kumbhikā raktapittātsyājjāmbuvāsthinibhā sitā 5
alajī syāttathā yādṛkpramehapiḍakā tathā
sā ca raktāsitā sphoṭacitā ca kathitā budhaiḥ 6
mṛditaṃ pīḍitaṃ yattu saṃrabdhaṃ vātakopataḥ 7
pāṇibhyāṃ bhṛśasaṃmūḍhe saṃmūḍha piḍikā bhavet 8
dīrghā bahvyaścapiḍakā dīryante madhyatastu yāḥ
sovamanthaḥ kaphāsṛgbhyāṃ vedanāromaharṣakṛt 9
piḍikā piḍikāvyāptā pittaśoṇitasambhavā
padmakarṇikasaṃsthānā jñeyā puṣkariketi sā 10
sparśahānintu janayecchoṇitaṃ śūkadūṣitam 11
mudgamāṣopamā raktā raktapittodbhavā ca yā
eṣottamākhyapiḍakā śūkājīrṇasamudbhavā 12
chidrai raṇumukhailiṅgaṃ ciraṃ yasya samantataḥ
vātaśoṇitajo vyādhirvijñeyaḥśataponakaḥ 13
vātapittakṛto jñeyastvakpāko jvaradāhakṛt 14
kṛṣṇaiḥ sphoṭaiḥ saraktābhiḥ piḍakābhirnipīḍitam
liṅgaṃ vāsturujaścogrā jñeyaṃ tacchoṇitārbudam 15
māṃsaduṣṭaṃ vijānīyādarbudaṃ māṃsasambhavam 16
śīryante yasya māṃsāni yasya sarvāśca vedanāḥ
vidyāt taṃ māṃsapākaṃ tu sarvadoṣakṛtaṃ bhiṣak 17
vidra dhiṃ sannipātena yathoktamabhinirdiśet 18
kṛṣṇāni citrāṇyathavā śuklāni saviṣāṇi tu
pātitāni pacantyāśu meḍhraṃ niravaśeṣataḥ 18
kālāni bhūtvā māṃsāni śīryante yasya dehinaḥ
sannipātasamutthāṃśca tānvidyāttilakālakān 19
tatra māṃsārbudaṃ yacca māṃsapākaśca yaḥsmṛtaḥ
vidra dhiśca na sidhyanti ye ca syustilakālakāḥ 20
śūkadoṣeṣu sarveṣu viṣaghnīṃ kārayetkriyām
jalaukābhirharedra ktaṃ recanaṃ laghu bhojayet
gugguluṃ pācayeccāpi triphalākvāthasaṃyutam
kṣīreṇa lepasekāṃśca śītenaiva hi kārayet 21
dārvīsurasayaṣṭyāhvairgṛhadhūmaniśāyutaiḥ
sampakvaṃ tailamabhyaṅgānmeḍhrarogaṃ hi nāśayet 22
rasāñjanaṃ sāhvayamekameva pralepamātreṇa nayatpraśāntim
sapūtipūyavraṇaśothakaṇḍūśūlānvitaṃ sarvamanaṅgarogam 23
iti tripañcāśattamaḥ śūkadoṣādhikāraḥ samāptaḥ 53
<2.4.6>
atha catuṣpañcāśattamaḥ kuṣṭharogādhikāraḥ 54
virodhīnyannapānāni dra vasnigdhagurūṇi ca
bhajatāmāgatāṃ chardiṃ vegāṃścānyānpratighnatām 1
vyāyāmamagnitāpañcāpyatibhuktvā niṣeviṇām
śītoṣṇalaṅghanāhārānkramaṃ muktvā niṣeviṇām 2
gharmaśramabhayārttānāṃ drutaṃ śītāmbusevinām
ajīrṇādhyaśināṃ cāpi pañcakarmāpacāriṇām 3
navānnadadhimatsyādilavaṇāmlaniṣeviṇām
māṣamūlakapiṣṭānnatilakṣīraguḍāśinām 4
vyavāyaṃ cāpyajīrṇe'nne divānidrāṃ niṣeviṇām
viprāngurūndharṣayatāṃ pāpaṃ karma ca kurvatām 5
vātādayastrayo doṣāstvagraktaṃ māṃsamambu ca
dūṣayanti sa kuṣṭhānāṃ saptako dra vyasaṅgrahaḥ
ataḥ kuṣṭhāni jāyante saptadhaikādaśaiva ca 6
pūrvaṃ trikaṃ tathā sidhmaṃ tataḥ kākaṇakaṃ tathā
puṇḍarīkarkṣajihvake mahākuṣṭhāni sapta ca 7
ekakuṣṭhaṃ smṛtaṃ pūrvaṃ gajacarma tataḥ smṛtam
tataścarmadalaṃ proktaṃ tataścāpi vicarcikā 8
vipādikā'bhidhā saiva pāmā kacchūstataḥ param
dadruvisphoṭakiṭibhālasakāni ca veṣṭitam 9
kṣudra kuṣṭhāni caitāni kathitāni bhiṣagvaraiḥ 10
kuṣṭhāni saptadhā doṣaiḥ pṛthagdvandvaiḥ samāgataiḥ
sarveṣvapi tridoṣeṣu vyapadeśo'dhikatvataḥ 11
atiślakṣṇaḥ kharasparśaḥ svedāsvedavivarṇatā
dāhaḥkaṇḍūstvaci svāpastodaḥ koṭhonnatiḥ klamaḥ 12
vraṇānāmadhikaṃ śūlaṃ śīghrotpattiścirasthitiḥ
rūḍhānāmapi rūkṣatvaṃ nimitte'lpe'pi kopanam
romaharṣo'sṛjaḥ kārṣṇyaṃ kuṣṭhalakṣaṇamagrajam 13
dūṣayanti ślathīkṛtya niścalatvāditastataḥ
tvacaḥ kurvanti vaivarṇyaṃ doṣāḥ kuṣṭhamuśanti tam 14
vātena kuṣṭhaṃ kāpālaṃ pittenaudumbaraṃ kaphāt
maṇḍalākhyaṃ vicarcī ca ṝkṣākhyaṃ vātapittataḥ 15
carmaikakuṣṭhakiṭibhasidhmālasavipādikāḥ
vātaśleṣmodbhavāḥ pittakaphāddadruśatāruṣī 16
puṇḍarīkaṃ savisphoṭaṃ pāmā carmadalaṃ tathā
sarvairevolvaṇairdoṣairāhuḥ kākaṇakaṃ budhāḥ 17
kṛṣṇāruṇakapālābhaṃ yadrū kṣaṃ paruṣaṃ tanu
kāpālaṃ todabahulaṃ tatkuṣṭhaṃ viṣamaṃ smṛtam 18
udumbaraphalābhāsaṃ kuṣṭhamaudumbaraṃ vadet
rugdāharāgakaṇḍūbhiḥ parītaṃ romapiñjaram 19
śvetaraktaṃ sthiraṃ styānaṃ snigdhamutsannamaṇḍalam
kṛcchramanyo'nyasaṃsaktaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ maṇḍalamucyate 20
śvetatāmrañca tanu yadra jo ghṛṣṭaṃ vimuñcati
prāyeṇorasi tatsidhmamalābukusumopamam 21
yatkākaṇantikāvarṇamapākaṃ tīvravedanam
tridoṣaliṅgaṃ tatkuṣṭhaṃ kākaṇaṃ naiva sidhyati 22
tacchvetaṃ raktaparyantaṃ puṇḍarīkadalopamam
sarāgañcaiva sotsedhaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ kapholvaṇam 23
karkaśaṃ raktaparyantamantaḥ śyāvaṃ savedanam
yadṛkṣajihvāsaṃsthānamṛkṣajihvaṃ taducyate 24
asvedanaṃ mahāvāstu yanmatsyaśakalopamam
tadekakuṣṭhaṃ carmākhyaṃ bahalaṃ gajacarmavat 25
raktaṃ saśūlaṃ kaṇḍūmatsasphoṭaṃ dalayatyapi
taccarmadalamākhyātaṃ sparśasyāsahanañca yat 26
sakaṇḍūḥ piḍakā śyāvā bahusrāvā vicarcikā 27
vaipādikaṃ pāṇipādasphuṭanaṃ tīvravedanam 28
sūkṣmā bahvyaḥ srāvavatyaḥ pradāhāḥ pāmetyuktāḥ piḍakāḥ kaṇḍumatyaḥ 29
saiva sphoṭastīvradāhairupetā jñeyā pāṇyoḥ kacchurugrā sphicośca 30
sakaṇḍūrāgapiḍakaṃ dadrurmaṇḍalamudgatam 31
sphoṭāḥ śyāvāruṇābhāsā visphoṭāḥ syustanutvacaḥ 32
śyāmaṃ kiṇakharasparśaṃ paruṣaṃ kiṭibhaṃ smṛtam 33
kaṇḍūmadbhiḥ sarāgaiśca gaṇḍairalasakaṃ citam 34
raktaśyāvaṃ sadāhārtti śatāruḥsyād bahuvraṇam 35
tvaksthe vaivarṇyamaṅgeṣu kuṣṭhe raukṣyañca jāyate
tvaksvāpo romaharṣaśca svedasyātipravarttanam 36
kaṇḍūrvipūyakaścaiva kuṣṭhe śoṇitasambhave 37
bāhulyaṃ vaktraśoṣaśca kārkaśyaṃ piḍakodgamaḥ
todaḥ sphoṭaḥ sthiratvañca kuṣṭhe māṃsasamāśrite 38
kauṇyaṃ gatikṣayo'ṅgānāṃ sambhedaḥ kṣatasarpaṇam
medaḥ sthānagate liṅgaṃ prāguktāni tathaiva ca 39
nāsābhaṅgo'kṣirāgaśca kṣateṣu kṛmisambhavaḥ
svaropaghātaḥ pīḍā ca bhavetkuṣṭhe'sthimajjage 40
dampatyoḥ kuṣṭhabāhulyād duṣṭaśoṇitaśukrayoḥ
yadapatyaṃ tayorjātaṃ jñeyaṃ tadapi kuṣṭhitam 41
kharaṃ śyāvāruṇaṃ rūkṣaṃ vātakuṣṭhaṃ savedanam
pittātprakuthitaṃ dāharāgasrāvānvitaṃ matam
kaphotkledi ghanaṃ snigdhaṃ sakaṇḍūśaityagauravam
dviliṅgaṃ dvandvajaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ triliṅgaṃ sānnipātikam 42
sādhyaṃ tvagraktamāṃsasthaṃ vātaśleṣmādhikañca yat
medogaṃ dvandvajaṃ yāpyaṃ varjyaṃ majjāsthisaṃśritam
kṛmikṛd dāhamandāgnisaṃyuktaṃ yat tridoṣajam 43
prabhinnaṃ prasrutāṅgañca raktanetraṃ hatasvaram
pañcakarmaguṇātītaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ hantīha kuṣṭhinam 44
kuṣṭhaikasambhavaṃ śvitraṃ kilāsaṃ cāruṇaṃ bhavet
nirdiṣṭamaparisrāvi tridhātūdbhavasaṃśrayam 45
vātādrū kṣāruṇaṃ pittāttāmraṃ kamalapatravat
sadāhaṃ romavidhvaṃsi kaphācchvetaṃ ghanaṃ guru
sakaṇḍūkaṃ kramādra ktamāṃsamedaḥsu cādiśet
varṇenaivedṛgubhayaṃ kṛcchraṃ taccottarottaram 46
aśuklaromābahalamasaṃśliṣṭamatho navam
anagnidagdhajaṃ sādhyaṃ śvitraṃ varjyamato'nyathā 47
guhyapāṇitalauṣṭheṣu jātamapyacirantanam
varjanīyaṃ viśeṣeṇa kilāsaṃ siddhimicchatā 48
prasaṅgādgātrasaṃsparśānniśvāsātsahabhojanāt
ekaśayyā'sanāccāpivastramālyānulepanāt
kaṇḍūkuṣṭhopadaṃśāśca bhūtonmādavraṇajvarāḥ
aupasargikarogāśca saṅkrāmanti narānnaram 49
mriyate yadi kuṣṭhena punarjātasya tadbhavet
ato ninditarogo'ya kuṣṭhaṃ kaṣṭaṃ prakīrttitam 50
vātottareṣu sarpirvamanaṃ śleṣmottareṣu kuṣṭheṣu
pittottareṣu lepaḥ seko raktasya mocanaṃ śreṣṭham 51
pathyākarañjasiddhārthaniśā'valgujasaindhavaiḥ
viḍaṅgasahitaiḥ piṣṭairlepo mūtreṇa kuṣṭhanut 52
somarājībhavaṃ cūrṇaṃ śṛṅgaberasamanvitam
udvarttanamidaṃ hanti kuṣṭhamugraṃ kṛtāspadam 53
rasāyanaṃ pravakṣyāmi brahmaṇā yadudāhṛtam
mārkaṇḍeyaprabhṛtibhiryatprayuktaṃ maharṣibhiḥ 54
puṣpakāle tu puṣpāṇi phalakāle phalāni ca
saṃgṛhya picumardasya tvaṅmūlāni dalāni ca 55
dviraṃśāni samāhṛtya bhāgikāni prakalpayet
triphalā tryūṣaṇaṃ brāhmī śvadaṃṣṭrā'ruṣkarāgnayaḥ 56
viḍaṅgasāravārāhīlohacūrṇāmṛtāḥ samāḥ
niśādvayāvalgujakaṃ vyādhighātaḥ saśarkaraḥ 57
kuṣṭhamindra yavāḥ pāṭhāḥ cūrṇameṣāṃ tu saṃyutam
khādirāsananimbānāṃ ghanakvāthena bhāvayet
saptadhā pañcanimbaṃ tu mārkavasyarasena ca
snigdhaḥ śuddhatanurdhīmānyojayettacchubhe dine 58
madhunā tiktahaviṣā khadirāsanavāriṇā
lehyamuṣṇāmbhasā vā'pi kālavṛddhyā palaṃ bhavet
jīrṇe tasminsamaśnīyātsnigdhaṃ laghu hitañca yat 59
vicarcikaudumbara puṇḍarīkakāpāladadrū kiṭibhālasādi
śatāruvisphoṭavisarpamālāḥ kaphaprakopaṃ trividhaṃ kilāsam 60
bhagandaraślīpadavātaraktajaḍāndha nāḍīvraṇaśīrṣarogān
sarvānpramehānpradarāṃśca sarvāndaṃṣṭrāviṣaṃ mūlaviṣaṃ nihanti 61
sthūlodaraḥ siṃhakṛśodaraḥ syātsuśliṣṭasandhirmadhunopayogāt
sadopayogādapi ye daśanti sarpādayo yānti vināśamāśu 62
jīvecciraṃ vyādhijarāvimuktaḥ śubhe rataścandra samānakāntiḥ 63
śaśilekhā pañcapalaṃ tāvadgirijasya guggulostu daśa
tāpyasya palatritayaṃ dve lohasrāvaṇīkayośca pale 64
triphalākarañjapallavakhadiraguḍūcītrivṛddantyaḥ
mustāviḍaṅgarajanīkuṭajatvaṅnimbavahnisaṃpākāḥ 65
etai racitāṃ vaṭikāṃ madhusaṃmiśrāṃ giletprātaḥ
gomūtreṇa ca kuṣṭhaṃ nudatyasṛgvātamacireṇa 66
śvitrāṇi pāṇḍurogaṃ viṣamānudarapramehagulmāṃśca
nāśayati valīpalitaṃ yogaḥ svāyambhuvo nāmnā 67
citrakaṃ triphalā vyoṣamajājī kāravī vacā
saindhavātiviṣākuṣṭhaṃ cavyailā ca yavāsakam 68
viḍaṅgānyajamodā ca mustā cāmaradāru ca
yāvantyetāni sarvāṇi tāvanmānantu gugguloḥ 69
saṅkuṭya sarpiṣā sārddhaṃ guṭikāṃ kārayedbhiṣak
prātarbhojanakāle ca khādedagnibalaṃ yathā 70
hantyaṣṭādaśa kuṣṭhāni kṛmiduṣṭavraṇāni ca
grahaṇyarśovikārāṃśca mukhāmayagalagrahān 71
gṛdhrasīmatha bhagnañca gulmaṃ cāpi niyacchati
vyādhīnkoṣṭhagatāṃścāpi jayedviṣṇurivāsurān 72
vātaraktādhikāroktaḥ puraḥ kaiśorakābhidhaḥ
kuṣṭhānāṃ vātaraktānāṃ nāśanaṃ paramauṣadham 73
bhallātakaprasthayugaṃ chitvā dro ṇajale kṣipet
prasthadvayaṃguḍūcyāśca kṣuṇṇaṃ tatrāmbhasi kṣipet
caturthāṃśāvaśeṣaṃ tu kaṣāyamavatārayet
vastrapūte kaṣāye tu vakṣyamāṇaṃ vinikṣipet 75
śarāvamātrakaṃsarpirdugdhaṃ syādāḍhakaṃ tathā
sitāṃ prasthamitāṃ dadyātprasthārddhaṃ mākṣikaṃ kṣipet 76
sarvāṇyekatra bhāṇḍe tu pacenmṛdvagninā śanaiḥ
sarvadra ve ghanībhūte pāvakādavatārayet 77
tatra kṣepyāṇi cūrṇāni brūmo bilvaviṣāmṛtāḥ
vākucī cātha dadra ghnuḥ picumardo harītakī 78
akṣo dhātrī ca mañjiṣṭhā maricaṃ nāgaraṃ kaṇā
yavānī saindhavaṃ mustaṃ tvagelā nāgakeśaram 79
parpaṭaṃ patrakaṃ bālamuśīraṃ candanaṃ tathā
gokṣurasya ca bījāni karcūro raktacandanam 80
pṛthakpalārddhamānānāṃ cūrṇameṣāmiha kṣipet
palamātramitaṃ prātaḥ samaśnīyājjalena hi 81
nāśayedavaleho'ya pathyānyannāni khādataḥ
kuṣṭhāni vātaraktāni sarvāṇyarśāṃsi sevitaḥ 82
vyāyāmamātapaṃ vahnimamlaṃ māṃsaṃ dadhi striyam
tailābhyaṅgaṃ tathādhvānaṃ naro bhallātakī tyajet 83
