murā karpūrakaścāpi śaileyaṃ bhadra mustakam
reṇukā ca priyaṅguśca śrīvāso guggulustathā 54
lākṣā nakhaśca rālaśca dhātakīkusumaṃ tathā
granthiparṇañca mañjiṣṭhā tagaraṃ sikthakaṃ tathā 55
etāni śāṇamānāni kalkīkṛtya śanaiḥ pacet
tailaṃ prasthamitaṃ samyagetatpātre śubhe kṣipet 56
anenābhyaktagātrastu vṛddho'śītisamo'pi saḥ
yuvā bhavati śukrāḍhyaḥ strīṇāmatyantavallabhaḥ 57
vandhyā'pi labhate garbhaṃ vṛddho'pi taruṇāyate
aputraḥ putramāpnoti jīvecca śaradāṃ śatam 58
candanādi mahātailaṃ raktapittaṃ kṣayaṃ jvaram
dāhaṃ prasvedadaurgandhyaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ kaṇḍūṃ vināśayet 59
daśamūlaṃ kaṇā vahniḥ kapitthañca vibhītakam
kaṭphalaṃ maricaṃ viśvamūlaṃ pippalisaindhavam 60
raktarohītakaṃ dantī drā kṣājājiniśādbayam
dhātrījantughnaśikhari śṛṅgīdārupunarnavāḥ 61
dhānyākaṃ devakusumaṃ rājavṛkṣastrikaṇṭakam
vṛddhadārukuberākṣyau mūlaṃ vīraṇikābhavam 62
eteṣāṃ palayugmantu bheṣajānāṃ pṛthakpṛthak
āḍhakañcāpi pathyāyāstoye pañcāḍhake pacet 63
svinnā pathyā bhavedyāvatpaścānmadhu vinikṣipet
gurūpadeśādvidhivattridinañca tataḥ param 64
punaḥ kṣipetpañcadinaṃ tathā ca daśavāsaram
saṃsiddhā cābhayā paścād ghṛtabhāṇḍe nidhāpayet 65
vimale sudṛḍhe kṣaudra paripūrṇeprayatnataḥ
paścātpūrvoktakāṇḍe tu kṣiped buddhiparāyaṇaḥ 66
eṣā harītakī caiva dhanvantari kṛtā śubhā
bhakṣayedyo naro nityaṃ rogā naśyanti sarvaśaḥ 67
śvāsaṃ kāsaṃ kṣayaṃ pāṇḍu hikkā chardimadabhramān
mukharogaṃ tathā tṛṣṇāmaruciṃ vahnimandatām 68
yakṛtplīhodarañcaiva vātaraktaṃ sudāruṇam
śiro'kṣikarṇajāṃ pīḍāṃ tathā baddhagudodaram 69
grahaṇīṃ durvikārāñca śeṣaṃ doṣatrayodbhavam
madhupakveti vikhyātā hanti rogānanekaśaḥ 70
bījāni tu kapikacchvāḥ kuḍavamitāni ca svedayecchanakaiḥ
prasthe gobhavadugdhe tāvad yāvad bhaved gāḍham 71
tvagrahitāni ca kṛtvā sūkṣmaṃ sampeṣayettāni
piṣṭikayā laghuvaṭikāḥ kṛtvā gavye pacedājye 62
dviguṇitaśarkarayā tā vaṭikāḥ sampakvayā lepyāḥ
vaṭikā mākṣikamadhye majjanayogye'khilāḥ sthāpyāḥ 73
pañcaṭaṅkamitāstāstu prātaḥ sāyañca bhakṣayet
anena śīghradrā vī yo yaśca syātpatitadhvajaḥ 74
so'pi prātnoti surate sāmarthyamati vājivat
nānena sadṛśaṃ kiñcid dra vyaṃ vājīkaraṃ param 75
ākārakarabhaḥ śuṇṭhī lavaṅgaṃ kuṅkumaṃ kaṇā
jātīphalaṃ jātipuṣpaṃ candanaṃ kārṣikaṃ pṛthak 76
cūrṇayedahiphenantu tatra dadyātpalonmitam
sarvamekīkṛtaṃ māṣamātraṃ kṣaudre ṇa bhakṣayet 77
śukrastambhakaraṃ puṃsāmidamānandakārakam
nārīṇāṃ prītijananaṃ seveta niśi kāmukaḥ 78
iti dvisaptatitamo vājīkaraṇādhikāraḥ samāptaḥ 72
<3.2>
atha trisaptatitamo rasāyanādhikāraḥ 73
yajjarāvyādhividhvaṃsi vayaḥ stambhakaraṃ tathā
cakṣuṣyaṃ bṛṃhaṇaṃ vṛṣyaṃ bheṣajaṃ tadra sāyanam 1
dīrghamāyuḥ smṛtiṃ medhāmārogyaṃ taruṇaṃ vayaḥ
dehendri yabalaṃ kāntiṃ naro vindedrrasāyanāt 2
nāviśuddhaśarīrasya yukto rāsāyano vidhiḥ
na bhāti vāsasi mliṣṭe raṅgayoga ivārpitaḥ 3
śītodakaṃ payaḥ kṣaudraṃ ghṛtamekaikaśo hitam
triśaḥ samastamathavā prākpītaṃ sthāpayedvayaḥ 4
maṇḍūkaparṇyā svarasaḥ prayojyaḥ kṣīreṇa yaṣṭīmadhukasya cūrṇam
raso guḍūcyāstu samūlapuṣpaḥ kalkaḥ prayojyaḥ khalu śaṅkhapuṣpyāḥ 5
āyuḥ pradānyāmayanāśanāni balāgnivarṇasvaravarddhanāni
medhyāni caitāni rasāyanāni medhyā viśeṣeṇa ca śaṅkhapuṣpī 6
mākṣikeṇa tugākṣīrī pippalyā lavaṇena ca
triphalā sitayā vā'pi yuktā siddhaṃ rasāyanam 7
sindhūtthaśarkarāśuṇṭhīkaṇāmadhuguḍaiḥ kramāt
varṣā'diṣvabhayā prāśyā rasāyanaguṇaiṣiṇā 8
punarnavasyārddhapalaṃ navasya piṣṭaṃ pibedyaḥ payasā'ddhamāsam
māsatrayaṃ tattriguṇaṃ samaṃ vā jīrṇo'pi bhūyaḥ sa punarnavaḥ syāt
ye māsamekaṃ svarasaṃ pibanti dine dine bhṛṅgarajaḥsamuttham
kṣīrāśinaste balavīryayuktāḥ samāḥ śataṃ jīvanamāpnuvanti 10
śatāvarī muṇḍitikā guḍūcī sahastikarṇā sahatālamūlī
eṃtāni kṛtvā samabhāgayuktānyājyena kiṃ vā madhunā'valihyāt 11
jarārujāmṛtyuviyuktadeho bhavennaro vīryabalādiyuktaḥ
vibhāti devapratimaḥ sa nityaṃ prabhāmayo bhūrivivṛddhiyuktaḥ 12
pītvā'śvagandhāṃ payasā'ddhamāsaṃ ghṛtena tailena sukhāmbunā vā
vīryasya puṣṭiṃ vapuṣo vidhatte bālasya vṛkṣasya yathā'mbuvṛṣṭiḥ 13
ayaḥ palaṃ guggulumatra yojyaṃ palatrayaṃ vyoṣapalāni pañca
palāni cāṣṭau triphalārajaśca karṣaṃ lihanyātyamaratvameva 14
na kevalaṃ dīrghamihāyuraśnute rasāyanaṃ yo vidhivanniṣevate
gatiṃ sa devarṣiniṣevitāṃ śubhāṃ prapadyate brahma tathaiva cākṣaram 15
iti trisaptatitamo rasāyanādhikāraḥ samāptaḥ 73
granthakarttṛśubhāśīḥ śaṃsanam
yāvad vyomani bimbamambaramaṇerindośca vidyotate
yāvat sapta payodhayaḥsagirayastiṣṭhanti pṛṣṭhe bhuvaḥ
yāvaccāvanimaṇḍalaṃ phaṇipaterāste phaṇāmaṇḍale
tāvatsadbhiṣajaḥ paṭhantu parito bhāvaprakāśaṃ śubham 1
granthasyāsyādhyāpakanāñjanānāṃ madhye nṝṇāmādaraṃ kurvatāṃ ca
śrīsomeśādityavipraprasādādāyurdīrghaṃ saukhyamāstāṃ sadaiva 2
samāptamidamuttarakhaṇḍam
samāptaścāyaṃ granthaḥ
